end)

	_G._ViolenceIntroMusicOptions = INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS

	task.spawn(function()
		local preferred = tonumber(_G._ViolenceIntroSongIndex) or 1
		if preferred < 1 or preferred > #INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS then preferred = 1 end
		local option = INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[preferred]
		if not option or option.silent then return end
		local soundId = nil
		pcall(function()
			if option.file and isfile and isfile(option.file) and getcustomasset then
				soundId = getcustomasset(option.file)
			end
		end)
		if not soundId and option.url then
			local data = nil
			pcall(function() if game.HttpGet then data = game:HttpGet(option.url) end end)
			if not data then
				pcall(function()
					local req = (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request
						or (getgenv and (getgenv().request or getgenv().http_request))
					if req then
						local res = req({Url = option.url, Method = "GET"})
						if res then data = res.Body or res.body end
					end
				end)
			end
			if data and type(data) == "string" and #data > 0 and writefile and option.file then
				pcall(function()
					writefile(option.file, data)
					if getcustomasset then soundId = getcustomasset(option.file) end
				end)
			end
			if not soundId then soundId = option.url end
		end
		if not soundId then return end
		pcall(function() introMusic.SoundId = tostring(soundId) end)
		local t0 = tick()
		while introMusic.Parent and not introMusic.IsLoaded and tick() - t0 < 4 do
			task.wait(0.05)
		end
		pcall(function() introMusic:Play() end)
	end)

	local splashDone = false
	local animConn = nil

	local function finishSplash()
		if splashDone then return end
		splashDone = true
		_G._ViolenceIntroDone = true
		if animConn then animConn:Disconnect(); animConn = nil end
		pcall(function()
			if introMusic then
				TweenService:Create(introMusic, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Volume = 0}):Play()
				task.delay(0.35, function()
					pcall(function() introMusic:Stop(); introMusic:Destroy() end)
				end)
			end
			-- fade out suave (estilo ADAPT)
			if titleShadow then
				TweenService:Create(titleShadow, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {TextTransparency = 1}):Play()
			end
			if title then
				TweenService:Create(title, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {TextTransparency = 1}):Play()
			end
			if skipHint then
				TweenService:Create(skipHint, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {TextTransparency = 1}):Play()
			end
			if gojo then
				TweenService:Create(gojo, TweenInfo.new(0.35), {ImageTransparency = 1}):Play()
			end
			if shadow then
				TweenService:Create(shadow, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
			end
			if fxLayer then
				for _, child in ipairs(fxLayer:GetDescendants()) do
					if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
						pcall(function()
							TweenService:Create(child, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
						end)
					end
					if child:IsA("UIStroke") then
						pcall(function()
							TweenService:Create(child, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
						end)
					end
				end
			end
			TweenService:Create(bg, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
		end)
		task.delay(0.45, function()
			pcall(function() if splashGui then splashGui:Destroy() end end)
		end)
	end

	-- Exige 2 toques na tela (dentro de 0.9s) para pular a intro
	local skipTapCount = 0
	local lastSkipTap = 0
	local SKIP_DOUBLE_WINDOW = 0.9

	local function trySkipSplash()
		if splashDone then return end
		local now = tick()
		-- evita contar o mesmo toque 2x (MouseButton1Click + InputBegan)
		if now - lastSkipTap < 0.05 then return end
		if now - lastSkipTap > SKIP_DOUBLE_WINDOW then
			skipTapCount = 0
		end
		lastSkipTap = now
		skipTapCount = skipTapCount + 1
		if skipTapCount >= 2 then
			finishSplash()
			if skipConn then skipConn:Disconnect(); skipConn = nil end
		else
			if skipHint then
				skipHint.Text = "TAP AGAIN TO SKIP"
				task.delay(SKIP_DOUBLE_WINDOW, function()
					if not splashDone and skipHint and skipTapCount < 2 then
						skipHint.Text = "TAP TWICE TO SKIP"
						skipTapCount = 0
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	tapBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(trySkipSplash)
	local skipConn
	skipConn = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if splashDone then return end
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			trySkipSplash()
		end
	end)

	-- Animação sutil e realista (igual estilo ADAPT: flutuação leve + orbes)
	local t0 = tick()
	-- fade-in: VIOLENCE + dica double-tap
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(0.25)
		if titleShadow then
			TweenService:Create(titleShadow, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextTransparency = 0.45}):Play()
		end
		if title then
			TweenService:Create(title, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextTransparency = 0}):Play()
		end
		task.wait(0.12)
		if skipHint then
			TweenService:Create(skipHint, TweenInfo.new(0.45), {TextTransparency = 0}):Play()
		end
	end)

	animConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if splashDone or not gojo.Parent then return end
		local t = tick() - t0

		-- flutuação bem suave (realista / cinematográfica)
		local breath = math.sin(t * 0.9) * 5.5
		local sway = math.sin(t * 0.55) * 2.8
		gojo.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, sway, 0.42, breath)

		-- sombra acompanha
		if shadow and shadow.Parent then
			shadow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, sway * 0.4, 0.72, breath * 0.15)
			shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 + math.sin(t * 0.9) * 0.08
		end

		-- anéis girando bem devagar
		for _, rd in ipairs(ringData) do
			if rd.obj and rd.obj.Parent then
				rd.obj.Rotation = (t * rd.speed) % 360
			end
		end

		-- orbes orbitando suavemente
		for _, o in ipairs(orbs) do
			if o.obj and o.obj.Parent then
				local ang = math.rad(o.angle + t * o.speed)
				local rx = math.cos(ang) * o.radius
				local ry = math.sin(ang) * o.radius * 0.68
				o.obj.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, rx, 0.42, ry + breath * 0.25)
				local s = 20 + math.sin(t * 1.8 + o.phase) * 3.5
				o.obj.Size = UDim2.new(0, s, 0, s)
				o.obj.BackgroundTransparency = 0.22 + math.sin(t * 1.8 + o.phase) * 0.1
			end
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(6.2)
		if not splashDone then finishSplash() end
	end)
end
end -- if not _skipIntro




local Players, RunService, UIS, TS, Lighting, HS = game:GetService("Players"), game:GetService("RunService"), game:GetService("UserInputService"), game:GetService("TweenService"), game:GetService("Lighting"), game:GetService("HttpService")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
-- Speeds em _G (sem locals extras — evita "Out of local registers")
_G.ViolenceSpeeds = _G.ViolenceSpeeds or { NS = 45, CS = 25, L1 = 20, L2 = 15 }
do
    local S = _G.ViolenceSpeeds
    if S.NS == nil then S.NS = 45 end
    if S.CS == nil then S.CS = 25 end
    if S.L1 == nil then S.L1 = 20 end
    if S.L2 == nil then S.L2 = 15 end
    function S.setNS(v)
        v = tonumber(v)
        if not v or v <= 0 or v > 500 then return false end
        S.NS = v
        return true
    end
    function S.setCS(v)
        v = tonumber(v)
        if not v or v <= 0 or v > 500 then return false end
        S.CS = v
        return true
    end
    function S.setL1(v)
        v = tonumber(v)
        if not v or v <= 0 or v > 500 then return false end
        S.L1 = v
        return true
    end
    function S.setL2(v)
        v = tonumber(v)
        if not v or v <= 0 or v > 500 then return false end
        S.L2 = v
        return true
    end
    function S.get(mode)
        if mode == "lagger2" or mode == 2 then return tonumber(S.L2) or 15 end
        if mode == "lagger1" or mode == 1 then return tonumber(S.L1) or 20 end
        if mode == "carry" then return tonumber(S.CS) or 25 end
        return tonumber(S.NS) or 45
    end
end

function formatSpeedModeText(speedVal)
    local n = string.format("%.0f", speedVal or 0)
    if laggerToggled then
        if laggerLevel == 2 then
            return "LAGGER CARRY " .. n
        end
        return "LAGGER NORMAL " .. n
    elseif speedMode then
        return "CARRY " .. n
    end
    return n
end

-- Fotos de botões flutuantes (do Adapt) — tudo em tabela pra não estourar limite de 200 locals
_G.ViolenceButtonImageIDs = _G.ViolenceButtonImageIDs or {

_G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage = _G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage or "rbxassetid://90631990302263"
_G.ViolenceMobileImg = _G.ViolenceMobileImg or {}
do
    local M = _G.ViolenceMobileImg
    function M.normalize(value)
        value = tostring(value or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if value == "" then return "" end
        local id = value:match("^(%d+)$")
        if id then return "rbxassetid://" .. id end
        local embedded = value:match("[?&]id=(%d+)") or value:match("/(%d+)[/%?]?")
        if embedded then return "rbxassetid://" .. embedded end
        if value:find("rbxassetid://") then return value end
        return value
    end
    -- branco puro: tint do tema deixava a foto preta/invisível
    function M.tint()
        return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    end
    function M.attach(btn, cornerRadius)
        if not btn then return nil end
        local radius = cornerRadius or UDim.new(0, 18)
        -- clipa a foto pra não sobrar ponta fora do canto arredondado
        if btn:IsA("GuiObject") then
            btn.ClipsDescendants = true
        end
        local existing = btn:FindFirstChild("MobileBackgroundImage")
        if existing then
            existing.ZIndex = math.max((btn.ZIndex or 1) + 1, 2)
            local ec = existing:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
            if ec then ec.CornerRadius = radius end
            return existing
        end
        local bgImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        bgImage.Name = "MobileBackgroundImage"
        bgImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bgImage.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bgImage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        bgImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        bgImage.Image = M.normalize(_G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage)
        bgImage.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        bgImage.ImageTransparency = 0 -- sem overlay escuro
        bgImage.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        bgImage.Visible = (bgImage.Image ~= "")
        bgImage.ZIndex = math.max((btn.ZIndex or 1) + 1, 2)
        bgImage.Parent = btn
        Instance.new("UICorner", bgImage).CornerRadius = radius
        -- botão transparente quando tem foto (evita fundo preto/escuro por cima)
        if btn:IsA("GuiObject") and bgImage.Image ~= "" then
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        end
        -- garante texto acima da foto
        local baseZ = math.max(btn.ZIndex or 1, 1)
        bgImage.ZIndex = baseZ + 1
        for _, ch in ipairs(btn:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then
                ch.ZIndex = baseZ + 10
                ch.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                ch.TextTransparency = 0
                ch.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                ch.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
            end
        end
        return bgImage
    end
    function M.applyAll()
        local image = M.normalize(_G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage)
        local function applyOne(btn)
            if not btn then return end
            local img = btn:FindFirstChild("MobileBackgroundImage")
            if not img then img = M.attach(btn) end
            if img then
                -- limpa e reaplica pra forçar reload do asset
                img.Image = ""
                img.Image = image
                img.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                img.Visible = image ~= ""
                img.ImageTransparency = 0 -- sem overlay escuro
                img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
                img.ZIndex = math.max((btn.ZIndex or 1) + 1, 2)
                local ic = img:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
                if not ic then
                    ic = Instance.new("UICorner", img)
                end
                ic.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 18)
            end
            if btn:IsA("GuiObject") then
                btn.ClipsDescendants = true
            end
            -- texto SEMPRE por cima da foto + contorno preto (legível em qualquer foto)
            local baseZ = math.max(btn.ZIndex or 1, 1)
            if img then img.ZIndex = baseZ + 1 end
            for _, ch in ipairs(btn:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    ch.ZIndex = baseZ + 10
                    ch.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    ch.TextTransparency = 0
                    ch.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    ch.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
                    ch.Visible = true
                elseif ch:IsA("TextButton") and ch.Name == "Click" then
                    ch.ZIndex = baseZ + 11
                end
            end
            if btn:IsA("GuiObject") then
                if image ~= "" then
                    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1 -- sem fundo escuro por cima da foto
                else
                    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                end
            end
        end
        if type(_G.ViolenceMobileButtons) == "table" then
            for _, data in pairs(_G.ViolenceMobileButtons) do
                applyOne(data and data.btn)
            end
        end
        local function findGui(name)
            local ok, parent = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
            if ok and parent then
                local g = parent:FindFirstChild(name)
                if g then return g end
            end
            local pg = LP and LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            return pg and pg:FindFirstChild(name) or nil
        end
        for _, guiName in ipairs({"BypassButton", "TPBatButton", "InstantResetButton"}) do
            local gui = findGui(guiName)
            if gui then applyOne(gui:FindFirstChild("Frame")) end
        end
        if _G.ViolenceTPBat and _G.ViolenceTPBat.btn then
            applyOne(_G.ViolenceTPBat.btn:FindFirstChild("Frame"))
        end
    end
end
_G.ViolenceApplyMobileButtonImage = function()
    return _G.ViolenceMobileImg.applyAll()
end

-- Feedback de toque nos flutuantes: anel branco ao apertar
-- e permanece enquanto o botão estiver ATIVO (toggle on)
_G.ViolenceShowFloatRing = function(btn, show)
    if not btn or not btn.Parent then return end
    local parent = btn.Parent
    local ringName = btn.Name .. "_PressRing"
    local ring = parent:FindFirstChild(ringName)
    if show then
        if not ring then
            ring = Instance.new("Frame")
            ring.Name = ringName
            ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ring.BorderSizePixel = 0
            ring.ZIndex = math.max((btn.ZIndex or 20) + 5, 25)
            ring.Active = false
            ring.Parent = parent
            local rc = Instance.new("UICorner", ring)
            rc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 20)
            local rs = Instance.new("UIStroke", ring)
            rs.Name = "WhitePressStroke"
            rs.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            rs.Thickness = 3
            rs.Transparency = 0
            rs.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        end
        local pad = 3
        local bp, bs = btn.Position, btn.Size
        ring.Size = UDim2.new(bs.X.Scale, bs.X.Offset + pad * 2, bs.Y.Scale, bs.Y.Offset + pad * 2)
        ring.Position = UDim2.new(bp.X.Scale, bp.X.Offset - pad, bp.Y.Scale, bp.Y.Offset - pad)
        ring.AnchorPoint = btn.AnchorPoint
        local corner = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
        local rc = ring:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
        if corner and rc then
            local off = corner.CornerRadius.Offset
            if off and off > 0 then
                rc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, off + 2)
            else
                rc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            end
        end
        local rs = ring:FindFirstChild("WhitePressStroke")
        if rs then
            rs.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            rs.Thickness = 3
            rs.Transparency = 0
        end
        ring.Visible = true
    else
        if ring then ring.Visible = false end
    end
end

_G.ViolenceBindFloatPressStroke = function(btn, stroke)
    if not btn then return end
    if btn:GetAttribute("ViolencePressStrokeBound") then return end
    btn:SetAttribute("ViolencePressStrokeBound", true)

    local pressed = false

    local function applyStrokeActive(active)
        if not stroke then return end
        if active then
            stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            stroke.Thickness = 2.2
            stroke.Transparency = 0
        else
            local C = _G.ViolenceBtnColor
            stroke.Color = (C and C.stroke) or Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 90)
            stroke.Thickness = 1.5
            stroke.Transparency = 0.35
        end
    end

    local function refreshRing()
        local active = btn:GetAttribute("ViolenceFloatActive") == true
        -- anel fica se estiver pressionando OU se o toggle estiver ligado
        _G.ViolenceShowFloatRing(btn, pressed or active)
        applyStrokeActive(pressed or active)
    end

    -- setActive externo chama isso pra manter/sumir o anel
    btn:SetAttribute("ViolenceFloatActive", btn:GetAttribute("ViolenceFloatActive") == true)

    local function isPressInput(input)
        return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
    end

    local function pressOn()
        pressed = true
        refreshRing()
    end

    local function pressOff()
        pressed = false
        -- NÃO some se o botão estiver ativo (toggle on)
        refreshRing()
    end

    btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if isPressInput(input) then pressOn() end
    end)
    btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if isPressInput(input) then pressOff() end
    end)

    local function bindClick(click)
        if not click or not click:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
        if click:GetAttribute("ViolencePressStrokeClickBound") then return end
        click:SetAttribute("ViolencePressStrokeClickBound", true)
        click.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if isPressInput(input) then pressOn() end
        end)
        click.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if isPressInput(input) then pressOff() end
        end)
    end
    bindClick(btn:FindFirstChild("Click"))
    btn.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
        if ch.Name == "Click" then bindClick(ch) end
    end)

    -- soltar fora do botão
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if pressed and isPressInput(input) then
            pressOff()
        end
    end)

    -- quando arrastar o botão, o anel acompanha
    btn:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function()
        if btn:GetAttribute("ViolenceFloatActive") or pressed then
            _G.ViolenceShowFloatRing(btn, true)
        end
    end)
end

local origFOV = 70

_G.applyCustomSky = function(mode)
	local S = _G.ViolenceSky
	mode = mode or S.theme or "Night"
	S.theme = mode
	for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
		if child:GetAttribute(S.tag) then pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end
	end
	local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
	if terrain then
		for _, child in ipairs(terrain:GetChildren()) do
			if child:GetAttribute(S.tag) then pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end
		end
	end
	local preset = S.presets[mode]
	local function cc(rgb) return Color3.fromRGB(rgb[1], rgb[2], rgb[3]) end
	if not preset or preset.kind == "off" then
		Lighting.ClockTime = 14; Lighting.Brightness = 2
		Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(127, 127, 127)
		Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(127, 127, 127)
		Lighting.FogEnd = 100000; Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
		return
	end
	Lighting.FogStart = 0; Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
	Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
	Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
	Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
	Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
	Lighting.ClockTime = preset.clock or 14
	Lighting.Brightness = preset.brightness or 2
	if preset.outAmb then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = cc(preset.outAmb) end
	if preset.ambient then Lighting.Ambient = cc(preset.ambient) end
	if preset.sky then
		local skyInst = Instance.new("Sky")
		skyInst:SetAttribute(S.tag, true)
		if preset.sky.stars then skyInst.StarCount = preset.sky.stars end
		if preset.sky.moon then skyInst.MoonAngularSize = preset.sky.moon end
		if preset.sky.sun then skyInst.SunAngularSize = preset.sky.sun end
		if preset.sky.moonTex then skyInst.MoonTextureId = "rbxasset://sky/moon.jpg" end
		skyInst.Parent = Lighting
	end
	if preset.atm then
		local atm = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
		atm:SetAttribute(S.tag, true)
		atm.Density = preset.atm.dens or 0.3
		atm.Color = cc(preset.atm.color)
		atm.Decay = cc(preset.atm.decay)
		atm.Glare = preset.atm.glare or 1
		atm.Haze = preset.atm.haze or 1
		atm.Parent = Lighting
	end
	if preset.clouds and terrain then
		local clouds = Instance.new("Clouds")
		clouds:SetAttribute(S.tag, true)
		clouds.Cover = preset.clouds.cover or 0.5
		clouds.Density = preset.clouds.dens or 0.5
		clouds.Color = cc(preset.clouds.color)
		clouds.Parent = terrain
	end
end

function stopAllBackgroundTasks()
    if movementLoop then movementLoop:Disconnect(); movementLoop = nil end
    if steppedConn then steppedConn:Disconnect(); steppedConn = nil end
    if enemySpeedConn then enemySpeedConn:Disconnect(); enemySpeedConn = nil end
    if stretchEnabled then disableStretch() end
    if stretchConn then stretchConn:Disconnect(); stretchConn = nil end
    if stretchFovConn then stretchFovConn:Disconnect(); stretchFovConn = nil end
    stopAntiRagdoll()
    stopJumpMode()
    stopBatCounter()
    stopMedusaCounter()
    stopAutoSteal()
    stopAutoTPDown()
    disableAutoBat()
    stopBypassAimbot()
    _G.ViolenceTPBat.stop()
    stopAutoLeft()
    stopAutoRight()
    if unwalkEnabled then stopUnwalk() end
    if antiLagEnabled then disableAntiLag() end
    if dropActive then stopDropBrainrot() end
    if _G.ViolenceAntiDrop.enabled then _G.ViolenceAntiDrop.stopAntiDrop() end
    for _, t in ipairs(dropConnections) do
        if type(t) == "thread" then pcall(task.cancel, t)
        elseif type(t) == "RBXScriptConnection" then pcall(t.Disconnect, t) end
    end
    dropConnections = {}
    dropActive = false
    isStealing = false
    Steal.cachedPrompts = {}
    Steal.promptCacheTime = 0
    _hittingCooldown = false
    bypassHittingCooldown = false
    alPhase = 1
    arPhase = 1
    lastDropTime = 0
    medusaDebounce = false
    medusaLastUsed = 0
    if _G.ViolenceESP and _G.ViolenceESP.stop then _G.ViolenceESP.stop() end
end

function setMedusaCounterState(state)
    medusaCounterEnabled = state
    if state then
        if LP.Character then setupMedusa(LP.Character) else stopMedusaCounter() end
    else
        stopMedusaCounter()
    end
    if setMedusaVisual then setMedusaVisual(state) end
end

local cursedResetRemote = nil

-- Instant Reset (Adapt HipHeight) — estado em 1 tabela (economiza locals)
_G.ViolenceReset = _G.ViolenceReset or {
    cooldown = false,
    thread = nil,
    char = nil,
    ok = false,
    stop = false,
    camLock = false,
    camCF = nil,
    maxDur = 0.05,
    floatPos = nil,
    floatBtn = nil,
    killing = false,
    _lastFire = 0,
}

-- Instant Reset: lógica IDÊNTICA ao Adapt (loop HipHeight 1e30 → fallback Health=0)
_G.stopViolenceInstantReset = function()
    _G.ViolenceReset.stop = true
    if _G.ViolenceReset.thread then
        pcall(task.cancel, _G.ViolenceReset.thread)
        _G.ViolenceReset.thread = nil
    end
    _G.ViolenceReset.cooldown = false
    _G.ViolenceReset.char = nil
    _G.ViolenceReset.camLock = false
    _G.ViolenceReset.ok = false
    _G.ViolenceReset.killing = false
end

_G.violenceInstantReset = function()
    if _G.ViolenceReset.cooldown then return end
    _G.ViolenceReset.cooldown = true
    _G.ViolenceReset.ok = false
    _G.ViolenceReset.stop = false
    _G.ViolenceReset.camLock = false

    local character = LP.Character
    if not character then
        _G.ViolenceReset.cooldown = false
        return
    end

    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then
        _G.ViolenceReset.cooldown = false
        return
    end
    if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        _G.ViolenceReset.cooldown = false
        return
    end

    -- trava câmera no frame atual (igual Adapt)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        _G.ViolenceReset.camCF = camera.CFrame
        _G.ViolenceReset.camLock = true
        camera.CFrame = _G.ViolenceReset.camCF
    end
    _G.ViolenceReset.char = character

    -- trava AntiDie enquanto o sequence roda
    _G.ViolenceReset.killing = true

    local isRespawning = false
    local originalHipHeight = humanoid.HipHeight
    local maxAttempts = 40
    local maxDur = _G.ViolenceReset.maxDur or 0.05

    _G.ViolenceReset.thread = task.spawn(function()
        local attempts = 0
        while character and character.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and not isRespawning and not _G.ViolenceReset.stop do
            if LP.Character ~= character then
                isRespawning = true
                break
            end

            pcall(function()
                humanoid.HipHeight = 1e30
                humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if rootPart then
                    rootPart.CanCollide = false
                end
                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end)

            if not character or not character.Parent or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or LP.Character ~= character then
                _G.ViolenceReset.ok = true
                break
            end

            attempts = attempts + 1
            if attempts >= maxAttempts then break end
            task.wait(maxDur)
        end

        -- fallback Health=0 (igual Adapt)
        if not _G.ViolenceReset.ok and character and character.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and not isRespawning then
            pcall(function()
                humanoid.Health = 0
            end)
            task.wait(0.1)
            if not character.Parent or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                _G.ViolenceReset.ok = true
            end
        end

        -- restaura se falhou (igual Adapt)
        if not _G.ViolenceReset.ok and character and character.Parent and humanoid then
            pcall(function()
                humanoid.HipHeight = originalHipHeight
                local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if rootPart then
                    rootPart.CanCollide = true
                end
                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        part.CanCollide = true
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        _G.ViolenceReset.camLock = false
        _G.ViolenceReset.cooldown = false
        _G.ViolenceReset.thread = nil
        _G.ViolenceReset.char = nil
        _G.ViolenceReset.stop = false
        task.delay(0.25, function()
            _G.ViolenceReset.killing = false
        end)
    end)
end

_G.insta_reset = function()
    _G.violenceInstantReset()
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    _G.stopViolenceInstantReset()
    _G.ViolenceReset.ok = false
    _G.ViolenceReset.stop = false
    _G.ViolenceReset.camLock = false
    _G.ViolenceReset.killing = false
end)

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if _G.ViolenceReset.camLock and _G.ViolenceReset.camCF and workspace.CurrentCamera then
        workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = _G.ViolenceReset.camCF
    end
end)

pcall(function()
    if hookfunction and newcclosure then
        local oldFire
        oldFire = hookfunction(Instance.new("RemoteEvent").FireServer, newcclosure(function(self, ...)
            if not cursedResetRemote and typeof(self) == "Instance" and self:IsA("RemoteEvent") and self.Name:sub(1,3) == "RE/" then
                cursedResetRemote = self
            end
            return oldFire(self, ...)
        end))
    end
end)

function findCursedResetRemote()
    if cursedResetRemote then return end

    -- Primeiro tenta os locais mais prováveis; evita varrer toda a árvore do jogo.
    local candidates = {
        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"),
        workspace
    }

    for _, root in ipairs(candidates) do
        if root then
            for _, desc in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if desc:IsA("RemoteEvent") and desc.Name:sub(1,3) == "RE/" then
                    cursedResetRemote = desc
                    return
                end
            end
        end
        if cursedResetRemote then return end
        task.wait()
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(2)
    pcall(findCursedResetRemote)
end)

local BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED = 58
local BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED = 60

-- ===================== MELEE AIMBOT (Body Lock - from Bless) =====================
-- Packed into 1 table to save local registers (Luau 200 limit)
_G.ViolenceMelee = _G.ViolenceMelee or {
    enabled = false,
    conn = nil,
    setVisual = nil,
    range = 150,
}
_G.ViolenceMelee.getClosest = function()
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil end
    local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
            local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < minDist then minDist = dist; closest = tRoot end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end
_G.ViolenceMelee.tick = function()
    local M = _G.ViolenceMelee
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not hum then return end
    local target = _G.ViolenceMelee.getClosest()
    if not target then
        if not hum.AutoRotate then hum.AutoRotate = true end
        return
    end
    local dist = (target.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
    if dist > (M.range or 150) then
        if not hum.AutoRotate then hum.AutoRotate = true end
        return
    end
    if hum.AutoRotate then hum.AutoRotate = false end
    local targetVel = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local speed = targetVel.Magnitude
    local predictTime = math.clamp(speed / 150, 0.05, 0.2)
    local predictedPos = target.Position + targetVel * predictTime
    local flatTarget = Vector3.new(predictedPos.X, root.Position.Y, predictedPos.Z)
    if (flatTarget - root.Position).Magnitude > 0.1 then
        local goalCF = CFrame.lookAt(root.Position, flatTarget)
        local diffCF = root.CFrame:Inverse() * goalCF
        local _, ry, _ = diffCF:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
        ry = math.clamp(ry, -2.5, 2.5)
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, ry * 42, 0))
    end
end
_G.ViolenceMelee.start = function()
    local M = _G.ViolenceMelee
    if M.conn then return end
    M.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if M.enabled then _G.ViolenceMelee.tick() end
    end)
end
_G.ViolenceMelee.stop = function()
    local M = _G.ViolenceMelee
    if M.conn then M.conn:Disconnect(); M.conn = nil end
    local c = LP.Character
    local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if root then root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
    local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
end

local selectedAnimPack = nil
local animMenuFrame = nil

local function applyAnimationPack(packName)
    local pack = AnimationPacks[packName]
    if not pack then return false end
    selectedAnimPack = packName
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return false end
    local animate = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if not animate then return false end

    local function setAnim(containerName, id)
        if not id then return end
        local container = animate:FindFirstChild(containerName)
        if not container then return end
        local anim = container:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animation")
        if anim then
            anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
        else
            local sv = container:FindFirstChild("AnimationId")
            if sv and sv:IsA("StringValue") then
                sv.Value = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
            end
        end
    end

    setAnim("walk", pack.WalkAnim)
    setAnim("run", pack.RunAnim)
    setAnim("jump", pack.JumpAnim)
    setAnim("fall", pack.FallAnim)
    setAnim("climb", pack.ClimbAnim)
    setAnim("swim", pack.Swim)
    setAnim("swimidle", pack.SwimIdle)

    if pack.Idle and type(pack.Idle) == "table" then
        setAnim("idle", pack.Idle[1])
        local idle1 = animate:FindFirstChild("idle")
        if idle1 then
            local a1 = idle1:FindFirstChild("Animation1")
            local a2 = idle1:FindFirstChild("Animation2")
            if a1 and a1:IsA("Animation") and pack.Idle[1] then a1.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(pack.Idle[1]) end
            if a2 and a2:IsA("Animation") and pack.Idle[2] then a2.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(pack.Idle[2]) end
        end
    else
        setAnim("idle", pack.Animation1)
        local idle1 = animate:FindFirstChild("idle")
        if idle1 then
            local a1 = idle1:FindFirstChild("Animation1")
            local a2 = idle1:FindFirstChild("Animation2")
            if a1 and a1:IsA("Animation") and pack.Animation1 then a1.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(pack.Animation1) end
            if a2 and a2:IsA("Animation") and pack.Animation2 then a2.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(pack.Animation2) end
        end
    end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
        end
    end

    selectedAnimPack = packName
    return true
end


local Steal = {
    AutoStealEnabled = false,
    StealMode = "Normal", -- "Normal" ou "Semi"
    StealRadius = 44,
    StealDuration = 1.3,
    Data = {},
    cachedPrompts = {},
    promptCacheTime = 0,
}
local isStealing = false
local stealStartTime = nil
local lastStealTick = 0
local STEAL_COOLDOWN = 0.1
local PROMPT_CACHE_REFRESH = 0.20
local FAST_CHARGE_TIME = 0.12
local stealModeBtnRef = nil

local function getEffectiveStealRadius()
    if Steal.StealMode == "Semi" then
        -- Prime range amplo pra iniciar hold; complete so dentro de _G.ViolenceSemiState.RADIUS
        return 80
    end
    return Steal.StealRadius
end

local Conns = {autoSteal = nil, batCounter = nil, anchor = {}, progress = nil,
    autoLeft = nil, autoRight = nil}
local progressFill = nil
local progressPct = nil
local progressRadLbl = nil
local pbFrame = nil

local function resetProgressBar()
    if progressPct then progressPct.Text = "0%" end
    if progressFill then progressFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0) end
end

local function isMyPlotByName(plotName)
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return false end
    local plot = plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
    if not plot then return false end
    local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
    if sign then
        local yb = sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
        if yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            return yb.Enabled == true
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function findNearestPrompt()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil, math.huge end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil, math.huge end

    local ct = tick()
    if ct - Steal.promptCacheTime < PROMPT_CACHE_REFRESH and #Steal.cachedPrompts > 0 then
        local np, nd = nil, math.huge
        for _, data in ipairs(Steal.cachedPrompts) do
            if data.prompt and data.prompt.Parent and data.prompt.Enabled ~= false then
                local dist = (data.spawn.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                local maxR = getEffectiveStealRadius()
                if dist <= maxR and dist < nd then
                    np = data.prompt
                    nd = dist
                end
            end
        end
        if np then return np, nd end
    end

    Steal.cachedPrompts = {}
    Steal.promptCacheTime = ct
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return nil, math.huge end

    local maxR = getEffectiveStealRadius()
    local np, nd = nil, math.huge
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        if isMyPlotByName(plot.Name) then continue end
        local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if not pods then continue end
        for _, pod in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
            pcall(function()
                local base = pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
                local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                if spawn then
                    local att = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                    if att then
                        for _, child in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do
                            if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and child.ActionText and child.ActionText:find("Steal") then
                                local dist = (spawn.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                                table.insert(Steal.cachedPrompts, {prompt = child, spawn = spawn})
                                if dist <= maxR and dist < nd then
                                    np = child
                                    nd = dist
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    return np, nd
end

local function executeSteal(prompt)
    local ct = tick()
    if ct - lastStealTick < STEAL_COOLDOWN then return end
    if isStealing then return end
    if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or prompt.Enabled == false then return end

    if not Steal.Data[prompt] then
        Steal.Data[prompt] = {hold = {}, trigger = {}, ready = true, useFallback = false}
        pcall(function()
            if getconnections then
                for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
                    if c.Function then table.insert(Steal.Data[prompt].hold, c.Function) end
                end
                for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do
                    if c.Function then table.insert(Steal.Data[prompt].trigger, c.Function) end
                end
            else
                Steal.Data[prompt].useFallback = true
            end
        end)
    end
    local data = Steal.Data[prompt]
    if not data.ready then return end
    data.ready = false
    isStealing = true
    stealStartTime = ct
    lastStealTick = ct

    if Conns.progress then Conns.progress:Disconnect() end
    Conns.progress = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not isStealing then
            Conns.progress:Disconnect()
            Conns.progress = nil
            return
        end
        local prog = math.clamp((tick() - stealStartTime) / Steal.StealDuration, 0, 1)
        _G.setStealProgress(prog, "STEALING")
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        local ok = false
        pcall(function()
            if not data.useFallback and #data.hold > 0 then
                for _, fn in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end) end
                task.wait(Steal.StealDuration)
                for _, fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end) end
                ok = true
            end
        end)
        if not ok and type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
            pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(prompt) end)
            ok = true
            task.wait(Steal.StealDuration)
        end
        if not ok then
            pcall(function()
                prompt:InputHoldBegin()
                task.wait(Steal.StealDuration)
                prompt:InputHoldEnd()
            end)
            ok = true
        end

        task.wait(Steal.StealDuration * 0)
        if Conns.progress then
            Conns.progress:Disconnect()
            Conns.progress = nil
        end
        resetProgressBar()
        task.wait(0)
        data.ready = true
        isStealing = false
    end)
end

_G.setStealProgress = function(prog, state, extra)
    prog = math.clamp(prog or 0, 0, 1)
    if progressFill then
        progressFill.Size = UDim2.new(prog, 0, 1, 0)
        -- glow sutil conforme progresso (estilo Adapt)
        progressFill.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(0.18 - (prog * 0.12), 0, 0.5)
    end
    if progressPct then
        if state == "WAITING RANGE" and type(extra) == "number" then
            progressPct.Text = string.format("%d%% | %.0fm", math.floor(prog * 100 + 0.5), extra)
        elseif state == "SUCCESS" then
            progressPct.Text = "OK"
        elseif state == "FAILED" then
            progressPct.Text = "MISS"
        else
            progressPct.Text = math.floor(prog * 100 + 0.5) .. "%"
        end
        progressPct.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    end
    -- sincroniza estado visual da barra (setBarState é definido no buildGui)
    if _G.ViolenceStealBar and _G.ViolenceStealBar.SetState then
        pcall(_G.ViolenceStealBar.SetState, state or "STEALING", extra)
    end
end

-- Semi Grab (Adapt) — constantes + estado numa tabela só
_G.ViolenceSemiState = _G.ViolenceSemiState or {
    active = false, phase = "idle", startTime = 0, lockedSpawn = nil,
    HOLD_MIN = 1.3, HOLD_MAX = 2.6, ENTRY_DELAY = 0.3, PRIME = 80, COOLDOWN = 0.05, RADIUS = 9,
}

_G.resolvePromptSpawn = function(prompt)
    if not prompt then return nil end
    for _, data in ipairs(Steal.cachedPrompts) do
        if data.prompt == prompt and data.spawn and data.spawn.Parent then
            return data.spawn
        end
    end
    local node = prompt.Parent
    for _ = 1, 10 do
        if not node then break end
        if node.Name == "Spawn" and node:IsA("BasePart") then return node end
        node = node.Parent
    end
    return nil
end

_G.distToSpawn = function(spawn)
    if not spawn or not spawn.Parent then return math.huge end
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return math.huge end
    return (spawn.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
end

_G.buildSemiCallbacks = function(prompt)
    if Steal.Data[prompt] then return Steal.Data[prompt] end
    local data = {hold = {}, trigger = {}, ready = true, useFallback = false}
    pcall(function()
        if getconnections then
            for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
                if c.Function then table.insert(data.hold, c.Function) end
            end
            for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do
                if c.Function then table.insert(data.trigger, c.Function) end
            end
        else
            data.useFallback = true
        end
    end)
    if #data.hold == 0 and #data.trigger == 0 then
        data.useFallback = true
    end
    Steal.Data[prompt] = data
    return data
end

_G.executeSemiSteal = function(prompt, dist)
    if isStealing or _G.ViolenceSemiState.active then return end
    if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or prompt.Enabled == false then return end
    dist = dist or math.huge

    if dist > _G.ViolenceSemiState.PRIME then
        if not isStealing then _G.setStealProgress(0, Steal.AutoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE") end
        return
    end

    -- Trava o spawn do animal (distancia sempre no mesmo alvo, igual Adapt)
    local lockedSpawn = _G.resolvePromptSpawn(prompt)
    if lockedSpawn then
        dist = _G.distToSpawn(lockedSpawn)
        if dist > _G.ViolenceSemiState.PRIME then
            if not isStealing then _G.setStealProgress(0, Steal.AutoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE") end
            return
        end
    end

    local data = _G.buildSemiCallbacks(prompt)
    if not data or not data.ready then return end
    if tick() - (lastStealTick or 0) < _G.ViolenceSemiState.COOLDOWN then return end

    data.ready = false
    isStealing = true
    _G.ViolenceSemiState.active = true
    _G.ViolenceSemiState.phase = "holding"
    _G.ViolenceSemiState.startTime = tick()
    _G.ViolenceSemiState.lockedSpawn = lockedSpawn
    lastStealTick = tick()
    local t0 = _G.ViolenceSemiState.startTime
    local alreadyInRange = dist <= _G.ViolenceSemiState.RADIUS

    task.spawn(function()
        -- 1) Hold callbacks (começa a "segurar" o grab)
        if not data.useFallback and #data.hold > 0 then
            for _, fn in ipairs(data.hold) do
                task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end)
            end
        else
            pcall(function()
                if prompt.InputHoldBegin then prompt:InputHoldBegin() end
            end)
        end

        -- 2) Hold mínimo: barra sobe proporcional a holdMax (Adapt: elapsed/holdMax)
        while Steal.AutoStealEnabled and Steal.StealMode == "Semi" and tick() - t0 < _G.ViolenceSemiState.HOLD_MIN do
            local elapsed = tick() - t0
            _G.setStealProgress(math.clamp(elapsed / _G.ViolenceSemiState.HOLD_MAX, 0, 1), "STEALING")
            task.wait()
        end

        if not Steal.AutoStealEnabled or Steal.StealMode ~= "Semi" then
            data.ready = true
            isStealing = false
            _G.ViolenceSemiState.active = false
            _G.ViolenceSemiState.phase = "idle"
            resetProgressBar()
            return
        end

        -- 3) Waiting range: barra continua até holdMax; só trigger perto do spawn travado
        _G.ViolenceSemiState.phase = "waitingRange"
        local fired = false
        while Steal.AutoStealEnabled and Steal.StealMode == "Semi" and prompt.Parent do
            local elapsed = tick() - t0
            if elapsed > _G.ViolenceSemiState.HOLD_MAX then break end
            if not prompt.Parent or prompt.Enabled == false then break end

            local dNow = lockedSpawn and _G.distToSpawn(lockedSpawn) or math.huge
            _G.setStealProgress(math.clamp(elapsed / _G.ViolenceSemiState.HOLD_MAX, 0, 1), "WAITING RANGE", dNow)

            if dNow <= _G.ViolenceSemiState.RADIUS then
                local canFire = true
                if not alreadyInRange then
                    task.wait(_G.ViolenceSemiState.ENTRY_DELAY)
                    dNow = lockedSpawn and _G.distToSpawn(lockedSpawn) or math.huge
                    if dNow > _G.ViolenceSemiState.RADIUS then
                        alreadyInRange = false
                        canFire = false
                    end
                end
                if canFire and Steal.AutoStealEnabled and Steal.StealMode == "Semi" and prompt.Parent then
                    if not data.useFallback and #data.trigger > 0 then
                        for _, fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do
                            task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end)
                        end
                    elseif type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
                        pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(prompt) end)
                    else
                        pcall(function()
                            if prompt.InputHoldEnd then prompt:InputHoldEnd() end
                        end)
                    end
                    fired = true
                    break
                end
            end
            task.wait()
        end

        if fired then
            _G.ViolenceSemiState.phase = "success"
            _G.setStealProgress(1, "SUCCESS")
        else
            _G.ViolenceSemiState.phase = "failed"
            _G.setStealProgress(1, "FAILED")
        end

        task.wait(_G.ViolenceSemiState.COOLDOWN)
        data.ready = true
        isStealing = false
        _G.ViolenceSemiState.active = false
        _G.ViolenceSemiState.lockedSpawn = nil
        -- barra some depois (igual Adapt ~0.8s)
        task.delay(0.8, function()
            if not _G.ViolenceSemiState.active then
                _G.ViolenceSemiState.phase = "idle"
                resetProgressBar()
                if _G.ViolenceStealBar and _G.ViolenceStealBar.SetState then
                    pcall(_G.ViolenceStealBar.SetState, Steal.AutoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
                end
            end
        end)
    end)
end

local function startAutoSteal()
    if Conns.autoSteal then return end
    Conns.autoSteal = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Steal.AutoStealEnabled then return end
        if isStealing or (_G.ViolenceSemiState and _G.ViolenceSemiState.active) then return end
        local p, d = findNearestPrompt()
        if p then
            if Steal.StealMode == "Semi" then
                _G.executeSemiSteal(p, d or math.huge)
            else
                executeSteal(p)
            end
        else
            if not isStealing and not (_G.ViolenceSemiState and _G.ViolenceSemiState.active) then
                _G.setStealProgress(0, "IDLE")
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function stopAutoSteal()
    if Conns.autoSteal then
        Conns.autoSteal:Disconnect()
        Conns.autoSteal = nil
    end
    if Conns.progress then
        Conns.progress:Disconnect()
        Conns.progress = nil
    end
    isStealing = false
    lastStealTick = 0
    if _G.ViolenceSemiState then
        _G.ViolenceSemiState.active = false
        _G.ViolenceSemiState.phase = "idle"
        _G.ViolenceSemiState.lockedSpawn = nil
    end
    Steal.cachedPrompts = {}
    Steal.promptCacheTime = 0
    resetProgressBar()
end

-- ============================================================
-- FOV (Anti-Sammy camera logic)
-- ============================================================
local fovOriginalCameras = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local fovConn = nil

local function applyStretchFOV(val)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then
        pcall(function()
            cam.FieldOfView = val
        end)
    end
end

local function applyFOV()
    if fovConn then
        fovConn:Disconnect()
        fovConn = nil
    end

    if not stretchEnabled then
        for cam, original in pairs(fovOriginalCameras) do
            if cam and cam.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    cam.FieldOfView = original
                end)
            end
        end
        return
    end

    fovConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            if fovOriginalCameras[cam] == nil then
                fovOriginalCameras[cam] = cam.FieldOfView
            end
            cam.FieldOfView = stretchFOV
        end
    end)

    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then
        if fovOriginalCameras[cam] == nil then
            fovOriginalCameras[cam] = cam.FieldOfView
        end
        cam.FieldOfView = stretchFOV
    end
end

local function enableStretch()
    stretchEnabled = true
    applyFOV()
end

local function disableStretch()
    stretchEnabled = false
    applyFOV()
end

applyFOV()


-- ===================== PLAYER ESP + TRACER =====================
_G.ViolenceESP = _G.ViolenceESP or {
	playerESP = true,
	tracerESP = false,
	highlightESP = false,
	entries = {},
	conn = nil,
	lineConn = nil,
	playerAdded = nil,
	playerRemoving = nil,
	hasDrawing = (typeof(Drawing) == "table" and type(Drawing.new) == "function"),
}

_G.ViolenceESP.clearPlayer = function(plr)
	local E = _G.ViolenceESP
	local e = E.entries[plr]
	if not e then return end
	if e.highlight then pcall(function() e.highlight:Destroy() end) end
	if e.billboard then pcall(function() e.billboard:Destroy() end) end
	if e.line then pcall(function() e.line:Remove() end) end
	if e.lineFrame then pcall(function() e.lineFrame:Destroy() end) end
	if e.charConn then pcall(function() e.charConn:Disconnect() end) end
	E.entries[plr] = nil
end

_G.ViolenceESP.makeEntry = function(plr)
	local E = _G.ViolenceESP
	if plr == LP then return end
	E.clearPlayer(plr)
	local entry = { highlight = nil, billboard = nil, line = nil, lineFrame = nil, charConn = nil }
	E.entries[plr] = entry

	local function attach(char)
		if not char then return end
		if entry.highlight then pcall(function() entry.highlight:Destroy() end) entry.highlight = nil end
		if entry.billboard then pcall(function() entry.billboard:Destroy() end) entry.billboard = nil end
		if entry.line then pcall(function() entry.line:Remove() end) entry.line = nil end
		if entry.lineFrame then pcall(function() entry.lineFrame:Destroy() end) entry.lineFrame = nil end

		-- Highlight separado (lógica Noxa)
		local themeCol = (_G.ViolenceBtnColor and _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		if E.highlightESP then
			local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
			hl.Name = "ViolenceESP_HL"
			hl.Adornee = char
			hl.FillColor = themeCol
			hl.OutlineColor = themeCol
			hl.FillTransparency = 0.65
			hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
			hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
			hl.Parent = char
			entry.highlight = hl
		end

		-- Tracer LINE (Drawing API se disponivel; senao updateLines usa Frame)
		if E.tracerESP then
			if E.hasDrawing then
				local ok, line = pcall(function()
					local l = Drawing.new("Line")
					l.Thickness = 1.5
					l.Color = themeCol
					l.Transparency = 1
					l.Visible = false
					return l
				end)
				if ok and line then entry.line = line end
			end
			-- lineFrame e criado sob demanda em updateLines se nao houver Drawing
		end

		-- Player ESP: display name + username + avatar circle
		if E.playerESP then
			local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not head then return end
			local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
			bb.Name = "ViolenceESP_BB"
			bb.Adornee = head
			bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 70)
			bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)
			bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
			bb.MaxDistance = 1000
			bb.Parent = head
			entry.billboard = bb

			local root = Instance.new("Frame", bb)
			root.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			root.BackgroundTransparency = 1

			local circle = Instance.new("Frame", root)
			circle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 36)
			circle.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 0)
			circle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
			circle.BorderSizePixel = 0
			Instance.new("UICorner", circle).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
			local cStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", circle)
			cStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			cStroke.Thickness = 1.5

			local avatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel", circle)
			avatar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4)
			avatar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
			avatar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			avatar.Image = ""
			Instance.new("UICorner", avatar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok, content = pcall(function()
					return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(plr.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
				end)
				if ok and content and avatar and avatar.Parent then
					avatar.Image = content
				end
			end)

			local dName = Instance.new("TextLabel", root)
			dName.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
			dName.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 38)
			dName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			dName.Text = plr.DisplayName or plr.Name
			dName.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			dName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			dName.TextSize = 12
			dName.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
			dName.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

			local uName = Instance.new("TextLabel", root)
			uName.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
			uName.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 52)
			uName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			uName.Text = "@" .. plr.Name
			uName.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
			uName.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
			uName.TextSize = 11
			uName.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
			uName.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
		end
	end

	if plr.Character then attach(plr.Character) end
	entry.charConn = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
		task.wait(0.3)
		attach(char)
	end)
end

_G.ViolenceESP.refresh = function()
	local E = _G.ViolenceESP
	for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if plr ~= LP then
			if E.playerESP or E.tracerESP or E.highlightESP then
				E.makeEntry(plr)
			else
				E.clearPlayer(plr)
			end
		end
	end
end

_G.ViolenceESP.ensureLineGui = function()
	local E = _G.ViolenceESP
	if E.lineGui and E.lineGui.Parent then return E.lineGui end
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	gui.Name = "ViolenceESP_Lines"
	gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
	gui.DisplayOrder = 100
	gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
	pcall(function() if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(gui) end end)
	if not pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) then
		gui.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
	end
	E.lineGui = gui
	return gui
end

_G.ViolenceESP.updateLines = function()
	local E = _G.ViolenceESP
	if not E.tracerESP then return end
	local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not cam then return end
	local vp = cam.ViewportSize
	local themeCol = (_G.ViolenceBtnColor and _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	-- linha sai do centro-baixo da tela
	local fromPos = Vector2.new(vp.X / 2, vp.Y)
	local myChar = LP.Character
	local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if myRoot then
		local sp = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(myRoot.Position)
		if sp.Z > 0 then
			fromPos = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y)
		end
	end

	for plr, entry in pairs(E.entries) do
		local char = plr.Character
		local root = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Head"))
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local alive = hum and hum.Health > 0

		if root and alive then
			local sp = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
			if sp.Z > 0 then
				local toPos = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y)
				-- Drawing API
				if entry.line then
					entry.line.From = fromPos
					entry.line.To = toPos
					entry.line.Color = themeCol
					entry.line.Visible = true
				else
					-- Fallback: Frame rotacionado na ScreenGui
					if not entry.lineFrame then
						local gui = E.ensureLineGui()
						local fr = Instance.new("Frame")
						fr.Name = "Tracer_" .. tostring(plr.UserId)
						fr.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
						fr.BorderSizePixel = 0
						fr.BackgroundColor3 = themeCol
						fr.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
						fr.ZIndex = 50
						fr.Parent = gui
						entry.lineFrame = fr
					end
					local dx = toPos.X - fromPos.X
					local dy = toPos.Y - fromPos.Y
					local dist = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
					local midX = (fromPos.X + toPos.X) / 2
					local midY = (fromPos.Y + toPos.Y) / 2
					local angle = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
					entry.lineFrame.BackgroundColor3 = themeCol
					entry.lineFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, dist, 0, 1.5)
					entry.lineFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, midX, 0, midY)
					entry.lineFrame.Rotation = angle
					entry.lineFrame.Visible = true
				end
			else
				if entry.line then entry.line.Visible = false end
				if entry.lineFrame then entry.lineFrame.Visible = false end
			end
		else
			if entry.line then entry.line.Visible = false end
			if entry.lineFrame then entry.lineFrame.Visible = false end
		end
	end
end

_G.ViolenceESP.startLineLoop = function()
	local E = _G.ViolenceESP
	if E.lineConn then return end
	E.lineConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		pcall(E.updateLines)
	end)
end

_G.ViolenceESP.stopLineLoop = function()
	local E = _G.ViolenceESP
	if E.lineConn then
		pcall(function() E.lineConn:Disconnect() end)
		E.lineConn = nil
	end
	for _, entry in pairs(E.entries) do
		if entry.line then
			pcall(function() entry.line.Visible = false end)
		end
	end
end

_G.ViolenceESP.start = function()
	local E = _G.ViolenceESP
	E.refresh()
	if E.tracerESP then
		E.startLineLoop()
	else
		E.stopLineLoop()
	end
	if not E.playerAdded then
		E.playerAdded = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
			task.wait(1)
			if E.playerESP or E.tracerESP or E.highlightESP then
				E.makeEntry(plr)
			end
		end)
	end
	if not E.playerRemoving then
		E.playerRemoving = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
			E.clearPlayer(plr)
		end)
	end
end

_G.ViolenceESP.stop = function()
	local E = _G.ViolenceESP
	E.stopLineLoop()
	for plr, _ in pairs(E.entries) do
		E.clearPlayer(plr)
	end
	if E.lineGui then pcall(function() E.lineGui:Destroy() end) E.lineGui = nil end
	if E.playerAdded then pcall(function() E.playerAdded:Disconnect() end) E.playerAdded = nil end
	if E.playerRemoving then pcall(function() E.playerRemoving:Disconnect() end) E.playerRemoving = nil end
end

_G.ViolenceESP.setPlayerESP = function(on)
	_G.ViolenceESP.playerESP = on and true or false
	if _G.ViolenceESP.playerESP or _G.ViolenceESP.tracerESP or _G.ViolenceESP.highlightESP then
		_G.ViolenceESP.start()
	else
		_G.ViolenceESP.stop()
	end
end

_G.ViolenceESP.setTracerESP = function(on)
	_G.ViolenceESP.tracerESP = on and true or false
	if _G.ViolenceESP.playerESP or _G.ViolenceESP.tracerESP or _G.ViolenceESP.highlightESP then
		_G.ViolenceESP.start()
	else
		_G.ViolenceESP.stop()
	end
end

-- Highlight separado (lógica Noxa): pode funcionar sozinho,
-- sem depender do Player ESP ou do Tracer.
_G.ViolenceESP.setHighlight = function(on)
	local E = _G.ViolenceESP
	E.highlightESP = on and true or false
	if E.playerESP or E.tracerESP or E.highlightESP then
		E.start()
	else
		E.stop()
	end
end

local enemySpeedConn = nil
local function updateEnemySpeedLabels()
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LP then
            local char = player.Character
            if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Health > 0 then
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local velocity = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                local speed = (Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z).Magnitude)
                local label = enemySpeedLabels[player]
                if not label then
                    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    if head then
                        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui", head)
                        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 25)
                        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
                        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
                        bb.Name = "EnemySpeedGui"
                        local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
                        textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                        textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        textLabel.Text = string.format("%.1f", speed)
                        textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                        textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
                        textLabel.TextScaled = true
                        textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                        textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 8)
                        label = textLabel
                        enemySpeedLabels[player] = label
                    end
                elseif label and label.Parent and label.Parent.Parent ~= char then
                    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    if head then
                        label.Parent.Parent = head
                    end
                end
                if label then
                    label.Text = string.format("%.1f", speed)
                end
            else
                local label = enemySpeedLabels[player]
                if label and label.Parent and label.Parent.Parent then
                    label.Parent.Parent = nil
                end
                enemySpeedLabels[player] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    for player, label in pairs(enemySpeedLabels) do
        if not player or not player.Character or not player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            if label and label.Parent and label.Parent.Parent then
                label.Parent.Parent = nil
            end
            enemySpeedLabels[player] = nil
        end
    end
end

local function startEnemySpeed()
    if enemySpeedConn then enemySpeedConn:Disconnect() end
    enemySpeedConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        updateEnemySpeedLabels()
    end)
end

local uiLocked = false
local mobileButtonsHidden = false
local MobilePanel = nil
local function applyMobileButtonsHidden()
    local show = not mobileButtonsHidden
    pcall(function()
        if MobilePanel then
            MobilePanel.Enabled = show
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if type(_G.ViolenceMobileButtons) == "table" then
            for _, data in pairs(_G.ViolenceMobileButtons) do
                if data and data.btn then
                    local holder = data.btn.Parent
                    if holder then holder.Visible = show end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    -- Reset / Bat V2 / TP Bat (ScreenGuis separados)
    pcall(function()
        local names = {"BypassButton", "TPBatButton", "InstantResetButton"}
        local parents = {}
        pcall(function() table.insert(parents, game:GetService("CoreGui")) end)
        pcall(function()
            local pg = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
            if pg then table.insert(parents, pg:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")) end
        end)
        for _, parent in ipairs(parents) do
            if parent then
                for _, name in ipairs(names) do
                    local g = parent:FindFirstChild(name)
                    if g then
                        if g:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                            g.Enabled = show
                        else
                            g.Visible = show
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local function setGui(g)
            if not g or typeof(g) ~= "Instance" then return end
            if g:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                g.Enabled = show
            else
                g.Visible = show
            end
        end
        -- refs diretas (btn/floatBtn SAO o ScreenGui)
        if _G.ViolenceTPBat then setGui(_G.ViolenceTPBat.btn) end
        if _G.ViolenceReset then setGui(_G.ViolenceReset.floatBtn) end
        setGui(bypassFloatingButton)
    end)
end

local mobSetDropBR, mobSetTpDown, mobSetCarry, mobSetLagger1, mobSetLagger2

local KB = {
    DropBrainrot={kb=Enum.KeyCode.X,gp=nil},
    AutoLeft    ={kb=Enum.KeyCode.Z,gp=nil},
    AutoRight   ={kb=Enum.KeyCode.C,gp=nil},
    AutoBat     ={kb=Enum.KeyCode.E,gp=nil},

    GuiHide     ={kb=Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl,gp=nil},

    InstantReset={kb=Enum.KeyCode.G,gp=nil},
}

local GAMEPAD_KEYS={
    [Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.ButtonY]=true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL1]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2]=true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL3]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR3]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.ButtonStart]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.ButtonSelect]=true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.DPadDown]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight]=true
}

local function isGamepadInput(inp)
    return inp and inp.UserInputType and inp.UserInputType.Name:match("^Gamepad") ~= nil
end

local function isBindableInput(inp)
    if not inp or inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return false end
    if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return true end
    return isGamepadInput(inp) and GAMEPAD_KEYS[inp.KeyCode] == true
end

local function kbMatch(entry, kc)
    return kc and (kc == entry.kb or (entry.gp and kc == entry.gp))
end

local lastMoveDir = Vector3.new(0,0,0)

local MOVE_KEYS={[Enum.KeyCode.W]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.A]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.S]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.D]=true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.Up]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.Left]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.Down]=true,[Enum.KeyCode.Right]=true}

local steppedConn = nil
local movementLoop = nil

steppedConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LP and p.Character then
            for _, part in ipairs(p.Character:GetChildren()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    part.CanCollide = false
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

movementLoop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not hrp then return end
    if not autoBatEnabled and not bypassToggled and not _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggled and not autoLeftEnabled and not autoRightEnabled then
        if not (_G.ViolenceAntiDrop and _G.ViolenceAntiDrop.enabled) then
            local md = hum.MoveDirection
            local spd
            do
                local sp = _G.ViolenceSpeeds or {}
                if laggerToggled then
                    spd = (laggerLevel == 2) and (tonumber(sp.L2) or 15) or (tonumber(sp.L1) or 20)
                else
                    spd = speedMode and (tonumber(sp.CS) or 25) or (tonumber(sp.NS) or 45)
                end
            end
            if md.Magnitude > 0 then
                lastMoveDir = md
                hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(md.X * spd, hrp.Velocity.Y, md.Z * spd)
            elseif antiRagdollEnabled and lastMoveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
                local anyHeld = false
                for key in pairs(MOVE_KEYS) do
                    if UIS:IsKeyDown(key) then anyHeld = true; break end
                end
                if anyHeld then
                    hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(lastMoveDir.X * spd, hrp.Velocity.Y, lastMoveDir.Z * spd)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if speedLabel then
        local dispSpd
        if _G.ViolenceAntiDrop and _G.ViolenceAntiDrop.enabled and _G.ViolenceAntiDrop._getDisplaySpeed then
            dispSpd = _G.ViolenceAntiDrop._getDisplaySpeed(hum)
        else
            dispSpd = Vector3.new(hrp.Velocity.X, 0, hrp.Velocity.Z).Magnitude
        end
        speedLabel.Text = formatSpeedModeText(dispSpd)
    end
end)

local alConn, arConn = nil, nil
local alPhase, arPhase = 1, 1

local function stopAutoLeft()
    if alConn then alConn:Disconnect(); alConn = nil end
    alPhase = 1
    local char = LP.Character
    if char then
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false) end
    end
    if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
    if mobSetAutoLeft then mobSetAutoLeft(false) end
end

local function stopAutoRight()
    if arConn then arConn:Disconnect(); arConn = nil end
    arPhase = 1
    local char = LP.Character
    if char then
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false) end
    end
    if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(false) end
    if mobSetAutoRight then mobSetAutoRight(false) end
end

local function disableAllAimbots()
    if autoBatEnabled then
        disableAutoBat()
        if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end
        if mobSetAutoBat then mobSetAutoBat(false) end
    end
    if bypassToggled then
        toggleBypass(false)
    end
    if _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggled then
        _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggle(false)
    end
end

function startAutoLeft()
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        stopAutoRight()
        if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(false) end
        if mobSetAutoRight then mobSetAutoRight(false) end
    end
    disableAllAimbots()
    if alConn then alConn:Disconnect() end
    alPhase = 1
    alConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not autoLeftEnabled then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not hum then return end
        local spd
        do
            local sp = _G.ViolenceSpeeds or {}
            if laggerToggled then
                spd = (laggerLevel == 2) and (tonumber(sp.L2) or 15) or (tonumber(sp.L1) or 20)
            elseif speedMode then
                spd = tonumber(sp.CS) or 25
            else
                spd = tonumber(sp.NS) or 45
            end
        end
        if alPhase == 1 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(AP.L1.X, root.Position.Y, AP.L1.Z)
            if (tgt - root.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                alPhase = 2
                local d = AP.L2 - root.Position
                local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
                hum:Move(mv, false)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
                return
            end
            local d = AP.L1 - root.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
        elseif alPhase == 2 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(AP.L2.X, root.Position.Y, AP.L2.Z)
            if (tgt - root.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                autoLeftEnabled = false
                if alConn then alConn:Disconnect(); alConn = nil end
                alPhase = 1
                if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
                if mobSetAutoLeft then mobSetAutoLeft(false) end
                local facePos = Vector3.new(AP.L_FACE.X, root.Position.Y, AP.L_FACE.Z)
                if (facePos - root.Position).Magnitude > 0.01 then
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position, facePos)
                end
                return
            end
            local d = AP.L2 - root.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
        end
    end)
end

function startAutoRight()
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        stopAutoLeft()
        if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
        if mobSetAutoLeft then mobSetAutoLeft(false) end
    end
    disableAllAimbots()
    if arConn then arConn:Disconnect() end
    arPhase = 1
    arConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not autoRightEnabled then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not hum then return end
        local spd
        do
            local sp = _G.ViolenceSpeeds or {}
            if laggerToggled then
                spd = (laggerLevel == 2) and (tonumber(sp.L2) or 15) or (tonumber(sp.L1) or 20)
            elseif speedMode then
                spd = tonumber(sp.CS) or 25
            else
                spd = tonumber(sp.NS) or 45
            end
        end
        if arPhase == 1 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(AP.R1.X, root.Position.Y, AP.R1.Z)
            if (tgt - root.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                arPhase = 2
                local d = AP.R2 - root.Position
                local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
                hum:Move(mv, false)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
                return
            end
            local d = AP.R1 - root.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
        elseif arPhase == 2 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(AP.R2.X, root.Position.Y, AP.R2.Z)
            if (tgt - root.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                autoRightEnabled = false
                if arConn then arConn:Disconnect(); arConn = nil end
                arPhase = 1
                if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(false) end
