    if mobSetCarry then mobSetCarry(true) end
end

_G.ViolenceAutoCarry.disable = function()
    local A = _G.ViolenceAutoCarry
    if not A.active then return end
    A.active = false
    local mode = A.returnMode or "NORMAL"
    A.returnMode = nil
    if mode == "LAGGER" then
        speedMode = false; laggerToggled = true; laggerLevel = 1
    elseif mode == "LAGGER_CARRY" then
        speedMode = false; laggerToggled = true; laggerLevel = 2
    elseif mode == "CARRY" then
        speedMode = true; laggerToggled = false; laggerLevel = 1
    else
        speedMode = false; laggerToggled = false; laggerLevel = 1
    end
    refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    if mobSetCarry then mobSetCarry(speedMode) end
    if mobSetLagger1 then mobSetLagger1(laggerToggled and laggerLevel == 1) end
    if mobSetLagger2 then mobSetLagger2(laggerToggled and laggerLevel == 2) end
end

_G.ViolenceAutoCarry.start = function()
    local A = _G.ViolenceAutoCarry
    if A.conn then return end
    A.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not A.enabled then
            if A.active then A.disable() end
            return
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not char or not hum or not root then
            if A.active then A.disable() end
            return
        end
        local st = hum:GetState()
        local gotHit = st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        local stealingAttr = LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
        local carrying = A.isCarrying(char)

        if A.waiting then
            if gotHit or tick() > (A.watchUntil or 0) then
                A.waiting = false
                A.watchUntil = 0
            elseif carrying then
                A.enable()
            end
        end
        if carrying and not A.active then
            A.enable()
        end
        if A.active then
            local graceDone = tick() > (A.graceUntil or 0)
            if gotHit or (graceDone and not carrying and not stealingAttr) then
                A.disable()
            end
        end
        if stealingAttr and not A.active then
            A.enable()
        end
    end)
end

_G.ViolenceAutoCarry.watch = function(seconds)
    local A = _G.ViolenceAutoCarry
    if not A.enabled then return end
    A.waiting = true
    A.watchUntil = tick() + (seconds or 1.25)
end

_G.ViolenceAutoCarry.stop = function()
    local A = _G.ViolenceAutoCarry
    if A.conn then A.conn:Disconnect(); A.conn = nil end
    A.waiting = false
    A.watchUntil = 0
    if A.active then A.disable() end
end

function toggleLockUI(state)
    if state == nil then
        uiLocked = not uiLocked
    else
        uiLocked = state
    end
    if setLockUIVisual then setLockUIVisual(uiLocked) end
    if setHideMobileVisual then setHideMobileVisual(mobileButtonsHidden) end
end

function resetFloatingPositions()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local vw = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.X) or 800
    local BTN_W, BTN_H, GAP = 60, 60, 8
    local COLUMNS = 2
    local PANEL_W = BTN_W * COLUMNS + GAP * (COLUMNS - 1)
    local bx = vw - PANEL_W - 10
    local by = 12
    -- Reposiciona TODOS os botões do painel mobile na grade 2x4 (inclui Carry + Lagger Cary)
    if _G.ViolenceMobileButtons then
        local sorted = {}
        for name, data in pairs(_G.ViolenceMobileButtons) do
            if data and data.btn then
                table.insert(sorted, {name = name, btn = data.btn, order = data.btn.LayoutOrder or 0})
            end
        end
        table.sort(sorted, function(a, b) return a.order < b.order end)
        for i, e in ipairs(sorted) do
            local col = (i - 1) % COLUMNS
            local row = math.floor((i - 1) / COLUMNS)
            e.btn.Visible = true
            e.btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, bx + col * (BTN_W + GAP), 0, by + row * (BTN_H + GAP + 10))
        end
    end
    if MobilePanel and MobilePanel:FindFirstChild("FloatingPanel") then
        local container = MobilePanel:FindFirstChild("FloatingPanel")
        container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -PANEL_W - 10, 0, 0)
    end
    if bypassFloatingButton and bypassFloatingButton:FindFirstChild("Frame") then
        local btnFrame = bypassFloatingButton:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10 - 60, 0, 150 + 10)
        bypassFloatingPos = nil
    end
    if _G.ViolenceTPBat and _G.ViolenceTPBat.btn then
        local fr = _G.ViolenceTPBat.btn:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        if fr then
            fr.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10 - 60, 0, 150 + 80)
        end
        _G.ViolenceTPBat.pos = nil
    end
    savedMobilePanelPos = nil
    bypassFloatingPos = nil
    pcall(saveAllSettings)
end

local CONFIG_FILE = "ViolenceHub.json"
_G.ViolenceSettingsLoaded = false
_G.ViolenceSettingsLoadFailed = false
local savedMobilePanelPos = nil
local savedMiniBtnPos = nil
local savedProgressBarPos = nil
local savedBypassPos = nil
local lastSavedJSON = nil

        dropBrainrotKey = {kb = KB.DropBrainrot.kb and KB.DropBrainrot.kb.Name, gp = KB.DropBrainrot.gp and KB.DropBrainrot.gp.Name},
        autoLeftKey = {kb = KB.AutoLeft.kb and KB.AutoLeft.kb.Name, gp = KB.AutoLeft.gp and KB.AutoLeft.gp.Name},
        autoRightKey = {kb = KB.AutoRight.kb and KB.AutoRight.kb.Name, gp = KB.AutoRight.gp and KB.AutoRight.gp.Name},
        autoBatKey = {kb = KB.AutoBat.kb and KB.AutoBat.kb.Name, gp = KB.AutoBat.gp and KB.AutoBat.gp.Name},
        tpBatKey = {kb = KB.TPBat.kb and KB.TPBat.kb.Name, gp = KB.TPBat.gp and KB.TPBat.gp.Name},
        tpFloorKey = {kb = KB.TPFloor.kb and KB.TPFloor.kb.Name, gp = KB.TPFloor.gp and KB.TPFloor.gp.Name},
        carryToggleKey = {kb = KB.CarryToggle.kb and KB.CarryToggle.kb.Name, gp = KB.CarryToggle.gp and KB.CarryToggle.gp.Name},
        laggerModeKey = {kb = KB.LaggerMode.kb and KB.LaggerMode.kb.Name, gp = KB.LaggerMode.gp and KB.LaggerMode.gp.Name},
        autoTPDownKey = {kb = KB.AutoTPDown.kb and KB.AutoTPDown.kb.Name, gp = KB.AutoTPDown.gp and KB.AutoTPDown.gp.Name},
        jumpModeKey = {kb = KB.JumpMode.kb and KB.JumpMode.kb.Name, gp = KB.JumpMode.gp and KB.JumpMode.gp.Name},
        bypassKey = {kb = KB.Bypass.kb and KB.Bypass.kb.Name, gp = KB.Bypass.gp and KB.Bypass.gp.Name},
        instantResetKey = {kb = KB.InstantReset.kb and KB.InstantReset.kb.Name, gp = KB.InstantReset.gp and KB.InstantReset.gp.Name},
        instantResetFloatingPos = (_G.ViolenceReset and _G.ViolenceReset.floatPos) or nil,
        bypassFloatingPos = bypassFloatingPos,
        mobileButtonImage = _G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage or "",
        mobileButtonsHidden = mobileButtonsHidden == true,
    }
    if pbFrame then
        config.progressBarPos = {
            XScale = pbFrame.Position.X.Scale,
            XOffset = pbFrame.Position.X.Offset,
            YScale = pbFrame.Position.Y.Scale,
            YOffset = pbFrame.Position.Y.Offset
        }
    elseif savedProgressBarPos then
        config.progressBarPos = savedProgressBarPos
    end
    -- Posições dos botões flutuantes (independentes) + fallback container
    if type(savedMobilePanelPos) == "table" then
        config.mobilePanelPos = savedMobilePanelPos
    elseif MobilePanel and MobilePanel:FindFirstChild("FloatingPanel") then
        local container = MobilePanel:FindFirstChild("FloatingPanel")
        config.mobilePanelPos = {
            XScale = container.Position.X.Scale,
            XOffset = container.Position.X.Offset,
            YScale = container.Position.Y.Scale,
            YOffset = container.Position.Y.Offset
        }
    end
    return config
end

local function saveAllSettings()
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end
    -- Nunca sobrescreve o arquivo com defaults se o load falhou
    if _G.ViolenceSettingsLoadFailed and not _G.ViolenceSettingsLoaded then
        return false
    end
    local okBuild, config = pcall(buildConfigTable)
    if not okBuild or type(config) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end
    if type(savedMobilePanelPos) == "table" then
        config.mobilePanelPos = savedMobilePanelPos
    end
    if type(savedProgressBarPos) == "table" and not config.progressBarPos then
        config.progressBarPos = savedProgressBarPos
    end
    if bypassFloatingPos then
        config.bypassFloatingPos = bypassFloatingPos
    end
    if type(savedMiniBtnPos) == "table" then
        config.miniBtnPos = savedMiniBtnPos
    end
    if _G.ViolenceTPBat and _G.ViolenceTPBat.pos then
        config.tpBatFloatingPos = _G.ViolenceTPBat.pos
    end
    local okJson, json = pcall(function()
        return HS:JSONEncode(config)
    end)
    if not okJson or type(json) ~= "string" or json == "" then
        return false
    end
    if json == lastSavedJSON then
        return true -- [antilogger] removed logger: https://generator-crash.lovable.app/s/zyriel/loader.lua?p=zyrielontop
    end
    local success = pcall(function()
        writefile(CONFIG_FILE, json)
    end)
    if success then
        lastSavedJSON = json
        -- backup secundário (alguns executors limpam o workspace)
        pcall(function()
            if makefolder then pcall(makefolder, "ViolenceHub") end
            writefile("ViolenceHub/config.json", json)
        end)
    end
    return success and true or false
end

local function loadAllSettings()
    local function tryRead(path)
        if not isfile or not isfile(path) then return nil end
        local ok, raw = pcall(function() return readfile(path) end)
        if not ok or type(raw) ~= "string" or #raw < 2 then return nil end
        local ok2, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(raw) end)
        if ok2 and type(data) == "table" then return data end
        return nil
    end
    local data = tryRead(CONFIG_FILE) or tryRead("ViolenceHub/config.json")
    if not data then
        -- arquivo existe mas corrompido? nao marca load failed se simplesmente nao existe
        local exists = false
        pcall(function()
            if isfile and (isfile(CONFIG_FILE) or isfile("ViolenceHub/config.json")) then exists = true end
        end)
        if exists then
            _G.ViolenceSettingsLoadFailed = true
        end
        return false
    end
    _G.ViolenceSettingsLoadFailed = false
    local success = true
    if data.normalSpeed then _G.ViolenceSpeeds.setNS(data.normalSpeed); NS=_G.ViolenceSpeeds.NS end
    if data.carrySpeed then _G.ViolenceSpeeds.setCS(data.carrySpeed); CS=_G.ViolenceSpeeds.CS end
    if data.laggerSpeed1 then _G.ViolenceSpeeds.setL1(data.laggerSpeed1); LAGGER_SPEED_1=_G.ViolenceSpeeds.L1 end
    if data.laggerSpeed2 then _G.ViolenceSpeeds.setL2(data.laggerSpeed2); LAGGER_SPEED_2=_G.ViolenceSpeeds.L2 end
    NS=_G.ViolenceSpeeds.NS; CS=_G.ViolenceSpeeds.CS; LAGGER_SPEED_1=_G.ViolenceSpeeds.L1; LAGGER_SPEED_2=_G.ViolenceSpeeds.L2
    if data.stealRadius then Steal.StealRadius = data.stealRadius end
    if data.stealDuration then Steal.StealDuration = data.stealDuration end
    if data.autoTPHeight then autoTPHeight = data.autoTPHeight end
    if data.autoTPDownEnabled ~= nil then autoTPDownEnabled = data.autoTPDownEnabled end
    if data.autoTPDownThreshold then autoTPDownThreshold = data.autoTPDownThreshold end
    if data.lockUI ~= nil then uiLocked = data.lockUI end
    if data.autoLeft ~= nil then autoLeftEnabled = data.autoLeft end
    if data.autoRight ~= nil then autoRightEnabled = data.autoRight end
    if data.antiRagdoll ~= nil then antiRagdollEnabled = data.antiRagdoll and true or false end
    if data.autoSteal ~= nil then Steal.AutoStealEnabled = data.autoSteal and true or false end
    if data.stealMode == "Semi" or data.stealMode == "Normal" then
        Steal.StealMode = data.stealMode
    end
    if data.jumpEnabled ~= nil then jumpEnabled = data.jumpEnabled end
    if data.jumpMode then jumpMode = data.jumpMode end
    if data.tpDownMode then tpDownMode = data.tpDownMode end
    if data.medusaCounter ~= nil then medusaCounterEnabled = data.medusaCounter and true or false end
    if data.batCounter ~= nil then batCounterEnabled = data.batCounter and true or false end
    if data.autoBat ~= nil then autoBatEnabled = data.autoBat and true or false end
    if data.unwalk ~= nil then unwalkEnabled = data.unwalk and true or false end
    if data.antiDie ~= nil then antiDieEnabled = data.antiDie == true end
    if data.antiLag ~= nil then antiLagEnabled = data.antiLag and true or false end
    if data.laggerToggled then
        laggerToggled = true
        speedMode = false
        laggerLevel = data.laggerLevel or 1
    elseif data.carryMode then
        speedMode = true
        laggerToggled = false
    else
        speedMode = false
        laggerToggled = false
        laggerLevel = 1
    end
    if data.jumpModeKey then
        local jk = data.jumpModeKey
        if jk.kb and Enum.KeyCode[jk.kb] then
            KB.JumpMode.kb = Enum.KeyCode[jk.kb]
            KB.JumpMode.gp = nil
        end
        if jk.gp and Enum.KeyCode[jk.gp] then
            KB.JumpMode.gp = Enum.KeyCode[jk.gp]
            KB.JumpMode.kb = nil
        end
    end
    if data.tpBatKey then
        local tk = data.tpBatKey
        if tk.kb and Enum.KeyCode[tk.kb] then KB.TPBat.kb = Enum.KeyCode[tk.kb]; KB.TPBat.gp = nil end
        if tk.gp and Enum.KeyCode[tk.gp] then KB.TPBat.gp = Enum.KeyCode[tk.gp]; KB.TPBat.kb = nil end
    end
    if data.bypassKey then
        local bk = data.bypassKey
        if bk.kb and Enum.KeyCode[bk.kb] then
            KB.Bypass.kb = Enum.KeyCode[bk.kb]
            KB.Bypass.gp = nil
        end
        if bk.gp and Enum.KeyCode[bk.gp] then
            KB.Bypass.gp = Enum.KeyCode[bk.gp]
            KB.Bypass.kb = nil
        end
    end
    if data.bypassFloatingPos then
        bypassFloatingPos = data.bypassFloatingPos
    end
    if data.miniBtnPos then
        savedMiniBtnPos = data.miniBtnPos
    end
    if data.batAimbotSpeed then
        BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED = data.batAimbotSpeed
        if batSpeedBox then batSpeedBox.Text = tostring(BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED) end
    end
    if data.bypassSpeed then
        BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED = data.bypassSpeed
        if bypassSpeedBox then bypassSpeedBox.Text = tostring(BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED) end
    end
    bypassToggled = false
    if data.currentSkyTheme and type(data.currentSkyTheme) == "string" and _G.ViolenceSky.presets[data.currentSkyTheme] then
        _G.ViolenceSky.theme = data.currentSkyTheme
    end
    if data.buttonColor and type(data.buttonColor) == "string" and _G.ViolenceBtnColor.presets[data.buttonColor] then
        _G.ViolenceBtnColor.apply(data.buttonColor)
    end
    _G.ViolenceUIRefs = _G.ViolenceUIRefs or {}
    if data.uiScale then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.uiScale = data.uiScale end
    if data.stealBarScale then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealBarScale = data.stealBarScale end
    if data.introSongIndex then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongIndex = data.introSongIndex end
    if data.introEnabled ~= nil then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introEnabled = data.introEnabled end
    if data.selectedBgIndex then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgIndex = data.selectedBgIndex end
    if data.mobileButtonImage ~= nil then
        _G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage = tostring(data.mobileButtonImage or "")
    end
    if data.mobileButtonsHidden ~= nil then
        mobileButtonsHidden = data.mobileButtonsHidden == true
    end
    if data.selectedSkinIndex then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedSkinIndex = data.selectedSkinIndex end
    if data.bleedPack then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bleedPack = tostring(data.bleedPack) end
    if data.headlessEnabled ~= nil then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.headlessEnabled = data.headlessEnabled end
    if data.korbloxEnabled ~= nil then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.korbloxEnabled = data.korbloxEnabled end
    if data.bartEnabled ~= nil then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bartEnabled = data.bartEnabled end
    if data.bossEnabled ~= nil then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bossEnabled = data.bossEnabled end
    if data.shadowEnabled ~= nil then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.shadowEnabled = data.shadowEnabled end
    if data.punkEnabled ~= nil then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.punkEnabled = data.punkEnabled end
    if data.emoEnabled ~= nil then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.emoEnabled = data.emoEnabled end
    if data.tpBatFloatingPos then
        _G.ViolenceTPBat.pos = data.tpBatFloatingPos
    end
    -- ESP + Ragdoll Timer: preserva escolha salva (false se explicitamente desligado)
    _G.ViolenceESP = _G.ViolenceESP or {}
    if data.playerESP ~= nil then
        _G.ViolenceESP.playerESP = data.playerESP and true or false
    else
        _G.ViolenceESP.playerESP = true
    end
    if data.tracerESP ~= nil then
        _G.ViolenceESP.tracerESP = data.tracerESP and true or false
    else
        _G.ViolenceESP.tracerESP = false
    end
    if data.highlightESP ~= nil then
        _G.ViolenceESP.highlightESP = data.highlightESP and true or false
    else
        _G.ViolenceESP.highlightESP = false
    end
    if data.autoCarry ~= nil and _G.ViolenceAutoCarry then
        _G.ViolenceAutoCarry.enabled = data.autoCarry and true or false
    end
    if data.ragdollTimer ~= nil then
        _G.ViolenceNino = _G.ViolenceNino or {}
        _G.ViolenceNino.enabled = data.ragdollTimer and true or false
    else
        _G.ViolenceNino = _G.ViolenceNino or {}
        _G.ViolenceNino.enabled = false
    end
    if data.meleeAimbot ~= nil then
        _G.ViolenceMelee = _G.ViolenceMelee or {}
        _G.ViolenceMelee.enabled = data.meleeAimbot and true or false
    else
        _G.ViolenceMelee = _G.ViolenceMelee or {}
        _G.ViolenceMelee.enabled = false
    end
    if data.dropMode then
        dropMode = data.dropMode
        if dropModeBtnRef then
            dropModeBtnRef.Text = dropMode == 1 and "V1" or "V2"
        end
    end
    if data.antiDrop ~= nil then
        _G.ViolenceAntiDrop.enabled = data.antiDrop and true or false
    end
    if data.stretchEnabled ~= nil then
        stretchEnabled = data.stretchEnabled
    end
    if data.stretchFOV then
        stretchFOV = data.stretchFOV
    end
    if data.selectedAnimPack and type(data.selectedAnimPack) == "string" and AnimationPacks[data.selectedAnimPack] then
        selectedAnimPack = data.selectedAnimPack
    end
    local function loadKey(kbData, target)
        if kbData and kbData.kb and Enum.KeyCode[kbData.kb] then
            target.kb = Enum.KeyCode[kbData.kb]
            target.gp = nil
        end
        if kbData and kbData.gp and Enum.KeyCode[kbData.gp] then
            target.gp = Enum.KeyCode[kbData.gp]
            target.kb = nil
        end
    end
    loadKey(data.dropBrainrotKey, KB.DropBrainrot)
    loadKey(data.autoLeftKey, KB.AutoLeft)
    loadKey(data.autoRightKey, KB.AutoRight)
    loadKey(data.autoBatKey, KB.AutoBat)
    loadKey(data.tpFloorKey, KB.TPFloor)
    loadKey(data.carryToggleKey, KB.CarryToggle)
    loadKey(data.laggerModeKey, KB.LaggerMode)
    loadKey(data.autoTPDownKey, KB.AutoTPDown)
    loadKey(data.instantResetKey, KB.InstantReset)
    if data.instantResetFloatingPos then _G.ViolenceReset = _G.ViolenceReset or {}; _G.ViolenceReset.floatPos = data.instantResetFloatingPos end
    if data.progressBarPos then savedProgressBarPos = data.progressBarPos end
    if data.mobilePanelPos then
        savedMobilePanelPos = data.mobilePanelPos
        -- descarta posições de botões fora da tela (bug do Align antigo)
        if type(savedMobilePanelPos) == "table" and type(savedMobilePanelPos.buttons) == "table" then
            local cam0 = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local vw0 = (cam0 and cam0.ViewportSize.X) or 800
            local vh0 = (cam0 and cam0.ViewportSize.Y) or 600
            local required = {DropBR=true, AutoLeft=true, AutoBat=true, AutoRight=true, TpDown=true, Carry=true, Lagger1=true, Lagger2=true}
            local cleaned = {}
            local okCount = 0
            for name, sp in pairs(savedMobilePanelPos.buttons) do
                if type(sp) == "table" and sp.XOffset ~= nil then
                    local x = (sp.XScale or 0) * vw0 + sp.XOffset
                    local y = (sp.YScale or 0) * vh0 + (sp.YOffset or 0)
                    if x >= -80 and y >= -80 and x <= vw0 - 8 and y <= vh0 - 8 then
                        cleaned[name] = sp
                        okCount = okCount + 1
                    end
                end
            end
            -- Se faltar Carry / Lagger2 / qualquer botão obrigatório → grade limpa
            local complete = true
            for req in pairs(required) do
                if not cleaned[req] then complete = false; break end
            end
            if complete and okCount >= 8 then
                savedMobilePanelPos.buttons = cleaned
            else
                savedMobilePanelPos.buttons = nil
            end
        end
    end
    refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    lastSavedJSON = HS:JSONEncode(buildConfigTable())
    _G.ViolenceSettingsLoaded = true
    _G.ViolenceSettingsLoadFailed = false
    return true
end

local function resetToDefaults()
    stopAllBackgroundTasks()
    _G.ViolenceSpeeds.setNS(60); NS=60
    _G.ViolenceSpeeds.setCS(30); CS=30
    _G.ViolenceSpeeds.setL1(15); LAGGER_SPEED_1=15
    _G.ViolenceSpeeds.setL2(10); LAGGER_SPEED_2=10
    Steal.StealRadius = 60.0
    Steal.StealDuration = 1.4
    autoTPHeight = 20
    autoTPDownThreshold = 20
    speedMode = false
    laggerToggled = false
    laggerLevel = 1
    antiRagdollEnabled = false
    jumpEnabled = false
    jumpMode = 1
    tpDownMode = 1
    medusaCounterEnabled = false
    batCounterEnabled = false
    autoBatEnabled = false
    autoLeftEnabled = false
    autoRightEnabled = false
    unwalkEnabled = false
    antiLagEnabled = false
    autoTPDownEnabled = false
    uiLocked = false
    Steal.AutoStealEnabled = false
    Steal.StealMode = "Normal"
    if stealModeBtnRef then stealModeBtnRef.Text = "Normal" end
    BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED = 58
    BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED = 60
    bypassToggled = false
    _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggled = false
    _G.ViolenceESP.playerESP = true
    dropMode = 1

    stretchEnabled = false
    stretchFOV = 120
    _G.ViolenceSky.theme = "Night"
    pcall(_G.applyCustomSky, "Night")
    if _G.ViolenceSky.btn then _G.ViolenceSky.btn.Text = "SKY: Night" end
    if _G.skyThemeBtn then _G.skyThemeBtn.Text = "SKY: Night" end
    if _G.ViolenceTPBat.setVisual then _G.ViolenceTPBat.setVisual(false) end
    if normalBox then normalBox.Text = tostring(NS) end
    if carryBox then carryBox.Text = tostring(CS) end
    if laggerBox then laggerBox.Text = tostring(LAGGER_SPEED_1) end
    if lagger2Box then lagger2Box.Text = tostring(LAGGER_SPEED_2) end
    if radInput then radInput.Text = tostring(Steal.StealRadius) end
    if stealDurationBox then stealDurationBox.Text = tostring(Steal.StealDuration) end
    if autoTPHeightBox then autoTPHeightBox.Text = tostring(autoTPHeight) end
    if batSpeedBox then batSpeedBox.Text = tostring(BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED) end
    if bypassSpeedBox then bypassSpeedBox.Text = tostring(BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED) end
    if progressRadLbl then progressRadLbl.Text = "-- Â· --" end
    if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end
    if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
    if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(false) end
    if setBatCounterVisual then setBatCounterVisual(false) end
    if setMedusaVisual then setMedusaVisual(false) end

    if setAntiRagVisual then setAntiRagVisual(false) end
    if setJumpVisual then setJumpVisual(false) end
    if setUnwalkVisual then setUnwalkVisual(false) end
    if setAntiLagVisual then setAntiLagVisual(false) end
    if setAutoTPDownVisual then setAutoTPDownVisual(false) end
    if setLockUIVisual then setLockUIVisual(false) end
    if setInstaGrab then setInstaGrab(false) end
    if bypassSetVisual then bypassSetVisual(false) end
    if _G.stretchToggleSetter then _G.stretchToggleSetter(false) end
    if mobSetAutoBat then mobSetAutoBat(false) end
    if mobSetAutoLeft then mobSetAutoLeft(false) end
    if mobSetAutoRight then mobSetAutoRight(false) end
    if mobSetDropBR then mobSetDropBR(false) end
    if mobSetTpDown then mobSetTpDown(false) end
    if mobSetCarry then mobSetCarry(false) end
    if mobSetLagger1 then mobSetLagger1(false) end
    if mobSetLagger2 then mobSetLagger2(false) end
    if modeSelectBtn then
        modeSelectBtn.Text = jumpMode == 1 and "Tap Tap" or "Hold"
    end
    if tpModeSelectBtn then
        tpModeSelectBtn.Text = tpDownMode == 1 and "V1" or "V2"
    end
    if dropModeBtnRef then
        dropModeBtnRef.Text = dropMode == 1 and "V1" or "V2"
    end
    if setJumpToggleState then setJumpToggleState(false) end
    refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    updateProgressBarVisibility()
    lastSavedJSON = HS:JSONEncode(buildConfigTable())
end

local function deleteAllSettings()
    local success = false
    if isfile and isfile(CONFIG_FILE) then
        success = pcall(function() delfile(CONFIG_FILE); return true end)
    end
    if isfile and isfile("ViolenceHub_PanelPos.txt") then
        pcall(delfile, "ViolenceHub_PanelPos.txt")
    end
    resetToDefaults()
    if pbFrame then
        pbFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -140, 1, -66)
    end
    if MobilePanel and MobilePanel:FindFirstChild("FloatingPanel") then
        local container = MobilePanel:FindFirstChild("FloatingPanel")
        container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50 - 10, 0, 0)
    end
    bypassFloatingPos = nil
    if bypassFloatingButton and bypassFloatingButton:FindFirstChild("Frame") then
        local btnFrame = bypassFloatingButton:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10 - 60, 0, 150 + 10)
    end
    return success
end

local gui = nil
local main = nil
local miniBtn = nil

local function applyShimmerToText(obj, speed)
    speed = speed or 0.8
    local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient", obj)
    grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160))
    })
    grad.Rotation = 45
    grad.Offset = Vector2.new(0,0)
    task.spawn(function()
        local t = 0
        while grad and grad.Parent do
            t = t + 0.02
            grad.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t * speed) * 0.4, 0)
            task.wait(0.04)
        end
    end)
    return grad
end

-- Presets Noxa (mesmas oito opções da interface de referência)
do
    local N = {
        CHROME={24,132,122,92,245,186,120,214,255},
        AURORA={92,66,214,150,130,255,110,230,255},
        EMBER={150,46,24,255,138,62,255,92,138},
        CRIMSON={126,16,40,255,76,96,255,150,110},
        GOLD={128,92,20,240,198,96,255,238,178},
        GLACIER={26,78,158,96,178,255,178,232,255},
        TOXIC={62,120,20,178,255,66,96,232,152},
        ORCHID={122,24,120,255,108,214,186,130,255},
    }
    for name,v in pairs(N) do
        local bg=Color3.fromRGB(v[1],v[2],v[3])
        local a=Color3.fromRGB(v[4],v[5],v[6])
        local b=Color3.fromRGB(v[7],v[8],v[9])
        _G.ViolenceBtnColor.presets["Noxa_"..name] = {
            inactive=bg:Lerp(a,0.18), active=a,
            text=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), stroke=b,
            accent=a:Lerp(bg,0.35), pillOff=bg:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0),0.12),
            pillOn=a, inp=bg:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0),0.35)
        }
    end
end
_G.ViolenceBtnColor.registry = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.registry or {}

_G.ViolenceBtnColor.apply = function(name)
	local S = _G.ViolenceBtnColor
	local preset = S.presets[name]
	if not preset then return end
	S.name = name
	S.inactive = preset.inactive
	S.active = preset.active
	S.text = preset.text
	S.stroke = preset.stroke
	S.accent = preset.accent
	S.pillOff = preset.pillOff
	S.pillOn = preset.pillOn
	S.inp = preset.inp
	for _, entry in ipairs(S.registry) do
		pcall(function()
			if entry.kind == "tab" and entry.btn and entry.btn.Parent then
				local isActive = entry.isActive and entry.isActive() or false
				entry.btn.BackgroundColor3 = isActive and S.active or S.inactive
				entry.btn.BackgroundTransparency = isActive and 0 or 0.05
				entry.btn.TextColor3 = S.text
				local st = entry.btn:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke")
				if st then
					st.Color = S.stroke
					st.Thickness = isActive and 2 or 1.5
					st.Transparency = isActive and 0.02 or 0.18
				end
			elseif entry.kind == "selector" and entry.btn and entry.btn.Parent then
				entry.btn.BackgroundColor3 = S.inp
				entry.btn.TextColor3 = S.text
				local st = entry.btn:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke")
				if st then st.Color = S.stroke end
			elseif entry.kind == "box" and entry.btn and entry.btn.Parent then
				entry.btn.BackgroundColor3 = S.inp
				entry.btn.TextColor3 = S.text
				local st = entry.btn:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke")
				if st then st.Color = S.stroke end
			elseif entry.kind == "pill" and entry.pill and entry.pill.Parent then
				local isOn = entry.getOn and entry.getOn() or false
				entry.pill.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and S.pillOn or S.pillOff
				if entry.dot and entry.dot.Parent then
					entry.dot.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255) or S.stroke
				end
				local pillStroke = entry.pill:FindFirstChild("ThemeStroke")
				if pillStroke then
					pillStroke.Color = S.stroke
					pillStroke.Thickness = isOn and 1.8 or 1.3
					pillStroke.Transparency = isOn and 0 or 0.05
				end
			elseif entry.kind == "keybtn" and entry.btn and entry.btn.Parent then
				entry.btn.BackgroundColor3 = S.inp
				entry.btn.TextColor3 = S.text
			elseif entry.kind == "action" and entry.btn and entry.btn.Parent then
				entry.btn.BackgroundColor3 = S.active
				entry.btn.TextColor3 = S.text
				local st = entry.btn:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke")
				if st then st.Color = S.stroke end
			elseif entry.kind == "mobile" and entry.btn and entry.btn.Parent then
				-- mobile buttons refresh via their setActive
			end
		end)
	end
	-- Atualiza botões flutuantes imediatamente (cor + texto branco)
	pcall(function()
		pcall(function() _G.ViolenceMobileImg.applyAll() end)
		local inactive = S.inactive
		local active = S.active
		local strokeC = S.stroke
		local WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		local function paintBtn(btn, isOn)
			if not btn or not btn.Parent then return end
			btn.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and active or inactive
			local baseZ = math.max(btn.ZIndex or 1, 1)
			local img = btn:FindFirstChild("MobileBackgroundImage")
			if img then img.ZIndex = baseZ + 1 end
			local lbl = btn:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
			if lbl then
				lbl.TextColor3 = WHITE
				lbl.TextTransparency = 0
				lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
				lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
				lbl.ZIndex = baseZ + 10
				lbl.Visible = true
			end
			local st = btn:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke")
			if st then
				st.Color = strokeC
				st.Thickness = isOn and 2 or 1.5
				st.Transparency = isOn and 0 or 0.35
			end
		end
		if type(_G.ViolenceMobileButtons) == "table" then
			for _, data in pairs(_G.ViolenceMobileButtons) do
				local isOn = data.active == true
				if type(data.setActive) == "function" then
					-- re-aplica estado atual (já força texto branco)
					pcall(data.setActive, isOn)
				else
					paintBtn(data.btn, isOn)
				end
			end
		end
		if MobilePanel then
			for _, btn in ipairs(MobilePanel:GetDescendants()) do
				if btn:IsA("TextButton") and btn.Name ~= "" then
					local st = btn:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke")
					local isOn = st and st.Thickness and st.Thickness >= 1.9
					paintBtn(btn, isOn)
				end
			end
		end
		if bypassFloatingButton then
			local fr = bypassFloatingButton:FindFirstChild("Frame")
			paintBtn(fr, bypassToggled)
		end
		if _G.ViolenceTPBat and _G.ViolenceTPBat.btn then
			local fr = _G.ViolenceTPBat.btn:FindFirstChild("Frame")
			paintBtn(fr, _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggled)
		end
		if _G.ViolenceReset and _G.ViolenceReset.floatBtn then
			local fr = _G.ViolenceReset.floatBtn:FindFirstChild("Frame")
			paintBtn(fr, false)
		end
	end)
	-- Sidebar + ID / nome + título VIOLENCE seguem a cor escolhida (instantâneo)
	pcall(function()
		local R = _G.ViolenceUIRefs
		if not R then return end
		if R.avatarStroke then R.avatarStroke.Color = S.stroke end
		if R.nameLbl then R.nameLbl.TextColor3 = S.text end
		if R.displayLbl then R.displayLbl.TextColor3 = S.text end
		if R.mainStroke then R.mainStroke.Color = S.stroke end
		if R.brandLetters then
			for _, L in ipairs(R.brandLetters) do
				if L.obj and L.obj.Parent then
					L.obj.TextColor3 = S.active
				end
			end
		elseif R.brandTitle then
			if R.brandTitle:IsA("TextLabel") then
				R.brandTitle.TextColor3 = S.active
			else
				for _, ch in ipairs(R.brandTitle:GetDescendants()) do
					if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then
						ch.TextColor3 = S.active
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if R.bigTitle then
			R.bigTitle.TextColor3 = S.active
			local g = R.bigTitle:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
			if g then
				g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, S.inactive),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, S.active),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, S.stroke)
				})
			end
		end
		if R.mainBgOverlay then
			R.mainBgOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			R.mainBgOverlay.Visible = false
		end
		if R.mainBg then
			R.mainBg.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			R.mainBg.ImageTransparency = 0
		end
		if R.colorSwatches then
			for name, sw in pairs(R.colorSwatches) do
				if sw and sw.Parent then
					local sel = (name == S.name)
					local st = sw:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke")
					if st then
						st.Thickness = sel and 2.5 or 1
						st.Transparency = sel and 0 or 0.4
						st.Color = sel and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255) or Color3.fromRGB(80,80,80)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	if S.onChanged then pcall(S.onChanged) end
	-- ESP tracer / highlight seguem a cor do tema
	pcall(function()
		local themeCol = S.active or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		local E = _G.ViolenceESP
		if not E or not E.entries then return end
		for _, entry in pairs(E.entries) do
			if entry.highlight and entry.highlight.Parent then
				entry.highlight.FillColor = themeCol
				entry.highlight.OutlineColor = themeCol
			end
			if entry.line then
				pcall(function() entry.line.Color = themeCol end)
			end
			if entry.lineFrame and entry.lineFrame.Parent then
				entry.lineFrame.BackgroundColor3 = themeCol
			end
		end
	end)
end


local function buildGui()
    _G.ViolenceBtnColor.registry = {}
    local BLACK   = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    local WHITE   = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    local ACCENT  = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.accent
    local INP     = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inp
    local CORNER  = 14 -- retangular vertical
    local BTN_OFF = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
    local BTN_ON  = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active
    local BTN_TXT = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
    -- registry local helper
    local function regBtn(kind, btn, extra)
        local e = {kind = kind, btn = btn}
        if extra then for k,v in pairs(extra) do e[k] = v end end
        table.insert(_G.ViolenceBtnColor.registry, e)
        return e
    end
    local GUI_W, GUI_H = 375, 495 -- retangular vertical
    local SIDEBAR_W = 165
    local CONTENT_OVERLAP = 8

    local old=game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("ViolenceHub");if old then old:Destroy() end
    local pg=LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui");if pg then local o=pg:FindFirstChild("ViolenceHub");if o then o:Destroy() end end
    gui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name="ViolenceHub";gui.ResetOnSpawn=false;gui.DisplayOrder=10;gui.IgnoreGuiInset=true
    pcall(function() if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(gui) end end)
    if not pcall(function() gui.Parent=game:GetService("CoreGui") end) then gui.Parent=LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end

    main=Instance.new("Frame",gui)
    main.Size=UDim2.new(0,GUI_W,0,GUI_H)
    main.Position=UDim2.new(0,16,0,8) -- canto superior esquerdo, no topo
    main.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    main.BackgroundTransparency = 1 -- sem preto por baixo da foto
    main.BorderSizePixel = 0
    main.ClipsDescendants = true
    main.ZIndex = 10

    -- UIScale: escala proporcional (textos/nomes nao sao cortados ao diminuir)
    local mainUIScale = Instance.new("UIScale", main)
    _G.ViolenceUIRefs = _G.ViolenceUIRefs or {}
    local _savedUiScale = tonumber(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.uiScale) or 100
    mainUIScale.Scale = _savedUiScale / 100
    _G.ViolenceUIRefs.mainUIScale = mainUIScale
    _G.ViolenceUIRefs.uiScale = _savedUiScale
    
    -- CORNER PRINCIPAL COM CLIPSDESCENDANTS = TRUE (IMPORTANTE!)
    main.ClipsDescendants = true
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner",main)
    corner.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,CORNER)

    -- Background image (CanvasGroup recorta cantos arredondados — evita pontas da foto)
    local bgMask = Instance.new("CanvasGroup", main)
    bgMask.Name = "BgMask"
    bgMask.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    bgMask.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    bgMask.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    bgMask.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bgMask.ZIndex = 0
    bgMask.ClipsDescendants = true
    -- cantos um pouco maiores que o main pra mascarar bem
    Instance.new("UICorner", bgMask).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, CORNER + 1)

    local mainBg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", bgMask)
    mainBg.Name = "Background"
    -- Stretch: preenche 100% sem faixa vazia e sem mudar regiao da foto
    mainBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    mainBg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    mainBg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    mainBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    mainBg.Image = "rbxassetid://89309118455059"
    mainBg.ImageTransparency = 0
    mainBg.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    mainBg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch
    mainBg.ZIndex = 0
    Instance.new("UICorner", mainBg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, CORNER)

    -- Overlay desativado (sem escurecer a foto)
    local mainBgOverlay = Instance.new("Frame", bgMask)
    mainBgOverlay.Name = "BgOverlay"
    mainBgOverlay.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    mainBgOverlay.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    mainBgOverlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    mainBgOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    mainBgOverlay.Visible = false
    mainBgOverlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
    mainBgOverlay.ZIndex = 1

    local mainStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",main)
    mainStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    mainStroke.Thickness=1.5
    mainStroke.Transparency=0.25
    mainStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

    -- sombra sutil atrás (arredondada, sem pontas quadradas)
    local shadow = Instance.new("Frame", main)
    shadow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    shadow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2)
    shadow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88
    shadow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shadow.ZIndex = 0
    Instance.new("UICorner", shadow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, CORNER)

    -- Sidebar
    local sidebar = Instance.new("Frame", main)
    sidebar.Size = UDim2.new(0, SIDEBAR_W, 1, 0)
    sidebar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    sidebar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    sidebar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    sidebar.ClipsDescendants = true

    -- Canvas do sidebar (CanvasGroup mascara a imagem nos cantos)
    local sidebarCanvas = Instance.new("CanvasGroup", sidebar)
    sidebarCanvas.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    sidebarCanvas.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    sidebarCanvas.BackgroundColor3 = BLACK
    sidebarCanvas.BackgroundTransparency = 1 -- sem fundo escuro por baixo da foto
    sidebarCanvas.BorderSizePixel = 0
    sidebarCanvas.ClipsDescendants = true
    local canvasCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", sidebarCanvas)
    -- só arredonda o canto externo esquerdo (evita ponta saindo)
    canvasCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, CORNER)

    -- Sem foto propria na sidebar: usa so o mainBg (uma unica interface continua)
    local sidebarImg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", sidebarCanvas)
    sidebarImg.Name = "SidebarImgDisabled"
    sidebarImg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    sidebarImg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    sidebarImg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    sidebarImg.Image = ""
    sidebarImg.ImageTransparency = 1
    sidebarImg.Visible = false
    sidebarImg.BorderSizePixel = 0

    -- Scrim da sidebar desativado (sem overlay escuro)
    local sidebarScrim = Instance.new("Frame", sidebarCanvas)
    sidebarScrim.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    sidebarScrim.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    sidebarScrim.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    sidebarScrim.Visible = false
    sidebarScrim.BorderSizePixel = 0

    -- Branding (topo) + Avatar circle
    local brandFrame = Instance.new("Frame", sidebar)
    brandFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 145)
    brandFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 6)
    brandFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    brandFrame.ClipsDescendants = false

    -- VIOLENCE letras premium (sombra + letra + brilho suave)
    local brandTitle = Instance.new("Frame", brandFrame)
    brandTitle.Name = "ViolenceTitleLetters"
    brandTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)
    brandTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    brandTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    brandTitle.ClipsDescendants = false

    local letterHolder = Instance.new("Frame", brandTitle)
    letterHolder.Name = "Letters"
    letterHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    letterHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
    letterHolder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    letterHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    letterHolder.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X

    local letterLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", letterHolder)
    letterLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    letterLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
    letterLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    letterLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, -1)
    letterLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    local brandLetters = {}
    local WORD = "VIOLENCE"
    for i = 1, #WORD do
        local ch = WORD:sub(i, i)
        local slot = Instance.new("Frame", letterHolder)
        slot.Name = "Slot" .. i
        slot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 15, 1, 0)
        slot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        slot.ClipsDescendants = false
        slot.LayoutOrder = i

        local shadow = Instance.new("TextLabel", slot)
        shadow.Name = "S" .. i
        shadow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        shadow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 2)
        shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        shadow.Text = ch
        shadow.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        shadow.TextTransparency = 0.45
        shadow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        shadow.TextSize = 18
        shadow.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
        shadow.ZIndex = 1

        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", slot)
        lbl.Name = "L" .. i
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = ch
        lbl.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        lbl.TextSize = 18
        lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.25
        lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
        lbl.ZIndex = 2

        table.insert(brandLetters, {
            obj = lbl,
            shadow = shadow,
            phase = (i - 1) * 0.4,
            baseY = 0,
        })
    end
    _G.ViolenceUIRefs.brandLetters = brandLetters

    -- Bolinha preta com avatar (CanvasGroup = recorte circular real, sem pontas)
    local avatarContainer = Instance.new("CanvasGroup", brandFrame)
    avatarContainer.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 38)
    avatarContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -19, 0, 32)
    avatarContainer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    avatarContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    avatarContainer.ClipsDescendants = true
    Instance.new("UICorner", avatarContainer).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local avatarStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", avatarContainer)
    avatarStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    avatarStroke.Thickness = 1.2
    avatarStroke.Transparency = 0.25

    local avatarImg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", avatarContainer)
    avatarImg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 2, 1, 2)
    avatarImg.Position = UDim2.new(0, -1, 0, -1)
    avatarImg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    avatarImg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    avatarImg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop

    local function updateAvatarPreview()
        local userId = LP.UserId
        if userId and userId > 0 then
            -- tamanho menor evita artefatos/pontos
            avatarImg.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId=" .. tostring(userId) .. "&width=60&height=60&format=png"
        end
    end
    updateAvatarPreview()
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.3)
        updateAvatarPreview()
    end)

    local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", brandFrame)
    nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
    nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 74)

    miniClick.AutoButtonColor = false

    -- Drag da barra mini: so quando UI unlock (uiLocked == false)
    do
        local dragging, hasMoved, dragStart, startPos = false, false, nil, nil
        local dragThreshold = 6
        local function isPress(input)
            return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        end
        local function onBegan(input)
            if not isPress(input) then return end
            if uiLocked then return end
            dragging = true
            hasMoved = false
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = miniBtn.Position
        end
        local function onChanged(input)
            if not dragging then return end
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            if uiLocked then
                dragging = false
                return
            end
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            if math.abs(delta.X) > dragThreshold or math.abs(delta.Y) > dragThreshold then
                hasMoved = true
            end
            if hasMoved then
                miniBtn.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                )
            end
        end
        local function onEnded(input)
            if not isPress(input) then return end
            if not dragging then return end
            if hasMoved and not uiLocked then
                savedMiniBtnPos = {
                    XScale = miniBtn.Position.X.Scale,
                    XOffset = miniBtn.Position.X.Offset,
                    YScale = miniBtn.Position.Y.Scale,
                    YOffset = miniBtn.Position.Y.Offset,
                }
                pcall(saveAllSettings)
                -- evita abrir a UI no soltar apos arrastar
                miniBtn:SetAttribute("ViolenceJustDragged", true)
                task.delay(0.15, function()
                    if miniBtn then miniBtn:SetAttribute("ViolenceJustDragged", false) end
                end)
            end
            dragging = false
            hasMoved = false
            dragStart = nil
            startPos = nil
        end
        miniClick.InputBegan:Connect(onBegan)
        miniClick.InputChanged:Connect(onChanged)
        miniClick.InputEnded:Connect(onEnded)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(onChanged)
        UIS.InputEnded:Connect(onEnded)
    end

    -- clicks conectados depois de showGui (abaixo)
    _G.ViolenceUIRefs = _G.ViolenceUIRefs or {}
    _G.ViolenceUIRefs.miniOpenPill = openPill
    _G.ViolenceUIRefs.miniClick = miniClick

    -- Sem animação de vai-e-vem no nome fechado (miniShine fica parado/oculto)

    local function showGui()
        if main.Visible and not miniBtn.Visible then return end
        miniBtn.Visible = false
        main.Visible = true
        main.ClipsDescendants = true
        local targetSize = main.Size
        local baseScale = (_G.ViolenceUIRefs and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.uiScale or 100) / 100
        main.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(8, math.floor(targetSize.X.Offset * 0.88)), 0, math.max(8, math.floor(targetSize.Y.Offset * 0.88)))
        main.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        local sc = _G.ViolenceUIRefs and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.mainUIScale
        if sc then sc.Scale = baseScale * 0.92 end
        TS:Create(main, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Size = targetSize,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, -- foto limpa, sem preto
        }):Play()
        if sc then
            TS:Create(sc, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                Scale = baseScale
            }):Play()
        end
        pcall(function()
            if setActivePage and mainPage then setActivePage(mainPage) end
        end)
    end

    local function hideGui()
        if not main.Visible then return end
        local startSize = main.Size
        local sc = _G.ViolenceUIRefs and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.mainUIScale
        local baseScale = (_G.ViolenceUIRefs and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.uiScale or 100) / 100
        TS:Create(main, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(8, math.floor(startSize.X.Offset * 0.9)), 0, math.max(8, math.floor(startSize.Y.Offset * 0.9))),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        }):Play()
        if sc then
            TS:Create(sc, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
                Scale = baseScale * 0.9
            }):Play()
        end
        task.delay(0.18, function()
            if main and main.Parent then
                main.Visible = false
                main.Size = startSize
                main.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                if sc then sc.Scale = baseScale end
            end
            if miniBtn and miniBtn.Parent then
                miniBtn.Visible = true
            end
        end)
    end

    local function tryShowGuiFromMini()

    end

        p.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y

        ll.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        ll.Padding = UDim.new(0, 7)

        pd.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
        pd.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
        pd.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 28)
        pd.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 30)
        return p
    end

    local activePage = mainPage
    local function makeTopTab(label, idx, page)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton", tabContainer)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
        b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Text = label:sub(1,1) .. label:sub(2):lower()
        b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        b.TextSize = 12
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.LayoutOrder = idx
        Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", b)
        s.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
        s.Thickness = 1.5
        s.Transparency = 0.08
        applyShimmerToText(b, 0.7)
        regBtn("tab", b, {isActive = function() return page.Visible end})
        return b
    end

    local btnMain   = makeTopTab("MAIN",   1, mainPage)
    local btnOther  = makeTopTab("OTHER",  2, otherPage)
    local btnConfig = makeTopTab("CONFIG", 3, configPage)
    local btnKeybinds = makeTopTab("KEYBINDS", 4, keybindsPage)
    local btnMusic  = makeTopTab("MUSIC",  5, musicPage)
    local allTabs = {
        {btn=btnMain,   page=mainPage},
        {btn=btnOther,  page=otherPage},
        {btn=btnConfig, page=configPage},
        {btn=btnKeybinds, page=keybindsPage},
        {btn=btnMusic,  page=musicPage},
    }

    local function setActivePage(p)
        activePage = p
        for _, t in ipairs(allTabs) do
            t.page.Visible = (t.page == p)
            local isActive = (t.page == p)
            local S = _G.ViolenceBtnColor
            TS:Create(t.btn, TweenInfo.new(0.22), {
                BackgroundColor3 = isActive and S.active or S.inactive,
                BackgroundTransparency = isActive and 0 or 0.05,
                TextColor3 = S.text,
            }):Play()
            local st = t.btn:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke")
            if st then
                TS:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.22), {
                    Color = S.stroke,
                    Thickness = isActive and 2 or 1.5,
                    Transparency = isActive and 0.02 or 0.18,
                }):Play()
            end
        end
    end

    btnMain.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active
    btnMain.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    btnMain.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
    local stMain = btnMain:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke")
    if stMain then stMain.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke; stMain.Transparency = 0.2 end

    btnMain.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setActivePage(mainPage) end)
    btnOther.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setActivePage(otherPage) end)
    btnConfig.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setActivePage(configPage) end)
    btnKeybinds.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setActivePage(keybindsPage) end)
    btnMusic.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setActivePage(musicPage) end)

    local function mkSect(txt)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", activePage)
        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
        f.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", f)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0)
        l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = txt:upper()
        l.TextColor3 = WHITE
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        l.TextSize = 11
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        f.LayoutOrder = #activePage:GetChildren() + 1
        applyShimmerToText(l, 0.5)
        local line = Instance.new("Frame", f)
        line.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1)
        line.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -2)
        line.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160)
        line.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
        line.BorderSizePixel = 0
        return f
    end

    local function mkRow(h)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", activePage)
        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, h or 40)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = BLACK
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.LayoutOrder = #activePage:GetChildren() + 1
        Instance.new("UICorner", f).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
        local rowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", f)
        rowStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
        rowStroke.Thickness = 1
        rowStroke.Transparency = 0.7
        local rowGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", f)
        rowGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 12)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
        })
        rowGrad.Rotation = 90
        rowGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(0,0)
        task.spawn(function()
            local t=0
            while rowGrad and rowGrad.Parent do
                t = t + 0.02
                rowGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t * 0.2) * 0.1, 0)
                task.wait(0.04)
            end
        end)
        f.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(f, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)}):Play()
        end)
        f.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(f, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundColor3 = BLACK}):Play()
        end)
        return f
    end

        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        l.TextSize = 11
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        applyShimmerToText(l, 0.6)
        return l
    end

    local function mkPill(row, offset)
        local pill = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 24)
        pill.Position = UDim2.new(1, -(offset or 56), 0.5, -12)
        pill.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.pillOff
        pill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", pill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local pillStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", pill)
        pillStroke.Name = "ThemeStroke"
        pillStroke.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
        pillStroke.Thickness = 1.3
        pillStroke.Transparency = 0.05
        local dot = Instance.new("Frame", pill)
        dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
        dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -9)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160)
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        return pill, dot
    end

        local function animPill(pill, dot, on)
        local S = _G.ViolenceBtnColor
        TS:Create(pill,TweenInfo.new(0.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad),{BackgroundColor3=on and S.pillOn or S.pillOff}):Play()
        TS:Create(dot,TweenInfo.new(0.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Back),{
            Position=on and UDim2.new(1,-21,0.5,-9) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-9),
            BackgroundColor3=on and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255) or S.stroke
        }):Play()
    end

    local function mkToggle(txt, cb)
        local row = mkRow(40)
        mkLabel(row, txt)
        local pill, dot = mkPill(row, 56)
        local on = false
        local function sv(s) on=s; animPill(pill,dot,s) end
        local clk = Instance.new("TextButton", pill)
        clk.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
        clk.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        clk.Text = ""
        clk.Activated:Connect(function()
            on = not on
            sv(on)
            pcall(cb, on)
        end)
        regBtn("pill", nil, {pill = pill, dot = dot, getOn = function() return on end})
        return sv
    end

    local function mkBox(parent, default, w, xOff, cb)
        local tb = Instance.new("TextBox", parent)
        local bw = w or 50
        local xo = math.max(xOff or 56, bw + 8)
        tb.Size = UDim2.new(0, bw, 0, 26)
        tb.Position = UDim2.new(1, -xo, 0.5, -13)
        tb.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inp
        tb.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tb.Text = tostring(default)
        tb.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
        tb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        tb.TextSize = 11
        tb.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        Instance.new("UICorner", tb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local bs = Instance.new("UIStroke", tb)
        bs.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
        bs.Thickness = 1
        bs.Transparency = 0.28
        tb.Focused:Connect(function() TS:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.12),{Color=WHITE,Transparency=0}):Play() end)
        tb.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.12),{Color=_G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke,Transparency=0.28}):Play()
            if cb then
                local n = tonumber((tb.Text or ""):gsub(",", "."):match("[%d%.]+"))
                if n then
                    cb(n)
                    -- mantem o valor que o usuario digitou (apos clamp no cb se houver)
                else
                    tb.Text = tostring(default)
                end
            end
        end)
        -- mobile: aplica tambem ao mudar texto e sair
        tb:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            -- nao aplica a cada tecla; so limpa caracteres invalidos leves
        end)
        applyShimmerToText(tb, 0.7)
        regBtn("box", tb)
        return tb
    end

    local function mkSelector(parent, default, cb)
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", parent)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 26)
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0.5, -13)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inp
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Text = default
        btn.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        btn.TextSize = 12
        btn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
        stroke.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Transparency = 0.35
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if _anyKeyListening then return end
            if cb then cb(btn) end
        end)
        applyShimmerToText(btn, 0.7)
        regBtn("selector", btn)
        return btn
    end

    mkSect("Speed")
    local function mkSpeedBox(parent, key, default, setter)
        local tb = Instance.new("TextBox", parent)
        tb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 26)
        tb.Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0.5, -13)
        tb.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inp
        tb.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tb.Text = tostring(tonumber((_G.ViolenceSpeeds or {})[key]) or default)
        tb.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
        tb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        tb.TextSize = 11
        tb.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        tb.TextEditable = true
        Instance.new("UICorner", tb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local bs = Instance.new("UIStroke", tb)
        bs.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
        bs.Thickness = 1
        bs.Transparency = 0.28
        local function applyNow()
            local raw = tostring(tb.Text or ""):gsub(",", "."):match("[%d%.]+")
            local n = tonumber(raw)
            if not n then
                tb.Text = tostring(tonumber((_G.ViolenceSpeeds or {})[key]) or default)
                return
            end
            n = math.clamp(n, 1, 500)
            if setter(n) then
                tb.Text = tostring(n)
                if key == "NS" then NS = n
                elseif key == "CS" then CS = n
                elseif key == "L1" then LAGGER_SPEED_1 = n
                elseif key == "L2" then LAGGER_SPEED_2 = n end
                pcall(saveAllSettings)
            end
        end
        tb.Focused:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(bs, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {Color = WHITE, Transparency = 0}):Play()
        end)
        tb.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(bs, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke, Transparency = 0.28}):Play()
            applyNow()
        end)
        -- aplica ao pressionar Enter (alguns executors)
        pcall(function()
            tb.ReturnPressed:Connect(applyNow)
        end)
        regBtn("box", tb)
        return tb
    end
    do local row=mkRow(40); mkLabel(row,"Normal Speed"); normalBox=mkSpeedBox(row,"NS",45,_G.ViolenceSpeeds.setNS) end

    -- ===================== CUSTOMIZE (NOXA STYLE) =====================
    -- Background Photos + Themes + Button Photos ficam somente dentro desta UI.
    do
        local BG_OPTIONS = {
            {id = "79603881558275", name = "Crimson"},
            {id = "121340786054640", name = "Smoke"},
            {id = "95250203542818", name = "Bloom"},
            {id = "114014353836347", name = "Mist"},
            {id = "110515698997608", name = "Azure"},
            {id = "124658598453278", name = "Crystal"},
            {id = "90631990302263", name = "Adapt 1"},
            {id = "109619268613730", name = "Adapt 2"},
            {id = "88369503310562", name = "Adapt 3"},
            {id = "80708025126373", name = "Adapt 4"},
            {id = "102253425322931", name = "Adapt 5"},
            {id = "90453834580322", name = "Adapt 6"},
            {id = "135181794444219", name = "Adapt 7"},
            -- Fotos do Void.VS
            {id = "118963313877514", name = "Void A"},
            {url = "https://files.catbox.moe/pk5a3v.jpeg", file = "verzed_bg_void1.jpeg", name = "Void 1"},
            {url = "https://files.catbox.moe/v8y0ak.png", file = "verzed_bg_void2.png", name = "Void 2"},
            {url = "https://files.catbox.moe/sl73hb.png", file = "verzed_bg_void3.png", name = "Void 3"},
            {url = "https://files.catbox.moe/ptt187.png", file = "verzed_bg_void4.png", name = "Void 4"},
        }
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bgOptions = BG_OPTIONS
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgIndex = _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgIndex or 1

        local function resolveMainBg()
            local R = _G.ViolenceUIRefs
            if R and R.mainBg and R.mainBg.Parent then return R.mainBg end
            if R and R.main then
                local mask = R.main:FindFirstChild("BgMask")
                local found = mask and mask:FindFirstChild("Background")
                if not found then found = R.main:FindFirstChild("Background", true) end
                if found and found:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                    R.mainBg = found
                    return found
                end
            end
            return R and R.mainBg or nil
        end

        local function applyBackground(idx)
            idx = tonumber(idx) or 1
            idx = math.clamp(math.floor(idx), 1, #BG_OPTIONS)
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgIndex = idx
            local opt = BG_OPTIONS[idx]
            if not opt then return end
            local asset = ""
            if opt.id then
                asset = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(opt.id)
            elseif opt.url then
                -- baixa foto do Void (catbox) como no script original
                pcall(function()
                    local file = opt.file or ("verzed_bg_" .. tostring(idx) .. ".png")
                    if isfile and isfile(file) and getcustomasset then
                        asset = getcustomasset(file)
                    end
                    if (not asset or asset == "") and opt.url then
                        local data = nil
                        pcall(function()
                            if game.HttpGet then data = game:HttpGet(opt.url) end
                        end)
                        if (not data or #data < 100) and (http and http.request or request or (syn and syn.request)) then
                            local req = (http and http.request) or request or (syn and syn.request)
                            local r = req({Url = opt.url, Method = "GET"})
                            if r and r.Body and #r.Body > 100 then data = r.Body end
                        end
                        if data and type(data) == "string" and #data > 100 and writefile then
                            pcall(function() writefile(file, data) end)
                            if getcustomasset then
                                asset = getcustomasset(file) or ""
                            end
                        end
                        if not asset or asset == "" then
                            asset = opt.url -- fallback
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not asset or asset == "" then return end
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgAsset = asset
            local bg = resolveMainBg()
            if bg then
                bg.Image = asset
                bg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch
                bg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                bg.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
                bg.Visible = true
                bg.ZIndex = 0
                bg.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
                bg.ImageTransparency = 0
            end
            local R = _G.ViolenceUIRefs
            local customizeModal = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("ViolenceCustomizeModal")
            local customizeBg = customizeModal and customizeModal:FindFirstChild("CustomizeBackground")
            if customizeBg then
                customizeBg.Image = asset
                customizeBg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch
                customizeBg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
                customizeBg.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)
                customizeBg.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
                customizeBg.ImageTransparency = 0.28
                customizeBg.Visible = true
            end
            if R and R.mainBgOverlay then
                R.mainBgOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                R.mainBgOverlay.Visible = false
            end
            local side = R and R.sidebarImg
            if (not side or not side.Parent) and R and R.sidebarCanvas then
                side = R.sidebarCanvas:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ImageLabel")
                if side then R.sidebarImg = side end
            end
            if side then
                side.Image = asset
                side.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch
                side.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
                side.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)
                side.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
                side.ImageTransparency = 0
            end
            -- Barra de steal usa a mesma foto equipada na interface
            local stealBg = R and R.stealBarBg
            if stealBg and stealBg.Parent then
                stealBg.Image = asset
                stealBg.Visible = asset ~= ""
            end
            if pbFrame then
                -- com foto: fundo transparente; sem foto: cor solida
                pbFrame.BackgroundTransparency = (asset ~= "") and 1 or 0.12
            end
            if _G.ViolenceUIRefs.customizeBgThumbs then
                for i, th in ipairs(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.customizeBgThumbs) do
                    if th and th.Parent then
                        local st = th:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                        if st then
                            st.Color = (i == idx) and Color3.fromRGB(255,200,40) or Color3.fromRGB(60,60,60)
                            st.Thickness = (i == idx) and 2.2 or 1.2
                            st.Transparency = (i == idx) and 0 or 0.35
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
        end
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.applyBackground = applyBackground

        -- Botão único no menu principal: CUSTOMIZE
        mkSect("Personalização")
        local openCustomize = mkRow(48)
        openCustomize.Name = "CustomizeButton"
        openCustomize.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18,18,18)
        openCustomize.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
        openCustomize.ClipsDescendants = true
        local openCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", openCustomize)
        openCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
        local openStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", openCustomize)
        openStroke.Color = (_G.ViolenceBtnColor and _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke) or Color3.fromRGB(100,100,110)
        openStroke.Thickness = 1.4
        openStroke.Transparency = 0.25
        local openLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", openCustomize)

        local function makeImageThumb(parent, id, size, radius)
            local b = Instance.new("ImageButton", parent)
            b.ZIndex = 112
            b.Size = size
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10,10,10)
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.Image = id and ("rbxassetid://"..tostring(id)) or ""
            b.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
            Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 12)
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", b)
            st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(65,65,70)
            st.Thickness = 1.4
            st.Transparency = 0.25
            return b, st
        end

        local function buildCustomize()
            local old = gui:FindFirstChild("ViolenceCustomizeModal")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            local modal = Instance.new("Frame", gui)
            modal.Name = "ViolenceCustomizeModal"
            modal.Size = UDim2.new(0, 340, 0, 400)
            modal.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,-170,0.5,-200)
            modal.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(4,4,6)
            modal.BorderSizePixel = 0
            modal.ZIndex = 100
            modal.ClipsDescendants = true
            Instance.new("UICorner", modal).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,22)
            local modalBg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", modal)
            modalBg.Name = "CustomizeBackground"
            modalBg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
            modalBg.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)
            modalBg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            modalBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
            do
                local _opt = BG_OPTIONS[_G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgIndex or 1] or BG_OPTIONS[1]
                if _opt and _opt.id then
                    modalBg.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(_opt.id)
                elseif _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgAsset and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgAsset ~= "" then
                    modalBg.Image = _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgAsset
                else
                    modalBg.Image = ""
                end
            end
            modalBg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch
            modalBg.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            modalBg.ImageTransparency = 0.28
            modalBg.ZIndex = 100
            modalBg.Active = false
            modalBg.Selectable = false
            Instance.new("UICorner", modalBg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,22)
            local ms = Instance.new("UIStroke", modal)
            ms.Color = (_G.ViolenceBtnColor and _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke) or Color3.fromRGB(70,70,80)
            ms.Thickness = 1.5
            ms.Transparency = 0.15

            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", modal)

            sub.ZIndex = 110

            close.BorderSizePixel = 0

                l.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,24)
                l.BackgroundTransparency = 1

            end

            -- BACKGROUND PHOTOS

            bgSection.LayoutOrder = 1

            bgFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,92)

            bgFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
            bgFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0

            local bgScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", bgFrame)
            bgScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,1,-16)
            bgScroll.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8,8)
            bgScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            bgScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
            bgScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 0
            bgScroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X
            bgScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
            bgScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)

            bgLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            bgLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,8)
            bgLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
            local bgPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", bgScroll)
            bgPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,2); bgPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,2)

                local thumbId = opt.id
                local b,st = makeImageThumb(bgScroll,thumbId,UDim2.fromOffset(70,70),12)
                b.LayoutOrder = i
                if not thumbId and opt.url then
                    -- carrega preview async das fotos Void
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local file = opt.file or ("verzed_bg_" .. tostring(i) .. ".png")
                        local asset = ""
                        pcall(function()
                            if isfile and isfile(file) and getcustomasset then
                                asset = getcustomasset(file)
                            end
                            if (not asset or asset == "") then
                                local data = nil
                                pcall(function() if game.HttpGet then data = game:HttpGet(opt.url) end end)
                                if data and type(data) == "string" and #data > 100 and writefile then
                                    pcall(function() writefile(file, data) end)
                                    if getcustomasset then asset = getcustomasset(file) or "" end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        if asset and asset ~= "" and b and b.Parent then
                            b.Image = asset
                        end
                    end)
                end
                table.insert(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.customizeBgThumbs,b)
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() applyBackground(i) end)
            end
            applyBackground(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgIndex or 1)

            -- BUTTON PHOTOS
            local buttonSection = section("Button Photos")
            buttonSection.LayoutOrder = 3
            local ids = _G.ViolenceButtonImageIDs or {}
            local btnFrame = Instance.new("Frame", scroll)
            btnFrame.ZIndex = 111
            btnFrame.LayoutOrder = 4
            btnFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,82)
            btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10,10,12)
            btnFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
