            local btnScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", btnFrame)
            btnScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,1,-16)
            btnScroll.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8,8)
            btnScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            btnScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
            btnScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 0
            btnScroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X
            btnScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
            btnScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
            local bl = Instance.new("UIListLayout", btnScroll)
            bl.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            bl.Padding = UDim.new(0,8)
            bl.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
            local bp = Instance.new("UIPadding", btnScroll)
            bp.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,2); bp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,2)
            local function selectedButtonIndex()
                local id = tostring(_G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage or ""):match("(%d+)")
                if not id then return 0 end
                for i,v in ipairs(ids) do if tostring(v) == id then return i end end
                return 0
            end
            local refs = {}
            local function chooseButton(index)
                if index == 0 then _G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage = ""
                else _G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage = "rbxassetid://"..tostring(ids[index]) end
                pcall(function() _G.ViolenceMobileImg.applyAll() end)
                pcall(saveAllSettings)
                local sel = selectedButtonIndex()
                for i,b in ipairs(refs) do
                    local st = b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                    if st then st.Thickness = (i == sel) and 2.2 or 1.2; st.Transparency = (i == sel) and 0 or 0.3 end
                end
            end
            local none = makeImageThumb(btnScroll,nil,UDim2.fromOffset(62,62),11)
            local nl = Instance.new("TextLabel", none)
            nl.ZIndex = 113
            nl.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1); nl.BackgroundTransparency = 1; nl.Text = "NONE"; nl.TextColor3 = WHITE; nl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack; nl.TextSize = 10
            table.insert(refs,none)
            none.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() chooseButton(0) end)
            for i,id in ipairs(ids) do
                local b = makeImageThumb(btnScroll,id,UDim2.fromOffset(62,62),11)
                table.insert(refs,b)
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() chooseButton(i) end)
            end
            local sel = selectedButtonIndex()
            for i,b in ipairs(refs) do
                local st = b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if st then st.Thickness = (i == sel + 1) and 2.2 or 1.2; st.Transparency = (i == sel + 1) and 0 or 0.3 end
            end

            -- THEME VIOLENCE (LAST: after Background Photos and Button Photos)
            local themeSection = section("Theme Violence")
            themeSection.LayoutOrder = 5
            local themes = {"Roxo","Azul","Rosa","Branco","Laranja","Preto","Verde","Vermelho","Noxa_CHROME","Noxa_AURORA","Noxa_EMBER","Noxa_CRIMSON","Noxa_GOLD","Noxa_GLACIER","Noxa_TOXIC","Noxa_ORCHID"}
            local themeScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", scroll)
            themeScroll.ZIndex = 111
            themeScroll.LayoutOrder = 6
            themeScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,112)
            themeScroll.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10,10,12)
            themeScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
            themeScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
            themeScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 0
            themeScroll.ScrollingEnabled = true
            themeScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            themeScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
            themeScroll.ClipsDescendants = true
            Instance.new("UICorner", themeScroll).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,16)
            local tg = Instance.new("UIGridLayout", themeScroll)
            tg.CellSize = UDim2.new(0,72,0,42)
            tg.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0,6,0,6)
            tg.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
            tg.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
            local tp = Instance.new("UIPadding", themeScroll)
            tp.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0,7); tp.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0,7)
            for i,name in ipairs(themes) do
                local preset = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.presets[name]
                if preset then
                    local b = Instance.new("TextButton", themeScroll)
                    b.ZIndex = 112
                    b.BackgroundColor3 = preset.active
                    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    b.Text = name:gsub("Noxa_","")
                    b.TextColor3 = preset.text
                    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    b.TextSize = 9
                    b.AutoButtonColor = false
                    b.LayoutOrder = i
                    Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,14)
                    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", b)
                    st.Color = preset.stroke
                    st.Thickness = (_G.ViolenceBtnColor.name == name) and 2.2 or 1
                    st.Transparency = (_G.ViolenceBtnColor.name == name) and 0 or 0.3
                    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        _G.ViolenceBtnColor.apply(name)
                        pcall(saveAllSettings)
                        for _,child in ipairs(themeScroll:GetChildren()) do
                            if child:IsA("TextButton") then
                                local pn = child.Text == "CHROME" and "Noxa_CHROME" or child.Text
                                local pp = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.presets[pn] or _G.ViolenceBtnColor.presets[child.Text]
                                if pp then child.BackgroundColor3 = pp.active end
                                local ss = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                                if ss then
                                    local selected = (_G.ViolenceBtnColor.name == pn or _G.ViolenceBtnColor.name == child.Text)
                                    ss.Thickness = selected and 2.2 or 1
                                    ss.Transparency = selected and 0 or 0.3
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end


            -- Arrastar a janela pelo cabeçalho
            local dragging, dragStart, startPos
            title.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true; dragStart = input.Position; startPos = modal.Position
                end
            end)
            UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if not dragging then return end
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                    modal.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale,startPos.X.Offset+delta.X,startPos.Y.Scale,startPos.Y.Offset+delta.Y)
                end
            end)
            UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false end
            end)
            return modal
        end

        openClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(buildCustomize)
    end

        local function applyHeadlessToChar(char, enabled)
            if not char then return end
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if not head then return end
            if enabled then
                head.Transparency = 1
                head.CanCollide = false
                local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
                if face then pcall(function() face:Destroy() end) end
                local mesh = head:FindFirstChild("HeadlessMesh")
                if not mesh then
                    mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                    mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                    mesh.MeshId = HEADLESS_MESH_ID
                    mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(0.001, 0.001, 0.001)
                    mesh.Name = "HeadlessMesh"
                    mesh.Parent = head
                end
                if not head:GetAttribute("VH_HeadlessHooked") then
                    head:SetAttribute("VH_HeadlessHooked", true)
                    head:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
                        if head:GetAttribute("VH_HeadlessHooked") and head.Transparency ~= 1 then
                            head.Transparency = 1
                        end
                    end)
                    head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                        if head:GetAttribute("VH_HeadlessHooked") and child.Name == "face" and child:IsA("Decal") then
                            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            else
                head.Transparency = 0
                head.CanCollide = true
                head:SetAttribute("VH_HeadlessHooked", nil)
                for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "HeadlessMesh" then
                        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function applyKorbloxToChar(char, enabled)
            if not char then return end
            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not humanoid then return end
            if enabled then
                if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
                    local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
                    if rightLeg then
                        for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                            if child:IsA("CharacterMesh") then pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end
                        end
                        local mesh = rightLeg:FindFirstChild("KorbloxMesh")
                        if not mesh then
                            for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                                if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") then pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end
                            end
                            mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                            mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                            mesh.MeshId = KORBLOX_MESH_ID
                            mesh.TextureId = KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID
                            mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                            mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                            mesh.Parent = rightLeg
                        end
                        rightLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR
                        if not rightLeg:GetAttribute("VH_KorbloxHooked") then
                            rightLeg:SetAttribute("VH_KorbloxHooked", true)
                            rightLeg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Color"):Connect(function()
                                if rightLeg:GetAttribute("VH_KorbloxHooked") and rightLeg.Color ~= DARK_GREY_COLOR then
                                    rightLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
                    local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
                    if rightUpperLeg then
                        rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 1
                        local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                        local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                        if rightLowerLeg then rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 1 end
                        if rightFoot then rightFoot.Transparency = 1 end
                        local korbloxLeg = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                        if not korbloxLeg then
                            korbloxLeg = Instance.new("Part")
                            korbloxLeg.Name = "KorbloxLeg"
                            korbloxLeg.Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1)
                            korbloxLeg.Anchored = false
                            korbloxLeg.CanCollide = false
                            korbloxLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR
                            korbloxLeg.Parent = char
                            local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                            mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                            mesh.MeshId = KORBLOX_MESH_ID
                            mesh.TextureId = KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID
                            mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                            mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                            mesh.Parent = korbloxLeg
                            local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
                            weld.Part0 = rightUpperLeg
                            weld.Part1 = korbloxLeg
                            weld.C0 = CFrame.new(0, -0.8, 0)
                            weld.Name = "KorbloxWeld"
                            weld.Parent = korbloxLeg
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
                    local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
                    if rightLeg then
                        rightLeg:SetAttribute("VH_KorbloxHooked", nil)
                        for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                            if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "KorbloxMesh" then
                                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
                    local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
                    if rightUpperLeg then
                        rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 0
                        local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                        local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                        if rightLowerLeg then rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 0 end
                        if rightFoot then rightFoot.Transparency = 0 end
                        local korbloxLeg = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                        if korbloxLeg then pcall(function() korbloxLeg:Destroy() end) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function resolveBartTemplates()
            if BART_CACHE.resolved and BART_CACHE.shirtTemplate and BART_CACHE.pantsTemplate then
                return BART_CACHE
            end
            -- 1) HumanoidDescription resolve (metodo 7UP — catalog ID -> template real)
            pcall(function()
                local desc = Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
                desc.Shirt = BART.shirtId
                desc.Pants = BART.pantsId
                if BART.headId then pcall(function() desc.Head = BART.headId end) end
                pcall(function() desc.HatAccessory = tostring(BART.hatId) end)
                local model = Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(desc, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
                desc:Destroy()
                if model then
                    local sh = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt")
                    local pa = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
                    if sh and sh.ShirtTemplate ~= "" then BART_CACHE.shirtTemplate = sh.ShirtTemplate end
                    if pa and pa.PantsTemplate ~= "" then BART_CACHE.pantsTemplate = pa.PantsTemplate end
                    model:Destroy()
                end
            end)
            -- 2) GetObjects fallback
            if not BART_CACHE.shirtTemplate then
                pcall(function()
                    local objs = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(BART.shirtId))
                    if objs then
                        for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
                            if o:IsA("Shirt") and o.ShirtTemplate ~= "" then
                                BART_CACHE.shirtTemplate = o.ShirtTemplate
                            end
                            pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not BART_CACHE.pantsTemplate then
                pcall(function()
                    local objs = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(BART.pantsId))
                    if objs then
                        for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
                            if o:IsA("Pants") and o.PantsTemplate ~= "" then
                                BART_CACHE.pantsTemplate = o.PantsTemplate
                            end
                            pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            BART_CACHE.shirtTemplate = BART_CACHE.shirtTemplate or ("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(BART.shirtId))
            BART_CACHE.pantsTemplate = BART_CACHE.pantsTemplate or ("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(BART.pantsId))
            BART_CACHE.resolved = true
            return BART_CACHE
        end

        local function applyBartToChar(char, enabled)
            if not char then return end
            if enabled then
                local assets = resolveBartTemplates()
                -- remove clothes antigas do Bart
                for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name == "VH_BartShirt" or child.Name == "VH_BartPants" then
                        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
                -- Shirt
                local shirt = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt")
                if not shirt then
                    shirt = Instance.new("Shirt")
                    shirt.Name = "VH_BartShirt"
                    shirt.Parent = char
                end
                shirt.Name = "VH_BartShirt"
                pcall(function() shirt.ShirtTemplate = assets.shirtTemplate end)
                -- Pants
                local pants = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
                if not pants then
                    pants = Instance.new("Pants")
                    pants.Name = "VH_BartPants"
                    pants.Parent = char
                end
                pants.Name = "VH_BartPants"
                pcall(function() pants.PantsTemplate = assets.pantsTemplate end)
                -- Body color amarelo Bart
                local bc = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
                if not bc then
                    bc = Instance.new("BodyColors")
                    bc.Parent = char
                end
                local c = BART.bodyColor
                pcall(function()
                    bc.HeadColor3 = c
                    bc.TorsoColor3 = c
                    bc.LeftArmColor3 = c
                    bc.RightArmColor3 = c
                    bc.LeftLegColor3 = c
                    bc.RightLegColor3 = c
                end)
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        pcall(function() part.Color = c end)
                    end
                end
                -- ApplyDescription path (roupa + hat + head)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not hum then return end
                    pcall(function()
                        local desc = hum:GetAppliedDescription()
                        if not desc then desc = Instance.new("HumanoidDescription") end
                        desc.Shirt = BART.shirtId
                        desc.Pants = BART.pantsId
                        if BART.headId then pcall(function() desc.Head = BART.headId end) end
                        local hat = tostring(BART.hatId)
                        local current = tostring(desc.HatAccessory or "")
                        if not current:find(hat, 1, true) then
                            desc.HatAccessory = current == "" and hat or (current .. "," .. hat)
                        end
                        hum:ApplyDescription(desc)
                    end)
                    -- reforça templates depois do ApplyDescription
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    local sh = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt")
                    local pa = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
                    if sh then pcall(function() sh.ShirtTemplate = assets.shirtTemplate end) end
                    if pa then pcall(function() pa.PantsTemplate = assets.pantsTemplate end) end
                    -- re-aplica cor (ApplyDescription pode sobrescrever)
                    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                            pcall(function() part.Color = c end)
                        end
                    end
                    local bc2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
                    if bc2 then
                        pcall(function()
                            bc2.HeadColor3 = c; bc2.TorsoColor3 = c
                            bc2.LeftArmColor3 = c; bc2.RightArmColor3 = c
                            bc2.LeftLegColor3 = c; bc2.RightLegColor3 = c
                        end)
                    end
                    -- hat via GetObjects se nao veio
                    local hasHat = false
                    for _, ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                        if ch:IsA("Accessory") and ch:GetAttribute("VH_BartHat") then hasHat = true end
                    end
                    if not hasHat then
                        pcall(function()
                            local objs = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(BART.hatId))
                            if objs and objs[1] then
                                local hatObj = objs[1]
                                hatObj:SetAttribute("VH_BartHat", true)
                                hatObj.Parent = char
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            else
                for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name == "VH_BartShirt" or child.Name == "VH_BartPants" then
                        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    elseif child:IsA("Accessory") and child:GetAttribute("VH_BartHat") then
                        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function clearTaggedAccessories(char, tag)
            if not char then return end
            for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if child:GetAttribute(tag) or (child.Name and tostring(child.Name):find("VH_Shadow", 1, true)) then
                    pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end

        local function loadAccessoryById(assetId, char, hum, tagName)
            local objs
            pcall(function()
                if game.GetObjects then
                    objs = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(assetId))
                end
            end)
            if type(objs) ~= "table" then return end
            for _, obj in ipairs(objs) do
                if obj:IsA("Accessory") then
                    obj.Name = tagName
                    obj:SetAttribute("VH_ShadowItem", true)
                    pcall(function()
                        if hum and hum.AddAccessory then
                            hum:AddAccessory(obj)
                        end
                    end)
                    if not obj.Parent then
                        pcall(function() obj.Parent = char end)
                    end
                else
                    -- try find accessory inside model
                    local acc = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Accessory")
                    if acc then
                        acc.Name = tagName
                        acc:SetAttribute("VH_ShadowItem", true)
                        pcall(function()
                            if hum and hum.AddAccessory then
                                hum:AddAccessory(acc)
                            end
                        end)
                        if not acc.Parent then
                            pcall(function() acc.Parent = char end)
                        end
                    end
                    pcall(function() if obj.Parent == nil or obj.Parent == game then obj:Destroy() end end)
                end
            end
        end

        local function applyShadowToChar(char, enabled)
            if not char then return end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if enabled then
                -- body all black
                local c = SHADOW.bodyColor
                local bc = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
                if not bc then
                    bc = Instance.new("BodyColors")
                    bc.Parent = char
                end
                pcall(function()
                    bc.HeadColor3 = c
                    bc.TorsoColor3 = c
                    bc.LeftArmColor3 = c
                    bc.RightArmColor3 = c
                    bc.LeftLegColor3 = c
                    bc.RightLegColor3 = c
                end)
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        pcall(function() part.Color = c end)
                    end
                end
                -- shirt / pants
                local shirt = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt")
                if not shirt then
                    shirt = Instance.new("Shirt")
                    shirt.Name = "VH_ShadowShirt"
                    shirt.Parent = char
                end
                pcall(function() shirt.ShirtTemplate = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(SHADOW.shirtId) end)
                if not shirt.ShirtTemplate or shirt.ShirtTemplate == "" or shirt.ShirtTemplate == "rbxassetid://0" then
                    pcall(function() shirt.ShirtTemplate = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=" .. tostring(SHADOW.shirtId) end)
                end
                local pants = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
                if not pants then
                    pants = Instance.new("Pants")
                    pants.Name = "VH_ShadowPants"
                    pants.Parent = char
                end
                pcall(function() pants.PantsTemplate = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(SHADOW.pantsId) end)
                if not pants.PantsTemplate or pants.PantsTemplate == "" or pants.PantsTemplate == "rbxassetid://0" then
                    pcall(function() pants.PantsTemplate = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=" .. tostring(SHADOW.pantsId) end)
                end
                -- accessories (cap, mask, hair)
                task.spawn(function()
                    clearTaggedAccessories(char, "VH_ShadowItem")
                    for i, aid in ipairs(SHADOW.accessories) do
                        loadAccessoryById(aid, char, hum, "VH_ShadowAcc_" .. i)
                    end
                end)
                -- korblox junto com shadow (como na foto)
                if SHADOW.withKorblox then
                    _G.ViolenceUIRefs.korbloxEnabled = true
                    applyKorbloxToChar(char, true)
                end
            else
                clearTaggedAccessories(char, "VH_ShadowItem")
                for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name == "VH_ShadowShirt" or child.Name == "VH_ShadowPants" then
                        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function applyColoredBody(char, torsoC, limbC, legC, headC)
            local bc = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
            if not bc then bc = Instance.new("BodyColors"); bc.Parent = char end
            pcall(function()
                if headC then bc.HeadColor3 = headC end
                if torsoC then bc.TorsoColor3 = torsoC end
                if limbC then
                    bc.LeftArmColor3 = limbC
                    bc.RightArmColor3 = limbC
                end
                if legC then
                    bc.LeftLegColor3 = legC
                    bc.RightLegColor3 = legC
                end
            end)
            for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                    local n = part.Name
                    if n == "Head" and headC then
                        pcall(function() part.Color = headC end)
                    elseif (n:find("Torso") or n == "UpperTorso" or n == "LowerTorso") and torsoC then
                        pcall(function() part.Color = torsoC end)
                    elseif (n:find("Arm") or n:find("Hand")) and limbC then
                        pcall(function() part.Color = limbC end)
                    elseif (n:find("Leg") or n:find("Foot")) and legC then
                        pcall(function() part.Color = legC end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function applyClothes(char, shirtId, pantsId, tag)
            local function setTemplate(obj, prop, id)
                if not obj or not id then return end
                local sid = tostring(id)
                -- tenta formatos que o Roblox aceita
                local ok = pcall(function() obj[prop] = "rbxassetid://" .. sid end)
                if not ok or obj[prop] == "" or obj[prop] == "rbxassetid://0" then
                    pcall(function() obj[prop] = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=" .. sid end)
                end
            end
            if shirtId then
                local shirt = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt")
                if not shirt then shirt = Instance.new("Shirt"); shirt.Name = tag .. "Shirt"; shirt.Parent = char end
                setTemplate(shirt, "ShirtTemplate", shirtId)
            end
            if pantsId then
                local pants = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
                if not pants then pants = Instance.new("Pants"); pants.Name = tag .. "Pants"; pants.Parent = char end
                setTemplate(pants, "PantsTemplate", pantsId)
            end
        end

        local function applyPunkToChar(char, enabled)
            if not char then return end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if enabled then
                applyColoredBody(char, PUNK.bodyColor, PUNK.skinColor, PUNK.bodyColor, PUNK.skinColor)
                applyClothes(char, PUNK.shirtId, PUNK.pantsId, "VH_Punk")
                task.spawn(function()
                    clearTaggedAccessories(char, "VH_PunkItem")
                    for i, aid in ipairs(PUNK.accessories) do
                        loadAccessoryById(aid, char, hum, "VH_PunkAcc_" .. i)
                        for _, ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                            if ch.Name == "VH_PunkAcc_" .. i then
                                ch:SetAttribute("VH_PunkItem", true)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            else
                clearTaggedAccessories(char, "VH_PunkItem")
                for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if tostring(child.Name):find("VH_Punk", 1, true) then
                        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function applyEmoToChar(char, enabled)
            if not char then return end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if enabled then
                applyColoredBody(char, EMO.bodyColor, EMO.bodyColor, EMO.bodyColor, EMO.skinColor)
                applyClothes(char, EMO.shirtId, EMO.pantsId, "VH_Emo")
                task.spawn(function()
                    clearTaggedAccessories(char, "VH_EmoItem")
                    for i, aid in ipairs(EMO.accessories) do
                        loadAccessoryById(aid, char, hum, "VH_EmoAcc_" .. i)
                        for _, ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                            if ch.Name == "VH_EmoAcc_" .. i then
                                ch:SetAttribute("VH_EmoItem", true)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                if EMO.withKorblox then
                    _G.ViolenceUIRefs.korbloxEnabled = true
                    applyKorbloxToChar(char, true)
                end
            else
                clearTaggedAccessories(char, "VH_EmoItem")
                for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if tostring(child.Name):find("VH_Emo", 1, true) then
                        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function clearBleedItems(char)
            if not char then return end
            for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if child.Name == "AuFfitAccessory" or child.Name == "VH_BleedKorblox" then
                    pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end

        local function applyBleedToChar(char, packName)
            if not char then return end
            clearBleedItems(char)
            if not packName or packName == "Off" or packName == "" then
                return
            end
            local config = BLEED_PACKS[packName]
            if not config then return end

            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
            if not head then return end

            -- head mesh (classic head look)
            if config.headMesh then
                for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if d:IsA("CharacterMesh") and d.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.Head then
                        pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
                local done = false
                if head:IsA("MeshPart") then
                    done = pcall(function()
                        head.MeshId = config.headMesh
                        if config.headTexture then head.TextureID = config.headTexture end
                    end)
                end
                if not done then
                    local sm = head:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh") or Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                    sm.Parent = head
                    sm.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                    sm.MeshId = config.headMesh
                    sm.TextureId = config.headTexture or ""
                end
            end

            if config.shirt then
                local s = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Shirt") or Instance.new("Shirt")
                s.Name = "Shirt"
                s.ShirtTemplate = config.shirt
                s.Parent = char
            end
            if config.pants then
                local p = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants") or Instance.new("Pants")
                p.Name = "Pants"
                p.PantsTemplate = config.pants
                p.Parent = char
            end

            if config.accessory and head then
                local objs = nil
                local ok, res = pcall(function()
                    return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(config.accessory))
                end)
                if ok and type(res) == "table" and #res > 0 then
                    objs = res
                else
                    ok, res = pcall(function()
                        return game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(config.accessory)
                    end)
                    if ok and res then objs = {res} end
                end
                if objs then
                    local handle
                    for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
                        if o:IsA("BasePart") then handle = o; break end
                        local f = o:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                        if f then handle = f; break end
                    end
                    if handle then
                        local h = handle:Clone()
                        h.Name = "AuFfitAccessory"
                        h.CanCollide = false
                        h.Anchored = false
                        h.Massless = true
                        h.Parent = char
                        local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
                        weld.Part0 = head
                        weld.Part1 = h
                        weld.C0 = CFrame.new(config.offset or Vector3.zero)
                        weld.Parent = h
                    end
                    for _, o in ipairs(objs) do pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
                end
            end

            if config.korblox and config.korblox ~= "none" then
                local function attachKorblox(side)
                    local ids = { left = 139607673, right = 139607718 }
                    local targets = { left = "LeftUpperLeg", right = "RightUpperLeg" }
                    local hides = {
                        left = {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"},
                        right = {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"},
                    }
                    local targetPart = char:FindFirstChild(targets[side])
                    if not targetPart then return end
                    for _, partName in ipairs(hides[side]) do
                        local limb = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
                        if limb and limb:IsA("BasePart") then limb.Transparency = 1 end
                    end
                    local success, objects = pcall(function()
                        return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(ids[side]))
                    end)
                    if not success or not objects or #objects == 0 then return end
                    local assetModel = objects[1]
                    local mainMesh = assetModel:IsA("BasePart") and assetModel or assetModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                    if not mainMesh then pcall(function() assetModel:Destroy() end); return end
                    mainMesh.CanCollide = false
                    mainMesh.Massless = true
                    mainMesh.Name = "VH_BleedKorblox"
                    mainMesh.CFrame = targetPart.CFrame
                    local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
                    weld.Part0 = targetPart
                    weld.Part1 = mainMesh
                    weld.Parent = mainMesh
                    assetModel.Parent = char
                end
                if config.korblox == "left" then attachKorblox("left")
                elseif config.korblox == "right" then attachKorblox("right") end
            end
        end

        local function resolveBossTemplates()
            if BOSS_CACHE.resolved and BOSS_CACHE.shirtTemplate and BOSS_CACHE.pantsTemplate then
                return BOSS_CACHE
            end
            pcall(function()
                local desc = Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
                desc.Shirt = BOSS.shirtId
                desc.Pants = BOSS.pantsId
                local model = Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(desc, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
                desc:Destroy()
                if model then
                    local sh = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt")
                    local pa = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
                    if sh and sh.ShirtTemplate ~= "" then BOSS_CACHE.shirtTemplate = sh.ShirtTemplate end
                    if pa and pa.PantsTemplate ~= "" then BOSS_CACHE.pantsTemplate = pa.PantsTemplate end
                    model:Destroy()
                end
            end)
            if not BOSS_CACHE.shirtTemplate then
                pcall(function()
                    local objs = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(BOSS.shirtId))
                    if objs then
                        for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
                            if o:IsA("Shirt") and o.ShirtTemplate ~= "" then BOSS_CACHE.shirtTemplate = o.ShirtTemplate end
                            pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not BOSS_CACHE.pantsTemplate then
                pcall(function()
                    local objs = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(BOSS.pantsId))
                    if objs then
                        for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
                            if o:IsA("Pants") and o.PantsTemplate ~= "" then BOSS_CACHE.pantsTemplate = o.PantsTemplate end
                            pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not BOSS_CACHE.shirtTemplate then BOSS_CACHE.shirtTemplate = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(BOSS.shirtId) end
            if not BOSS_CACHE.pantsTemplate then BOSS_CACHE.pantsTemplate = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(BOSS.pantsId) end
            BOSS_CACHE.resolved = true
            return BOSS_CACHE
        end

        local function loadAccessoryId(assetId, char, hum, tagName)
            if not assetId or not char then return false end
            local function adopt(acc)
                if not acc or not acc:IsA("Accessory") then return false end
                acc:SetAttribute(tagName, true)
                acc.Name = tagName
                if hum and hum.AddAccessory then
                    pcall(function() hum:AddAccessory(acc) end)
                end
                if not acc.Parent then acc.Parent = char end
                return acc.Parent == char or (acc.Parent and acc.Parent:IsDescendantOf(char))
            end
            -- 1) game:GetObjects (executor)
            local ok1, objs = pcall(function()
                return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(assetId))
            end)
            if ok1 and type(objs) == "table" then
                for _, obj in ipairs(objs) do
                    if obj:IsA("Accessory") then
                        if adopt(obj) then return true end
                    else
                        for _, d in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do
                            if d:IsA("Accessory") then
                                if adopt(d) then return true end
                            end
                        end
                        pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
            -- 2) InsertService:LoadAsset
            local ok2, model = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(tonumber(assetId))
            end)
            if ok2 and model then
                for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("Accessory") then
                        d.Parent = nil
                        if adopt(d) then
                            pcall(function() model:Destroy() end)
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                end
                if model:IsA("Accessory") then
                    if adopt(model) then return true end
                end
                pcall(function() model:Destroy() end)
            end
            return false
        end

        local function applyBossToChar(char, enabled)
            if not char then return end
            if enabled then
                local assets = resolveBossTemplates()
                local shirt = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt") or Instance.new("Shirt")
                shirt.Name = "VH_BossShirt"
                shirt.Parent = char
                pcall(function() shirt.ShirtTemplate = assets.shirtTemplate end)
                local pants = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants") or Instance.new("Pants")
                pants.Name = "VH_BossPants"
                pants.Parent = char
                pcall(function() pants.PantsTemplate = assets.pantsTemplate end)
                local c = BOSS.bodyColor
                local bc = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors") or Instance.new("BodyColors")
                bc.Parent = char
                pcall(function()
                    bc.HeadColor3=c; bc.TorsoColor3=c; bc.LeftArmColor3=c
                    bc.RightArmColor3=c; bc.LeftLegColor3=c; bc.RightLegColor3=c
                end)
                -- chifre roxo fogo NA HORA (sync) + roupa via description
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then
                    pcall(function()
                        local desc = hum:GetAppliedDescription() or Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
                        desc.Shirt = BOSS.shirtId
                        desc.Pants = BOSS.pantsId
                        local hat = tostring(BOSS.hatId)
                        local cur = tostring(desc.HatAccessory or "")
                        if not cur:find(hat, 1, true) then
                            desc.HatAccessory = cur == "" and hat or (cur .. "," .. hat)
                        end
                        hum:ApplyDescription(desc)
                    end)
                    -- reforça template + força o hat se description falhar
                    local sh = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt")
                    local pa = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
                    if sh then sh.Name="VH_BossShirt"; pcall(function() sh.ShirtTemplate=assets.shirtTemplate end) end
                    if pa then pa.Name="VH_BossPants"; pcall(function() pa.PantsTemplate=assets.pantsTemplate end) end
                    local hasHat=false
                    for _,ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                        if ch:IsA("Accessory") and (ch:GetAttribute("VH_BossHat") or ch.Name=="VH_BossHat") then hasHat=true end
                    end
                    if not hasHat then loadAccessoryId(BOSS.hatId, char, hum, "VH_BossHat") end
                end
                -- reforço async (alguns executors demoram no GetObjects)
                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(0.35)
                    if not char or not char.Parent then return end
                    local hum2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local hasHat=false
                    for _,ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                        if ch:IsA("Accessory") and (ch:GetAttribute("VH_BossHat") or ch.Name=="VH_BossHat") then hasHat=true end
                    end
                    if not hasHat then loadAccessoryId(BOSS.hatId, char, hum2, "VH_BossHat") end
                    local sh = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt")
                    local pa = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
                    if sh then sh.Name="VH_BossShirt"; pcall(function() sh.ShirtTemplate=assets.shirtTemplate end) end
                    if pa then pa.Name="VH_BossPants"; pcall(function() pa.PantsTemplate=assets.pantsTemplate end) end
                end)
            else
                for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name=="VH_BossShirt" or child.Name=="VH_BossPants" then pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    elseif child:IsA("Accessory") and (child:GetAttribute("VH_BossHat") or child.Name=="VH_BossHat") then
                        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end


        local function reapplyAllSkins(char)
            char = char or LP.Character
            if not char then return end
            local bossOn = _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bossEnabled == true
            applyHeadlessToChar(char, _G.ViolenceUIRefs.headlessEnabled == true)
            local bleed = _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bleedPack
            if bleed and bleed ~= "Off" and BLEED_PACKS[bleed] then
                applyBartToChar(char, false)
                applyShadowToChar(char, false)
                applyBossToChar(char, false)
                applyBleedToChar(char, bleed)
                if _G.ViolenceUIRefs.headlessEnabled then applyHeadlessToChar(char, true) end
            elseif bossOn then
                applyBartToChar(char, false)
                applyShadowToChar(char, false)
                applyBleedToChar(char, "Off")
                applyKorbloxToChar(char, _G.ViolenceUIRefs.korbloxEnabled == true)
                applyBossToChar(char, true)
            else
                applyKorbloxToChar(char, _G.ViolenceUIRefs.korbloxEnabled == true)
                applyBartToChar(char, _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bartEnabled == true)
                applyShadowToChar(char, _G.ViolenceUIRefs.shadowEnabled == true)
                applyBossToChar(char, false)
                applyBleedToChar(char, "Off")
            end
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.punkEnabled = false
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.emoEnabled = false
            pcall(function() applyPunkToChar(char, false) end)
            pcall(function() applyEmoToChar(char, false) end)
        end
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.reapplyAllSkins = reapplyAllSkins

        local function clearAllOutfitSkins()
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bartEnabled = false
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.shadowEnabled = false
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bossEnabled = false
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.punkEnabled = false
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.emoEnabled = false
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bleedPack = "Off"
        end

        local function applySkinVisual(idx)
            local opt = SKIN_OPTIONS[idx]
            if not opt then return end
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedSkinIndex = idx
            if opt.kind == "bart" then
                local on = not _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bartEnabled
                clearAllOutfitSkins(); _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bartEnabled = on
            elseif opt.kind == "shadow" then
                local on = not _G.ViolenceUIRefs.shadowEnabled
                clearAllOutfitSkins(); _G.ViolenceUIRefs.shadowEnabled = on
            elseif opt.kind == "boss" then
                local on = not _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bossEnabled
                clearAllOutfitSkins(); _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bossEnabled = on
            elseif opt.kind == "bleed1" or opt.kind == "bleed2" or opt.kind == "bleed3" then
                local packName = opt.name
                local on = _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bleedPack ~= packName
                clearAllOutfitSkins()
                if on then _G.ViolenceUIRefs.bleedPack = packName end
            end
            local char = LP.Character
            reapplyAllSkins(char)
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
        end
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.applySkinVisual = applySkinVisual

        -- Reaplica ao respawn
        LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.wait(0.35)
            pcall(reapplyAllSkins, char)
        end)

            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.setHeadlessVisual = setH

        end

            local detail = {root=nil, spinConn=nil, token=0, baseY=0}

            local function destroyDetail()
                if detail.spinConn then pcall(function() detail.spinConn:Disconnect() end); detail.spinConn=nil end
                if detail.root then pcall(function() detail.root:Destroy() end); detail.root=nil end
            end

            local function refreshCards()
                for _, child in ipairs(grid:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local st = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                        local kindOpt=nil
                        for j,o in ipairs(SKIN_OPTIONS) do
                            if j==child.LayoutOrder then kindOpt=o; break end
                        end
                        if st and kindOpt then
                            local now=isOn(kindOpt)
                            st.Thickness=now and 2.4 or 1
                            st.Transparency=now and 0 or 0.35
                            st.Color=now and Color3.fromRGB(255,200,40) or Color3.fromRGB(45,45,45)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local function freezeModel(model)
                local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then
                    pcall(function()
                        hum.AutoRotate = false
                        hum.PlatformStand = true
                        hum.WalkSpeed = 0
                        hum.JumpPower = 0
                        hum.JumpHeight = 0
                        hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
                    end)
                    pcall(function()
                        local anim = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                        if anim then
                            for _, t in ipairs(anim:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                                pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") or d:IsA("Tool") or d:IsA("Sound") or d.Name == "Animate" then
                        pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
                    elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
                        d.Anchored = true
                        d.CanCollide = false
                        d.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        d.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end
            end

            local function buildPreviewModel(opt, world)
                -- 1) tenta CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription (melhor pra roupa/hat)
                local preview = nil
                pcall(function()
                    local desc = Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
                    -- base: copia description do player se existir
                    local srcHum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if srcHum then
                        local okd, d = pcall(function() return srcHum:GetAppliedDescription() end)
                        if okd and d then
                            desc = d:Clone()
                        end
                    end
                    if opt.kind == "boss" then
                        desc.Shirt = BOSS.shirtId
                        desc.Pants = BOSS.pantsId
                        local hat = tostring(BOSS.hatId)
                        local cur = tostring(desc.HatAccessory or "")
                        if not cur:find(hat, 1, true) then
                            desc.HatAccessory = cur == "" and hat or (cur .. "," .. hat)
                        end
                    elseif opt.kind == "bart" and BART then
                        desc.Shirt = BART.shirtId
                        desc.Pants = BART.pantsId
                        if BART.hatId then
                            local hat = tostring(BART.hatId)
                            local cur = tostring(desc.HatAccessory or "")
                            if not cur:find(hat, 1, true) then
                                desc.HatAccessory = cur == "" and hat or (cur .. "," .. hat)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    preview = Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(desc, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
                    pcall(function() desc:Destroy() end)
                end)

                -- 2) fallback: clone do character
                if not preview then
                    local source = LP.Character
                    if not source then return nil end
                    local was = source.Archivable
                    source.Archivable = true
                    pcall(function() preview = source:Clone() end)
                    source.Archivable = was
                end
                if not preview then return nil end
                preview.Name = "VerzedSkinPreview"

                -- parent cedo no WorldModel (Render no Viewport precisa disso)
                preview.Parent = world

                for _, d in ipairs(preview:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") or d:IsA("Tool") or d:IsA("Sound") or d.Name == "Animate" then
                        pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end

                -- aplica skin no modelo já parentado
                pcall(function()
                    if opt.kind == "bart" then applyBartToChar(preview, true)
                    elseif opt.kind == "shadow" then applyShadowToChar(preview, true)
                    elseif opt.kind == "boss" then applyBossToChar(preview, true)
                    elseif opt.kind == "bleed1" or opt.kind == "bleed2" or opt.kind == "bleed3" then
                        applyBleedToChar(preview, opt.name)
                    end
                end)

                -- espera hat/roupa
                local t0 = tick()
                while tick() - t0 < 0.8 do
                    local hasAcc = false
                    for _, ch in ipairs(preview:GetChildren()) do
                        if ch:IsA("Accessory") then hasAcc = true; break end
                    end
                    if hasAcc and opt.kind ~= "shadow" then break end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
                task.wait(0.15)

                freezeModel(preview)

                for _, d in ipairs(preview:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                        d.Anchored = true
                        d.CanCollide = false
                        d.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                        if d.Transparency > 0.95 and d.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                            -- não força headless no preview
                        end
                    end
                end

                local root = preview:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    or preview:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                    or preview:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                    or preview:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                if root then preview.PrimaryPart = root end

                local ok, boxCF, boxSize = pcall(function()
                    local cf, sz = preview:GetBoundingBox()
                    return cf, sz
                end)
                if ok and boxCF and boxSize then
                    local bottom = boxCF.Position.Y - boxSize.Y * 0.5
                    detail.baseY = -bottom
                    preview:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, detail.baseY, 0))
                else
                    detail.baseY = 0
                    pcall(function() preview:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)) end)
                end
                return preview
            end

            function openSkinDetailPreview(idx)
                local opt = SKIN_OPTIONS[idx]
                if not opt then return end
                destroyDetail()
                detail.token = detail.token + 1
                local token = detail.token

                local root = Instance.new("Frame", modal)
                root.Name = "SkinDetailPreview"
                root.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                root.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                root.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                root.BorderSizePixel = 0
                root.ZIndex = 130
                detail.root = root

                local panel = Instance.new("Frame", root)
                panel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                panel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
                panel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16)
                panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12)
                panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
                panel.ZIndex = 131
                Instance.new("UICorner", panel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)

                local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", panel)
                title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 28)
                title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 10)
                title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                title.Text = string.upper(opt.name) .. "  ·  PREVIEW"
                title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                title.TextSize = 15
                title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                title.ZIndex = 132

                local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", panel)
                closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28)
                closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 10)
                closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 32)
                closeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                closeBtn.Text = "X"
                closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                closeBtn.TextSize = 13
                closeBtn.ZIndex = 133
                Instance.new("UICorner", closeBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
                closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(destroyDetail)

                local previewCard = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
                previewCard.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, -118)
                previewCard.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 44)
                previewCard.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 20)
                previewCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
                previewCard.ClipsDescendants = true
                previewCard.ZIndex = 132
                Instance.new("UICorner", previewCard).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)

                local viewport = Instance.new("ViewportFrame", previewCard)
                viewport.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                viewport.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                viewport.BorderSizePixel = 0
                viewport.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 220)
                viewport.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                viewport.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-0.5, -1, -0.5)
                viewport.ZIndex = 133

                local loading = Instance.new("TextLabel", previewCard)
                loading.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                loading.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                loading.Text = "LOADING..."
                loading.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
                loading.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                loading.TextSize = 13
                loading.ZIndex = 134

                local badge = Instance.new("TextLabel", previewCard)
                badge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 88, 0, 22)
                badge.Position = UDim2.new(1, -98, 0, 10)
                badge.BackgroundColor3 = isOn(opt) and Color3.fromRGB(40, 160, 70) or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 48)
                badge.BorderSizePixel = 0
                badge.Text = isOn(opt) and "EQUIPPED" or "PREVIEW"
                badge.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                badge.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                badge.TextSize = 10
                badge.ZIndex = 135
                Instance.new("UICorner", badge).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

                local applyBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", panel)
                applyBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -20, 0, 40)
                applyBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -52)
                applyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = isOn(opt) and Color3.fromRGB(30, 120, 55) or Color3.fromRGB(0, 174, 73)
                applyBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                applyBtn.Text = isOn(opt) and "EQUIPPED" or "APPLY SKIN"
                applyBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                applyBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                applyBtn.TextSize = 12
                applyBtn.ZIndex = 133
                Instance.new("UICorner", applyBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

                local removeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", panel)
                removeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -20, 0, 40)
                removeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 1, -52)
                removeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = isOn(opt) and Color3.fromRGB(180, 40, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 52)
                removeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                removeBtn.Text = "REMOVE"
                removeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 245)
                removeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                removeBtn.TextSize = 12
                removeBtn.ZIndex = 133
                Instance.new("UICorner", removeBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

                local function forceOn(wantOn)
                    local now = isOn(opt)
                    if wantOn ~= now then applySkinVisual(idx)
                    elseif wantOn then pcall(reapplyAllSkins, LP.Character) end
                end
                applyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    forceOn(true)
                    badge.Text = "EQUIPPED"
                    badge.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 160, 70)
                    applyBtn.Text = "EQUIPPED"
                    applyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 120, 55)
                    removeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 40, 50)
                    refreshCards()
                end)
                removeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    forceOn(false)
                    badge.Text = "PREVIEW"
                    badge.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 48)
                    applyBtn.Text = "APPLY SKIN"
                    applyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 174, 73)
                    removeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 52)
                    refreshCards()
                end)

                task.spawn(function()
                    if token ~= detail.token then return end

                    local cam = Instance.new("Camera")
                    cam.FieldOfView = 30
                    cam.Parent = viewport
                    viewport.CurrentCamera = cam

                    local world = Instance.new("WorldModel")
                    world.Parent = viewport

                    local model = buildPreviewModel(opt, world)
                    if token ~= detail.token or not viewport.Parent then
                        if model then pcall(function() model:Destroy() end) end
                        return
                    end
                    if not model then
                        loading.Text = "PREVIEW UNAVAILABLE"
                        return
                    end

                    -- enquadra depois dos accessories
                    local boxCF, boxSize = model:GetBoundingBox()
                    local center = boxCF.Position
                    local maxDim = math.max(boxSize.X, boxSize.Y, boxSize.Z)
                    local dist = math.clamp(maxDim * 1.25, 5.5, 12)
                    local midY = center.Y
                    cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(
                        Vector3.new(0, midY + boxSize.Y * 0.05, dist),
                        Vector3.new(0, midY, 0)
                    )

                    loading.Visible = false
                    local angle = 0.35
                    local yOff = detail.baseY or 0
                    detail.spinConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
                        if token ~= detail.token or not model or not model.Parent then return end
                        angle = angle + dt * 1.0
                        pcall(function()
                            model:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, yOff, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0))
                        end)
                    end)
                end)
            end

            closeM.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() destroyDetail() end)
            modal.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if not parent then destroyDetail() end
            end)
        end)
    end

            -- Usa UIScale para escalar tudo (textos, botoes, nomes) sem cortar

                sc.Scale = v / 100
            elseif main then
                local baseW, baseH = 375, 495
                main.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(baseW * v / 100), 0, math.floor(baseH * v / 100))
            end
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
        end
        local minus = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        minus.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)

        minus.BorderSizePixel = 0

        minus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        minus.TextSize = 14

        plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() applyUIScale((_G.ViolenceUIRefs.uiScale or 100) + 5) end)
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.applyUIScale = applyUIScale
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.uiScaleLbl = valLbl
