    -- Steal Bar Size
    do
        local row = mkRow(40)
        mkLabel(row, "Steal Bar Size")
        local valLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        valLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 26)
        valLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -78, 0.5, -13)
        valLbl.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
        valLbl.BorderSizePixel = 0
        valLbl.Text = tostring(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealBarScale or 100)
        valLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        valLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        valLbl.TextSize = 12
        Instance.new("UICorner", valLbl).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local function applyStealBarScale(v)
            v = math.clamp(math.floor(v + 0.5), 60, 150)

            -- Mantém o valor em memória ANTES de qualquer save.
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs = _G.ViolenceUIRefs or {}
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealBarScale = v
            valLbl.Text = tostring(v)

            if pbFrame then
                local baseW, baseH = 360, 44
                pbFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
                    0, math.floor(baseW * v / 100),
                    0, math.floor(baseH * v / 100)
                )
            end

            -- Salva imediatamente e também mantém o valor no config normal.
            pcall(saveAllSettings)

            -- Backup dedicado: evita que outro save posterior com uma referência antiga
            -- faça a barra voltar para 100 ao reiniciar.
            pcall(function()
                if type(writefile) ~= "function" or type(HS) ~= "table" then return end
                local path = CONFIG_FILE or "ViolenceHub.json"
                local data = nil

                if type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(path) and type(readfile) == "function" then
                    local ok, raw = pcall(readfile, path)
                    if ok and type(raw) == "string" then
                        local ok2, decoded = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(raw) end)
                        if ok2 and type(decoded) == "table" then data = decoded end
                    end
                end

                data = data or {}
                data.stealBarScale = v
                local encoded = HS:JSONEncode(data)
                writefile(path, encoded)

                if makefolder then pcall(makefolder, "ViolenceHub") end
                pcall(function() writefile("ViolenceHub/config.json", encoded) end)
            end)
        end
        local minus = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        minus.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
        minus.Position = UDim2.new(1, -108, 0.5, -13)
        minus.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
        minus.BorderSizePixel = 0
        minus.Text = "<"
        minus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        minus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        minus.TextSize = 14
        Instance.new("UICorner", minus).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local plus = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        plus.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
        plus.Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0.5, -13)
        plus.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
        plus.BorderSizePixel = 0
        plus.Text = ">"
        plus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        plus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        plus.TextSize = 14
        Instance.new("UICorner", plus).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        minus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() applyStealBarScale((_G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealBarScale or 100) - 5) end)
        plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() applyStealBarScale((_G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealBarScale or 100) + 5) end)
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.applyStealBarScale = applyStealBarScale
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealBarScaleLbl = valLbl
    end

    -- Intro Song selector
    do
        local row = mkRow(40)
        mkLabel(row, "Intro Song")
        local songs = {"Blue Bands", "Legacy", "Paznerknacker", "Tesla", "King Nasir", "Pure Cocaine", "Nuts", "Gelato", "Freaked Out", "Scam Likely", "No Hook", "Overseas", "Backr00ms", "On That Time", "NO MUSIC"}
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongIndex = _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongIndex or 1
        local valLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        valLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 26)
        valLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -102, 0.5, -13)
        valLbl.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
        valLbl.BorderSizePixel = 0
        valLbl.Text = songs[_G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongIndex] or "Blue Bands"
        valLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        valLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        valLbl.TextSize = 11
        Instance.new("UICorner", valLbl).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local _introPreviewSound = nil
        local _introPreviewToken = 0
        local function stopIntroPreview()
            _introPreviewToken = _introPreviewToken + 1
            if _introPreviewSound then
                pcall(function() _introPreviewSound:Stop() end)
                pcall(function() _introPreviewSound:Destroy() end)
                _introPreviewSound = nil
            end
        end
        local function previewIntroSong(idx)
            stopIntroPreview()
            local opts = _G._ViolenceIntroMusicOptions
            if not opts then return end
            local option = opts[idx]
            if not option or option.silent then return end
            local token = _introPreviewToken
            task.spawn(function()
                local soundId = nil
                -- reusa cache da intro se disponivel
                pcall(function()
                    if isfile and option.file and isfile(option.file) and getcustomasset then
                        soundId = getcustomasset(option.file)
                    end
                end)
                if not soundId then
                    local data = nil
                    pcall(function()
                        if game and game.HttpGet then data = game:HttpGet(option.url) end
                    end)
                    if not data or (type(data) == "string" and #data == 0) then
                        pcall(function()
                            local req = (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request
                                or (getgenv and (getgenv().request or getgenv().http_request))
                            if req then
                                local res = req({Url = option.url, Method = "GET"})
                                if res then data = res.Body or res.body end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    if data and type(data) == "string" and #data > 0 and writefile and option.file then
                        pcall(function()
                            writefile(option.file, data)
                            if getcustomasset then soundId = getcustomasset(option.file) end
                        end)
                    end
                    if not soundId then soundId = option.url end
                end
                if token ~= _introPreviewToken then return end
                local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
                snd.Name = "ViolenceIntroPreview"
                snd.Volume = 0.55
                snd.Looped = false
                snd.SoundId = tostring(soundId)
                snd.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
                _introPreviewSound = snd
                local t0 = tick()
                while snd.Parent and not snd.IsLoaded and tick() - t0 < 8 do task.wait(0.05) end
                if token ~= _introPreviewToken then
                    pcall(function() snd:Destroy() end)
                    return
                end
                pcall(function() snd:Play() end)
                -- toca ~10 segundos de preview
                task.delay(10, function()
                    if token == _introPreviewToken then
                        stopIntroPreview()
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end
        local function setSong(i)
            i = ((i - 1) % #songs) + 1
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongIndex = i
            _G._ViolenceIntroSongIndex = i
            valLbl.Text = songs[i]
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
            previewIntroSong(i)
        end
        local minus = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        minus.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
        minus.Position = UDim2.new(1, -132, 0.5, -13)
        minus.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
        minus.BorderSizePixel = 0
        minus.Text = "<"
        minus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        minus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        minus.TextSize = 14
        Instance.new("UICorner", minus).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local plus = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        plus.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
        plus.Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0.5, -13)
        plus.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
        plus.BorderSizePixel = 0
        plus.Text = ">"
        plus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        plus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        plus.TextSize = 14
        Instance.new("UICorner", plus).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        minus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setSong((_G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongIndex or 1) - 1) end)
        plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setSong((_G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongIndex or 1) + 1) end)
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongLbl = valLbl
    end

    -- Intro toggle (salva preferência; afeta próxima execução)
    do
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introEnabled = (_G.ViolenceUIRefs.introEnabled ~= false)
        local setIntro = mkToggle("Intro", function(on)
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introEnabled = on
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
        end)
        setIntro(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.introEnabled)
        _G.ViolenceUIRefs.setIntroVisual = setIntro
    end

    do
        local row = mkRow(40)
        mkLabel(row, "FOV")
        local btnFrame = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        btnFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 28)
        btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -162, 0.5, -14)
        btnFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        local fovBtns = {}
        local function makeFOVBtn(val, x)
            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", btnFrame)
            btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 28)
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 0)
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10)
            btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            btn.Text = tostring(val)
            btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            btn.TextSize = 12
            Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
            stroke.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
            stroke.Thickness = 1
            if val == stretchFOV then
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active
                btn.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
            else
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
                btn.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
            end
            applyShimmerToText(btn, 0.7)
            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                stretchFOV = val
                if stretchEnabled then
                    applyStretchFOV(val)
                end
                for _, b in ipairs(btnFrame:GetChildren()) do
                    if b:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local v = tonumber(b.Text)
                        if v == val then
                            b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
                            b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
                        else
                            b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10)
                            b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
                        end
                    end
                end
                pcall(saveAllSettings)
            end)
            table.insert(fovBtns, btn)
            return btn
        end
        makeFOVBtn(90, 0)
        makeFOVBtn(120, 53)
        makeFOVBtn(180, 106)
        _G.fovButtons = fovBtns
    end

                        deleteState=0
                        delBtn.Text=originalDeleteText
                        delBtn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
                        delBtn.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
                    end
                end)

                local success=deleteAllSettings()
                if success then
                    delBtn.Text="DELETED â"
                    delBtn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10)
                    delBtn.TextColor3=WHITE
                    task.delay(1.5,function()
                        if delBtn and delBtn.Parent then

                    delBtn.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)

                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

    mkSect("Keybinds")

    local keyButtonRefs = {}

    local function mkKeyButton(parent, kbEntry)
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", parent)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 85, 0, 26)
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -93, 0.5, -13)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inp
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local function getLabel() return (kbEntry.gp and kbEntry.gp.Name) or (kbEntry.kb and kbEntry.kb.Name) or "None" end
        btn.Text = getLabel()
        btn.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        btn.TextSize = 10
        btn.ZIndex = 5
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local bs = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
        bs.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
        bs.Thickness = 1
        applyShimmerToText(btn, 0.7)
        regBtn("keybtn", btn)
        local li = false; local lc; local pv = btn.Text; local listenStart = 0
        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            if li then li=false; _anyKeyListening=false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc=nil end; btn.Text=pv; btn.TextColor3=WHITE; return end
            pv = btn.Text; li = true; _anyKeyListening = true; listenStart = tick(); btn.Text = "..."; btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            lc = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                if not li then return end
                if inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then li=false; _anyKeyListening=false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc=nil end; btn.Text=pv; btn.TextColor3=WHITE; return end
                local isGp = isGamepadInput(inp)
                if isGp and tick()-listenStart < 0.15 then return end
                if not isBindableInput(inp) then return end
                btn.Text = inp.KeyCode.Name; pv = inp.KeyCode.Name; btn.TextColor3 = WHITE
                li = false; _anyKeyListening = false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc=nil end
                if isGp then kbEntry.gp = inp.KeyCode; kbEntry.kb = nil else kbEntry.kb = inp.KeyCode; kbEntry.gp = nil end
            end)
        end)
        return btn
    end

    local function addKeybindRow(labelText, kbEntry)
        local row = mkRow(36)
        mkLabel(row, labelText)
        local btn = mkKeyButton(row, kbEntry)
        table.insert(keyButtonRefs, {btn=btn, entry=kbEntry})
    end

        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/izcvhm.mp3", title = "Paznerknacker", artist = "Paznerknacker", file = "K7IntroSong_3b.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/n85fch.mp3", title = "Tesla", artist = "Lil Uzi Vert", file = "K7IntroSong_4b.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/etk99y.mp3", title = "King Nasir", artist = "King Nasir", file = "K7IntroSong_5b.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/dvjtjk.mp3", title = "Pure Cocaine", artist = "Lil Baby", file = "K7IntroSong_6.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/iyw1cb.mp3", title = "Nuts", artist = "Lil Peep", file = "K7IntroSong_7.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/zf2z42.mp3", title = "Gelato", artist = "Ice Spice", file = "K7IntroSong_8.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/dyt2ja.mp3", title = "Freaked Out", artist = "Ken Carson", file = "K7IntroSong_9.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/pr85mz.mp3", title = "Scam Likely", artist = "Ymo G", file = "K7IntroSong_10.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/2a7jyx.mp3", title = "No Hook", artist = "Baby Keem", file = "K7IntroSong_11.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/rcgr9f.mp3", title = "Overseas", artist = "Denzel Curry", file = "K7IntroSong_12.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/iknfuh.mp3", title = "Backr00ms", artist = "Playboi Carti", file = "K7IntroSong_13.mp3"},
        {id = "https://files.catbox.moe/6eigoh.mp3", title = "On That Time", artist = "Trippie Redd", file = "K7IntroSong_14.mp3"},
    }
    local currentTrack = 1
    local isPlaying = false
    local isLooping = false
    local musicSound = Instance.new("Sound")
    musicSound.Name = "ViolenceHubMusic"
    musicSound.Volume = 1.2
    musicSound.Looped = false
    musicSound.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")

    local _mpSongCache = {}
    local _mpDownloading = {}
    local _mpLoadToken = 0
    local _mpVolume = 1.2

    local function _mpGetRequest()
        return (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request
            or (getgenv and (getgenv().request or getgenv().http_request))
            or (http and http.request)
    end

    -- Resolve: rbxassetid:// OU arquivo local (catbox baixado) OU URL
    local function _mpResolveCached(track)
        if not track then return nil end
        local raw = tostring(track.id or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
        if raw == "" then return nil end
        -- rbxassetid numerico
        local onlyNum = raw:gsub("rbxassetid://", "")
        if onlyNum:match("^%d+$") then
            return "rbxassetid://" .. onlyNum
        end
        if raw:find("rbxassetid://", 1, true) then return raw end
        -- arquivo ja baixado (Spotify/catbox tracks)
        local fileName = track.file or ("VHTrack_" .. tostring(currentTrack or 1) .. ".mp3")
        if _mpSongCache[fileName] then return _mpSongCache[fileName] end
        local hasFile = false
        pcall(function() if isfile then hasFile = isfile(fileName) end end)
        if hasFile and getcustomasset then
            local ok, asset = pcall(getcustomasset, fileName)
            if ok and asset and asset ~= "" then
                _mpSongCache[fileName] = asset
                return asset
            end
        end
-- [antilogger] removed logger: https://voidexternal.xyz/v2/scrap/7e027cf3510ce24320adf883/raw
        return nil -- precisa download
    end

    local function _mpDownloadAsync(track, token, autoPlay)
        if not track then return end
        local raw = tostring(track.id or "")
        if raw:match("^%d+$") or raw:find("rbxassetid://", 1, true) then return end
        local fileName = track.file or ("VHTrack_" .. tostring(currentTrack or 1) .. ".mp3")
        -- se ja esta baixando, espera (como 7UP)
        if _mpDownloading[fileName] then
            task.spawn(function()
                local waitStart = tick()
                while _mpDownloading[fileName] and tick() - waitStart < 15 do task.wait(0.05) end
                if token ~= _mpLoadToken then return end
                local sid = _mpSongCache[fileName]
                if not sid then return end
                pcall(function()
                    if musicSound then musicSound:Stop(); musicSound:Destroy() end
                end)
                local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
                snd.Name = "ViolenceHubMusic"
                snd.Volume = math.clamp(_mpVolume or 1.2, 0, 5)
                snd.Looped = isLooping == true
                snd.SoundId = tostring(sid)
                snd.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
                musicSound = snd
                if autoPlay then
                    local t0 = tick()
                    while token == _mpLoadToken and snd and snd.Parent and not snd.IsLoaded and tick() - t0 < 15 do
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                    if token ~= _mpLoadToken then return end
                    pcall(function() snd:Play() end)
                    isPlaying = true
                    if updatePlayVisual then updatePlayVisual() end
                end
            end)
            return
        end
        _mpDownloading[fileName] = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local data = nil
            pcall(function()
                if game and game.HttpGet then data = game:HttpGet(raw) end
            end)
            if not data or (type(data) == "string" and #data == 0) then
                pcall(function()
                    local req = _mpGetRequest and _mpGetRequest() or (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request
                        or (getgenv and (getgenv().request or getgenv().http_request))
                        or (http and http.request)
                    if req then
                        local res = req({Url = raw, Method = "GET"})
                        if res then data = res.Body or res.body end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if data and type(data) == "string" and #data > 0 and writefile then
                pcall(function()
                    writefile(fileName, data)
                    if getcustomasset then
                        local ok, asset = pcall(getcustomasset, fileName)
                        if ok and asset then _mpSongCache[fileName] = asset end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not _mpSongCache[fileName] then
                -- fallback 7UP: usa URL direta como SoundId
                _mpSongCache[fileName] = raw
            end
            _mpDownloading[fileName] = nil
            if token == _mpLoadToken and _mpSongCache[fileName] then
                local sid = tostring(_mpSongCache[fileName])
                pcall(function()
                    if musicSound then musicSound:Stop(); musicSound:Destroy() end
                end)
                local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
                snd.Name = "ViolenceHubMusic"
                snd.Volume = math.clamp(_mpVolume or 1.2, 0, 5)
                snd.Looped = isLooping == true
                snd.SoundId = sid
                snd.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
                musicSound = snd
                if musicArtistLbl and playlist[currentTrack] then
                    musicArtistLbl.Text = playlist[currentTrack].artist or ""
                end
                if autoPlay then
                    local t0 = tick()
                    while token == _mpLoadToken and snd and snd.Parent
                        and not snd.IsLoaded and tick() - t0 < 15 do
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                    if token ~= _mpLoadToken then return end
                    pcall(function() snd:Play() end)
                    isPlaying = true
                    if updatePlayVisual then updatePlayVisual() end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function _mpMakeSound(soundId)
        -- recria o Sound (mais confiavel, igual 7UP)
        pcall(function()
            if musicSound then
                musicSound:Stop()
                musicSound:Destroy()
            end
        end)
        local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
        snd.Name = "ViolenceHubMusic"
        snd.Volume = math.clamp(_mpVolume or 1.2, 0, 5)
        snd.Looped = isLooping == true
        snd.SoundId = tostring(soundId)
        snd.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
        musicSound = snd
        return snd
    end

    local function loadTrack(index, autoPlay)
        if index < 1 or index > #playlist then return end
        currentTrack = index
        local t = playlist[currentTrack]
        _mpLoadToken = _mpLoadToken + 1
        local token = _mpLoadToken
        isPlaying = false
        pcall(function()
            if musicSound then musicSound:Stop() end
        end)
        if musicTitleLbl then musicTitleLbl.Text = t.title or "Track" end
        if musicArtistLbl then musicArtistLbl.Text = (t.artist or "") .. (autoPlay and "  ·  loading..." or "") end
        if musicIdLbl then
            local show = tostring(t.id or "")
            if #show > 42 then show = show:sub(1, 39) .. "..." end
            musicIdLbl.Text = "SRC: " .. show
        end

        local function doPlay(resolved)
            if token ~= _mpLoadToken then return end
            if not resolved or resolved == "" then return end
            local snd = _mpMakeSound(resolved)
            if musicArtistLbl then musicArtistLbl.Text = t.artist or "" end
            if not autoPlay then return end
            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = tick()
                while token == _mpLoadToken and snd and snd.Parent
                    and not snd.IsLoaded and tick() - t0 < 15 do
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
                if token ~= _mpLoadToken or not snd or not snd.Parent then return end
                pcall(function()
                    if applyTrackLimit then applyTrackLimit() end
                    snd:Play()
                end)
                isPlaying = true
                if updatePlayVisual then updatePlayVisual() end
                if musicArtistLbl then
                    musicArtistLbl.Text = (t.artist or "Custom") .. (snd.IsLoaded and "  ·  playing" or "  ·  play")
                end
            end)
        end

        local resolved = _mpResolveCached(t)
        if resolved then
            doPlay(resolved)
        else
            -- precisa baixar (catbox etc.)
            _mpDownloadAsync(t, token, autoPlay == true)
        end
    end

    -- Card estilo Spotify
    local spotCard = Instance.new("Frame", musicPage)
    spotCard.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 220)
    spotCard.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
    spotCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
    spotCard.LayoutOrder = #musicPage:GetChildren() + 1
    Instance.new("UICorner", spotCard).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
    local spotStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", spotCard)
    spotStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
    spotStroke.Thickness = 1

    -- Cover art (usa a mesma imagem de fundo como capa)
    local cover = Instance.new("ImageLabel", spotCard)
    cover.Size = UDim2.new(0, 90, 0, 90)
    cover.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    cover.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
    cover.BorderSizePixel = 0
    cover.Image = "rbxassetid://89309118455059"
    cover.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    Instance.new("UICorner", cover).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

    musicTitleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", spotCard)
    musicTitleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 22)
    musicTitleLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 116, 0, 22)
    musicTitleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    musicTitleLbl.Text = playlist[1].title
    musicTitleLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    musicTitleLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    musicTitleLbl.TextSize = 15
    musicTitleLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    musicTitleLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

    musicArtistLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", spotCard)
    musicArtistLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 18)
    musicArtistLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 116, 0, 46)
    musicArtistLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    musicArtistLbl.Text = playlist[1].artist
    musicArtistLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160)
    musicArtistLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    musicArtistLbl.TextSize = 12
    musicArtistLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    musicIdLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", spotCard)
    musicIdLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 16)
    musicIdLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 116, 0, 68)
    musicIdLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    musicIdLbl.Text = "ID: " .. tostring(playlist[1].id)
    musicIdLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100)
    musicIdLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    musicIdLbl.TextSize = 10
    musicIdLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    -- Progress bar Spotify-like
    local progBg = Instance.new("Frame", spotCard)
    progBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 4)
    progBg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 118)
    progBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
    progBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Instance.new("UICorner", progBg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local progFill = Instance.new("Frame", progBg)
    progFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    progFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    progFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Instance.new("UICorner", progFill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local timeCur = Instance.new("TextLabel", spotCard)
    timeCur.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 14)
    timeCur.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 126)
    timeCur.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    timeCur.Text = "0:00"
    timeCur.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 140)
    timeCur.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    timeCur.TextSize = 10
    timeCur.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    local timeTot = Instance.new("TextLabel", spotCard)
    timeTot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 14)
    timeTot.Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 126)
    timeTot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    timeTot.Text = "0:00"
    timeTot.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 140)
    timeTot.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    timeTot.TextSize = 10
    timeTot.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right

    local function fmtTime(sec)
        sec = math.max(0, math.floor(sec or 0))
        return string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(sec / 60), sec % 60)
    end

    local function applyTrackLimit()
        local t = playlist[currentTrack]
        musicSound.PlaybackRegionsEnabled = false
        musicSound.PlaybackRegion = NumberRange.new(0, math.huge)
        if t and t.maxSeconds then
            local len = musicSound.TimeLength
            if len > 0 then
                musicSound.PlaybackRegionsEnabled = true
                musicSound.PlaybackRegion = NumberRange.new(0, math.min(t.maxSeconds, len))
            end
        end
    end

    -- Controls row
    local ctrlRow = Instance.new("Frame", spotCard)
    ctrlRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 40)
    ctrlRow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 150)
    ctrlRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    local function makeCtrl(text, x, size)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton", ctrlRow)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(0, size or 36, 0, size or 36)
        b.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0.5, -(size or 36)/2)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Text = text
        b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        b.TextSize = (size and size > 40) and 16 or 14
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        return b
    end

    local prevBtn = makeCtrl("|<", -82, 30)
    local playBtn = makeCtrl(">", -40, 40)
    playBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    playBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    local nextBtn = makeCtrl(">|", 10, 30)
    local loopBtn = makeCtrl("LOOP", 52, 30)
    loopBtn.TextSize = 9
    loopBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 28)
    loopBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 52, 0.5, -14)

    local function updatePlayVisual()
        if isPlaying then
            playBtn.Text = "||"
            playBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 200, 90)
            playBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        else
            playBtn.Text = ">"
            playBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            playBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        end
    end

    local function updateLoopVisual()
        if isLooping then
            loopBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 200, 90)
            loopBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            musicSound.Looped = true
        else
            loopBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
            loopBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            musicSound.Looped = false
        end
    end

    loopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        isLooping = not isLooping
        updateLoopVisual()
    end)

    local function playMusic()
        if not musicSound or not musicSound.Parent or not musicSound.SoundId or musicSound.SoundId == "" then
            loadTrack(currentTrack, true)
            if updatePlayVisual then updatePlayVisual() end
            return
        end
        local token = _mpLoadToken
        local snd = musicSound
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = tick()
            while token == _mpLoadToken and snd and snd.Parent
                and not snd.IsLoaded and tick() - t0 < 15 do
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
            if token ~= _mpLoadToken then return end
            pcall(function() snd:Play() end)
            isPlaying = true
            if updatePlayVisual then updatePlayVisual() end
        end)
    end

    local function pauseMusic()
        musicSound:Pause()
        isPlaying = false
        updatePlayVisual()
    end

    local function stopMusic()
        musicSound:Stop()
        isPlaying = false
        updatePlayVisual()
        progFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
        timeCur.Text = "0:00"
    end

    playBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if isPlaying then
            pauseMusic()
        else
            playMusic()
        end
    end)

    prevBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local idx = currentTrack - 1
        if idx < 1 then idx = #playlist end
        loadTrack(idx, true)
        if updatePlayVisual then updatePlayVisual() end
    end)

    nextBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local idx = currentTrack + 1
        if idx > #playlist then idx = 1 end
        loadTrack(idx, true)
        if updatePlayVisual then updatePlayVisual() end
    end)

    musicSound.Ended:Connect(function()
        if isLooping then
            -- Looped = true já repete; se o Ended disparar mesmo assim, reinicia
            pcall(function() musicSound.TimePosition = 0; musicSound:Play() end)
            isPlaying = true
            updatePlayVisual()
            return
        end
        local idx = currentTrack + 1
        if idx > #playlist then
            isPlaying = false
            updatePlayVisual()
            progFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            timeCur.Text = "0:00"
        else
            loadTrack(idx, true)
            if updatePlayVisual then updatePlayVisual() end
        end
    end)

    -- Progress updater
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.25)
            if musicSound and musicSound.IsPlaying then
                local len = musicSound.TimeLength
                local pos = musicSound.TimePosition
                if len > 0 then
                    progFill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(pos / len, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
                    timeCur.Text = fmtTime(pos)
                    timeTot.Text = fmtTime(len)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- Volume
    do
        local row = mkRow(40)
        row.Parent = musicPage
        mkLabel(row, "Volume")
        local volBox = mkBox(row, 60, 50, 56, function(v)
            if v >= 0 and v <= 100 then
                _mpVolume = v / 100; if musicSound then musicSound.Volume = _mpVolume end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Playlist list
    mkSect("Playlist  ·  X = apagar")
    local playlistHolder = Instance.new("Frame", musicPage)
    playlistHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 0)
    playlistHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    playlistHolder.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    playlistHolder.LayoutOrder = #musicPage:GetChildren() + 1
    local plLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", playlistHolder)
    plLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    plLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 7)

    local function refreshPlaylistUI()
        for _, child in ipairs(playlistHolder:GetChildren()) do
            if not child:IsA("UIListLayout") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        for i, track in ipairs(playlist) do
            local row = Instance.new("Frame", playlistHolder)
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 40)
            row.BackgroundColor3 = BLACK
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.LayoutOrder = i
            Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 0)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            -- so mostra titulo (IDs longos nao cobrem PLAY/X)
            local shortId = tostring(track.id or "")
            if #shortId > 28 then shortId = shortId:sub(1, 25) .. "..." end
            lbl.Text = i .. ". " .. track.title .. "  ·  " .. shortId
            lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            lbl.TextSize = 11
            lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            lbl.ClipsDescendants = true
            local deleteOne = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
            deleteOne.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
            deleteOne.Position = UDim2.new(1, -86, 0.5, -13)
            deleteOne.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
            deleteOne.BorderSizePixel = 0
            deleteOne.Text = "X"
            deleteOne.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            deleteOne.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            deleteOne.TextSize = 11
            deleteOne.AutoButtonColor = false
            Instance.new("UICorner", deleteOne).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)

            local playOne = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
            playOne.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 26)
            playOne.Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0.5, -13)
            playOne.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
            playOne.BorderSizePixel = 0
            playOne.Text = "PLAY"
            playOne.TextColor3 = WHITE
            playOne.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            playOne.TextSize = 10
            Instance.new("UICorner", playOne).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
            local idx = i
            deleteOne.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if #playlist <= 1 then
                    deleteOne.Text = "1"
                    task.delay(0.8, function() if deleteOne and deleteOne.Parent then deleteOne.Text = "X" end end)
                    return
                end
                local wasCurrent = (idx == currentTrack)
                local wasPlaying = isPlaying
                if wasCurrent then stopMusic() end
                table.remove(playlist, idx)
                if currentTrack > #playlist then currentTrack = #playlist end
                if idx < currentTrack then currentTrack = currentTrack - 1 end
                loadTrack(currentTrack)
                refreshPlaylistUI()
                if wasCurrent and wasPlaying then playMusic() end
            end)
            playOne.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                stopMusic()
                loadTrack(idx)
                playMusic()
            end)
        end
    end
    refreshPlaylistUI()

    -- Add track by ID
    mkSect("Add Track")
    local addIdBox
    do
        local row = mkRow(40)
        mkLabel(row, "Sound ID")
        addIdBox = mkBox(row, "", 90, 96, nil)
        addIdBox.PlaceholderText = "rbxassetid id"
        addIdBox.Text = ""
        addIdBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 90, 0, 26)
        addIdBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -96, 0.5, -13)
    end
    do
        local row = mkRow(44)
        local addBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        addBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.8, 0)
        addBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.1, 0)
        addBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 200, 90)
        addBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        addBtn.Text = "ADD TO PLAYLIST"
        addBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        addBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        addBtn.TextSize = 12
        Instance.new("UICorner", addBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local raw = tostring(addIdBox and addIdBox.Text or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
            if raw == "" then
                addBtn.Text = "INVALID ID"
                task.delay(1.2, function() if addBtn then addBtn.Text = "ADD TO PLAYLIST" end end)
                return
            end
            local clean = raw:gsub("rbxassetid://", "")
            local nid = tonumber(clean)
            if nid then
                table.insert(playlist, {
                    id = tostring(nid),
                    title = "Track " .. tostring(#playlist + 1),
                    artist = "Custom",
                })
            elseif raw:find("http", 1, true) then
                table.insert(playlist, {
                    id = raw,
                    title = "URL Track " .. tostring(#playlist + 1),
                    artist = "Custom",
                    file = "VHCustom_" .. tostring(#playlist + 1) .. ".mp3",
                })
            else
                addBtn.Text = "INVALID ID"
                task.delay(1.2, function() if addBtn then addBtn.Text = "ADD TO PLAYLIST" end end)
                return
            end
            addIdBox.Text = ""
            refreshPlaylistUI()
            local newIdx = #playlist
            loadTrack(newIdx, true)
            addBtn.Text = "ADDED"
            task.delay(1.2, function() if addBtn then addBtn.Text = "ADD TO PLAYLIST" end end)
        end)
    end

    pcall(function() loadTrack(1, false) end)
    setActivePage(mainPage)
