    local function dragProgress(f)
        local dn,ds,sp,di = false
        f.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if uiLocked then return end
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dn = true
                ds = i.Position
                sp = f.Position
                i.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dn = false end
                end)
            end
        end)
        f.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                di = i
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if i == di and dn then
                if uiLocked then dn = false; return end
                local nX = sp.X.Offset + (i.Position.X - ds.X)
                local nY = sp.Y.Offset + (i.Position.Y - ds.Y)
                f.Position = UDim2.new(sp.X.Scale, nX, sp.Y.Scale, nY)
                savedProgressBarPos = {
                    XScale = f.Position.X.Scale,
                    XOffset = f.Position.X.Offset,
                    YScale = f.Position.Y.Scale,
                    YOffset = f.Position.Y.Offset
                }
                pcall(saveAllSettings)
            end
        end)
    end
    dragProgress(pbFrame)

    -- Aplica escala salva da steal bar assim que a barra existe
    do
        local v = tonumber(_G.ViolenceUIRefs and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealBarScale) or 100
        if pbFrame then
            local baseW, baseH = 320, 42
            pbFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(baseW * v / 100), 0, math.floor(baseH * v / 100))
        end
    end

    function updateProgressBarVisibility()
        if pbFrame then
            pbFrame.Visible = Steal.AutoStealEnabled
        end
    end

        local _pbState = "IDLE"
    local function setBarState(state, distance)
        -- durante Semi ativo, deixa o setStealProgress controlar o texto/%
        if _G.ViolenceSemiState and _G.ViolenceSemiState.active and state ~= "STEALING" and state ~= "WAITING RANGE" and state ~= "SUCCESS" and state ~= "FAILED" then
            return
        end
        if state == _pbState and state ~= "READY" and state ~= "WAITING RANGE" and state ~= "STEALING" then return end
        _pbState = state
        if state == "STEALING" then
            if stealLbl then
                stealLbl.Text = (Steal.StealMode == "Semi") and "SEMI" or "STEAL"
                TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)}):Play()
            end
            if fillRegion then
                TS:Create(fillRegion, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)}):Play()
            end
            if progressPct then progressPct.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255) end
        elseif state == "WAITING RANGE" then
            if stealLbl then
                stealLbl.Text = "RANGE"
                TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120)}):Play()
            end
            if fillRegion then
                TS:Create(fillRegion, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 22, 10)}):Play()
            end
            if progressPct then progressPct.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 150) end
        elseif state == "SUCCESS" then
            if stealLbl then
                stealLbl.Text = "OK"
                TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 255, 160)}):Play()
            end
            if fillRegion then
                TS:Create(fillRegion, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 30, 18)}):Play()
            end
            if progressPct then
                progressPct.Text = "OK"
                progressPct.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 255, 170)
            end
        elseif state == "FAILED" then
            if stealLbl then
                stealLbl.Text = "MISS"
                TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120)}):Play()
            end
            if fillRegion then
                TS:Create(fillRegion, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 12, 12)}):Play()
            end
            if progressPct then
                progressPct.Text = "MISS"
                progressPct.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 140)
            end
        elseif state == "READY" then
            if stealLbl then
                stealLbl.Text = (Steal.StealMode == "Semi") and "SEMI" or "STEAL"
                TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)}):Play()
            end
            if fillRegion then
                TS:Create(fillRegion, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)}):Play()
            end
            if progressPct and not isStealing then
                progressPct.Text = distance and (math.floor(distance).."m") or "READY"
                progressPct.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            end
        else
            if stealLbl then
                stealLbl.Text = "STEAL"
                TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160)}):Play()
            end
            if fillRegion then
                TS:Create(fillRegion, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = BLACK}):Play()
            end
            if progressPct and not isStealing then
                progressPct.Text = "—"
                progressPct.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160)
            end
        end
    end

    _G.ViolenceStealBar = {
        SetProgress = function(p)
            _G.setStealProgress(p)
        end,
        SetState = function(state, extra)
            setBarState(state, extra)
        end,
        Reset = function()
            resetProgressBar()
            setBarState(Steal.AutoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
        end,
    }

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.15) do
            -- Semi ativo: setStealProgress já atualiza a barra; não sobrescreve
            if _G.ViolenceSemiState and _G.ViolenceSemiState.active then
                -- noop visual controlado pelo executeSemiSteal
            elseif isStealing then
                setBarState("STEALING")
            else
                local p, d = findNearestPrompt()
                if p then
                    setBarState("READY", d)
                else
                    setBarState("IDLE")
                end
            end
        end
    end)
task.spawn(function()
        local lastFrame = tick()
        local fpsSamples = {}
        local fpsAvg = 60
        RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            local now = tick()
            local dt = now - lastFrame
            lastFrame = now
            if dt > 0 then
                table.insert(fpsSamples, 1 / dt)
                if #fpsSamples > 30 then table.remove(fpsSamples, 1) end
                local sum = 0
                for _, v in ipairs(fpsSamples) do sum = sum + v end
                fpsAvg = sum / #fpsSamples
            end
        end)
        while true do
            local ping = 0
            pcall(function()
                ping = LP:GetNetworkPing() * 1000
            end)
            local fpsTxt = string.format("FPS: %d", math.floor(fpsAvg + 0.5))
            local pingTxt = string.format("PING: %dms", math.floor(ping + 0.5))
            if _G.ViolenceUIRefs and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealFpsLbl then
                _G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealFpsLbl.Text = fpsTxt
            end
            if progressRadLbl then
                progressRadLbl.Text = pingTxt
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)

    local function drag(f)
        local dn,ds,sp,di=false
        f.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if uiLocked then return end
            if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dn=true; ds=i.Position; sp=f.Position
                i.Changed:Connect(function() if i.UserInputState==Enum.UserInputState.End then dn=false end end)
            end
        end)
        f.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then di=i end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if i==di and dn then
                if uiLocked then dn=false; return end
                local nX=sp.X.Offset+(i.Position.X-ds.X)
                local nY=sp.Y.Offset+(i.Position.Y-ds.Y)
                f.Position=UDim2.new(sp.X.Scale,nX,sp.Y.Scale,nY)
            end
        end)
    end
    drag(main)

    if savedProgressBarPos then
        pbFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
            savedProgressBarPos.XScale or 0.5,
            savedProgressBarPos.XOffset or -150,
            savedProgressBarPos.YScale or 1,
            savedProgressBarPos.YOffset or -58
        )
    end
end

local function updateUIFromLoaded()
    task.wait()
    if normalBox then normalBox.Text=tostring(NS) end
    if carryBox then carryBox.Text=tostring(CS) end
    if radInput then radInput.Text=tostring(Steal.StealRadius) end
    if stealDurationBox then stealDurationBox.Text=tostring(Steal.StealDuration) end
    if laggerBox then laggerBox.Text=tostring(LAGGER_SPEED_1) end
    if lagger2Box then lagger2Box.Text=tostring(LAGGER_SPEED_2) end
    if autoTPHeightBox then autoTPHeightBox.Text=tostring(autoTPHeight) end
    if batSpeedBox then batSpeedBox.Text = tostring(BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED) end
    if bypassSpeedBox then bypassSpeedBox.Text = tostring(BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED) end
    -- Restaura posição da barra STEAL
    if pbFrame and savedProgressBarPos then
        pbFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
            savedProgressBarPos.XScale or 0.5,
            savedProgressBarPos.XOffset or -150,
            savedProgressBarPos.YScale or 1,
            savedProgressBarPos.YOffset or -58
        )
    end
    if dropModeBtnRef then
        dropModeBtnRef.Text = dropMode == 1 and "V1" or "V2"
    end
    if tpModeSelectBtn then
        tpModeSelectBtn.Text = tpDownMode == 1 and "V1" or "V2"
    end
    refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    if _G.ViolenceSky.theme and _G.ViolenceSky.presets[_G.ViolenceSky.theme] then
        pcall(_G.applyCustomSky, _G.ViolenceSky.theme)
        if _G.ViolenceSky.btn then _G.ViolenceSky.btn.Text = "SKY: " .. _G.ViolenceSky.theme end
        if _G.skyThemeBtn then _G.skyThemeBtn.Text = "SKY: " .. _G.ViolenceSky.theme end
    end
    if _G.ViolenceBtnColor and _G.ViolenceBtnColor.name then
        pcall(_G.ViolenceBtnColor.apply, _G.ViolenceBtnColor.name)
    end
    if _G.ViolenceUIRefs then
        if _G.ViolenceUIRefs.applyUIScale and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.uiScale then
            pcall(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.applyUIScale, _G.ViolenceUIRefs.uiScale)
        end
        if _G.ViolenceUIRefs.applyStealBarScale and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealBarScale then
            pcall(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.applyStealBarScale, _G.ViolenceUIRefs.stealBarScale)
        end
        if _G.ViolenceUIRefs.setIntroVisual then
            pcall(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.setIntroVisual, _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introEnabled ~= false)
        end
        if _G.ViolenceUIRefs.applyBackground and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgIndex then
            pcall(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.applyBackground, _G.ViolenceUIRefs.selectedBgIndex)
        end
        if _G.ViolenceUIRefs.setHeadlessVisual and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.headlessEnabled then
            pcall(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.setHeadlessVisual, true)
        end
        if _G.ViolenceUIRefs.setKorbloxVisual and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.korbloxEnabled then
            pcall(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.setKorbloxVisual, true)
        end
        if _G.ViolenceUIRefs.reapplyAllSkins then
            task.delay(0.4, function()
                pcall(_G.ViolenceUIRefs.reapplyAllSkins, LP.Character)
            end)
        end
        if _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongLbl and _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongIndex then
            local songs = {"Blue Bands", "Legacy", "Paznerknacker", "Tesla", "King Nasir", "Pure Cocaine", "Nuts", "Gelato", "Freaked Out", "Scam Likely", "No Hook", "Overseas", "Backr00ms", "On That Time", "NO MUSIC"}
            _G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongLbl.Text = songs[_G.ViolenceUIRefs.introSongIndex] or "Blue Bands"
        end
    end
    -- Restaura ESP / Ragdoll Timer / Melee a partir do save
    if playerESPSetVisual then playerESPSetVisual(_G.ViolenceESP.playerESP == true) end
    if setTracerESPVisual then setTracerESPVisual(_G.ViolenceESP.tracerESP == true) end
    if highlightESPSetVisual then highlightESPSetVisual(_G.ViolenceESP.highlightESP == true) end
    -- reaplica de verdade (visual do toggle nao chama o callback)
    pcall(function()
        _G.ViolenceESP.setPlayerESP(_G.ViolenceESP.playerESP == true)
        _G.ViolenceESP.setTracerESP(_G.ViolenceESP.tracerESP == true)
        _G.ViolenceESP.setHighlight(_G.ViolenceESP.highlightESP == true)
    end)
    if setAutoCarryVisual then setAutoCarryVisual(_G.ViolenceAutoCarry and _G.ViolenceAutoCarry.enabled) end
    if _G.ViolenceNino and _G.ViolenceNino.setVisual then _G.ViolenceNino.setVisual(_G.ViolenceNino.enabled == true) end
    pcall(function() if _G.ViolenceNino then _G.ViolenceNino.setEnabled(_G.ViolenceNino.enabled == true) end end)
    if _G.ViolenceMelee and _G.ViolenceMelee.setVisual then _G.ViolenceMelee.setVisual(_G.ViolenceMelee.enabled == true) end
    if _G.ViolenceMelee and _G.ViolenceMelee.enabled then pcall(_G.ViolenceMelee.start) else pcall(_G.ViolenceMelee.stop) end
    if _G.ViolenceAntiDrop.enabled then
        if _G.ViolenceAntiDrop.setVisual then _G.ViolenceAntiDrop.setVisual(true) end
        pcall(_G.ViolenceAntiDrop.startAntiDrop)
    end
    if uiLocked and setLockUIVisual then setLockUIVisual(true) end
    if antiRagdollEnabled and setAntiRagVisual then setAntiRagVisual(true); startAntiRagdoll() end
    if stealModeBtnRef then
        stealModeBtnRef.Text = Steal.StealMode == "Semi" and "Semi" or "Normal"
    end
    if Steal.AutoStealEnabled and setInstaGrab then setInstaGrab(true); pcall(startAutoSteal) end
    if jumpEnabled then
        if setJumpVisual then setJumpVisual(true) end
        startJumpMode()
    else
        if setJumpVisual then setJumpVisual(false) end
    end

    if medusaCounterEnabled then
        if setMedusaVisual then setMedusaVisual(true) end
        if LP.Character then setupMedusa(LP.Character) end
    else
        if setMedusaVisual then setMedusaVisual(false) end
        stopMedusaCounter()
    end

    if batCounterEnabled and setBatCounterVisual then
        setBatCounterVisual(true)
        startBatCounter()
    end
    if autoTPDownEnabled then if setAutoTPDownVisual then setAutoTPDownVisual(true) end; startAutoTPDown() end
    if autoBatEnabled and autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(true); enableAutoBat() end
    if autoLeftEnabled and autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(true) end
    if autoRightEnabled and autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(true) end
    if unwalkEnabled and setUnwalkVisual then setUnwalkVisual(true); task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.5); startUnwalk() end) end
    if antiDieEnabled then
        if setAntiDieVisual then pcall(setAntiDieVisual, true) end
        task.delay(0.5, function() pcall(startAntiDieFling) end)
    end
    if antiLagEnabled and setAntiLagVisual then enableAntiLag(); setAntiLagVisual(true) end

    if stretchEnabled then
        enableStretch()
        if _G.stretchToggleSetter then _G.stretchToggleSetter(true) end
    else
        if _G.stretchToggleSetter then _G.stretchToggleSetter(false) end
    end
    if _G.fovButtons then
        for _, btn in ipairs(_G.fovButtons) do
            local val = tonumber(btn.Text)
            if val == stretchFOV then
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active
                btn.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
            else
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
                btn.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
            end
        end
    end

    if _G.keyButtonRefs then
        for _, ref in ipairs(_G.keyButtonRefs) do
            local entry = ref.entry
            local label = (entry.gp and entry.gp.Name) or (entry.kb and entry.kb.Name) or "None"
            ref.btn.Text = label
        end
    end

    if modeSelectBtn then
        modeSelectBtn.Text = jumpMode == 1 and "Tap Tap" or "Hold"
    end

    if mobSetAutoBat then mobSetAutoBat(autoBatEnabled) end
    if mobSetAutoLeft then mobSetAutoLeft(autoLeftEnabled) end
    if mobSetAutoRight then mobSetAutoRight(autoRightEnabled) end
    if mobSetCarry then mobSetCarry(speedMode) end
    if mobSetLagger1 then mobSetLagger1(laggerToggled and laggerLevel==1) end
    if mobSetLagger2 then mobSetLagger2(laggerToggled and laggerLevel==2) end

    if selectedAnimPack and AnimationPacks[selectedAnimPack] then
        if _G.animPackBtn then
            _G.animPackBtn.Text = "ANIM: " .. selectedAnimPack
        end
        if LP.Character then
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(0.3)
                pcall(applyAnimationPack, selectedAnimPack)
            end)
        end
    end

    updateProgressBarVisibility()
    startEnemySpeed()
end

local function createMobilePanel()
    local panel = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    panel.Name = "ViolenceHubMobilePanel"
    panel.ResetOnSpawn = false
    panel.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    pcall(function() if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(panel) end end)
    if not pcall(function() panel.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) then
        panel.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end

    local BTN_W, BTN_H = 60, 60
    local GAP = 8
    local COLUMNS = 2
    local ROWS = 4
    local PANEL_W = BTN_W * COLUMNS + GAP * (COLUMNS - 1)
    local PANEL_H = BTN_H * ROWS + (GAP + 10) * (ROWS - 1)

    local container = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
    container.Name = "FloatingPanel"
    container.Size = UDim2.new(0, PANEL_W, 0, PANEL_H)
    container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -PANEL_W - 10, 0, 0)
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    container.BorderSizePixel = 0
    container.Active = true
    container.Selectable = true

    local btnContainer = Instance.new("Frame", container)
    btnContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    btnContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    local grid = Instance.new("UIGridLayout", btnContainer)
    grid.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, BTN_W, 0, BTN_H)
    grid.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, GAP, 0, GAP + 10)
    grid.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    grid.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    grid.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
    grid.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top

    local ACCENT = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
    local INACTIVE_BG = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
    local INACTIVE_TEXT = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
    local STROKE_COLOR = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
    local ACTIVE_BG = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active
    local ACTIVE_TEXT = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text

    local buttons = {}

    local function createButton(name, text, order, isToggle, callback)
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", btnContainer)
        btn.Name = name
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, BTN_W, 0, BTN_H)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = INACTIVE_BG
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Text = ""
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.LayoutOrder = order
        btn.ClipsDescendants = true -- evita pontas da foto saindo do canto
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 18)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
        stroke.Color = STROKE_COLOR
        stroke.Thickness = 1.2
        stroke.Transparency = 0.4
        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4)
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = text
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        label.TextTransparency = 0
        label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        label.TextSize = 11
        label.TextWrapped = true
        label.ZIndex = 50
        _G.ViolenceMobileImg.attach(btn, UDim.new(0, 18))
        label.ZIndex = 50
        pcall(function() _G.ViolenceBindFloatPressStroke(btn, stroke) end)

        local active = false
        local function setActive(state, level)
            active = state and true or false
            btn:SetAttribute("ViolenceFloatActive", active)
            local C = _G.ViolenceBtnColor
            if active then
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = C.active
                stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                stroke.Transparency = 0
                stroke.Thickness = 2.2
            else
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = C.inactive
                stroke.Color = C.stroke
                stroke.Transparency = 0.35
                stroke.Thickness = 1.5
            end
            pcall(function() _G.ViolenceShowFloatRing(btn, active) end)
            label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            label.TextTransparency = 0
            label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
            label.ZIndex = math.max((btn.ZIndex or 1) + 10, 50)
            label.Visible = true
            local img = btn:FindFirstChild("MobileBackgroundImage")
            local hasImg = _G.ViolenceMobileImg.normalize(_G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage) ~= ""
            if img then
                img.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                img.ImageTransparency = 0
                img.Visible = hasImg
                img.ZIndex = math.max((btn.ZIndex or 1) + 1, 2)
            end
            if hasImg then
                btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1 -- sem overlay escuro
            else
                btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            end
            if buttons[name] then buttons[name].active = active end
        end
        -- Lógica VisDuels: clique vs arraste pelo InputEnded (melhor no mobile)
        local dragging = false
        local hasMoved = false
        local dragStart = nil
        local startPos = nil
        local movedDistance = 0

        local function onInputBegan(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                hasMoved = false
                movedDistance = 0
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = btn.Position
            end
        end

        local function onInputChanged(input)
            if not dragging then return end
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            movedDistance = delta.Magnitude
            -- arrasta só com UI desbloqueada (Lock UI off)
            if not uiLocked and movedDistance > 3 then
                hasMoved = true
                btn.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                )
            end
        end

        local function onInputEnded(input)
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            if not dragging then return end
            if movedDistance < 3 then
                -- clique real
                if callback then
                    if isToggle then
                        callback(setActive, nil)
                    else
                        callback(setActive, active)
                    end
                end
            elseif hasMoved and not uiLocked then
                -- salvou posição ao soltar
                if type(savedMobilePanelPos) ~= "table" then savedMobilePanelPos = {} end
                savedMobilePanelPos.buttons = savedMobilePanelPos.buttons or {}
                savedMobilePanelPos.buttons[name] = {
                    XScale = btn.Position.X.Scale,
                    XOffset = btn.Position.X.Offset,
                    YScale = btn.Position.Y.Scale,
                    YOffset = btn.Position.Y.Offset,
                }
                pcall(saveAllSettings)
            end
            dragging = false
            hasMoved = false
            dragStart = nil
            startPos = nil
            movedDistance = 0
        end

        btn.InputBegan:Connect(onInputBegan)
        btn.InputChanged:Connect(onInputChanged)
        btn.InputEnded:Connect(onInputEnded)

        buttons[name] = {btn=btn, setActive=setActive, label=label, active=false, stroke=stroke}
        return setActive
    end

    mobSetDropBR = createButton("DropBR", "DROP\nBR", 0, true, function(setActive, _)
        -- Botão SEMPRE funciona (não trava por Bat V1 nem Anti Drop)
        setActive(true)
        executeDropWithToggle(function(v)
            if dropBrainrotSetVisual then dropBrainrotSetVisual(v) end
        end)
        task.delay(0.3, function() setActive(false) end)
    end)
    mobSetAutoLeft = createButton("AutoLeft", "AUTO\nLEFT", 1, true, function(setActive, _)
        autoLeftEnabled = not autoLeftEnabled
        setActive(autoLeftEnabled)
        if autoLeftEnabled then startAutoLeft() else stopAutoLeft() end
        if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(autoLeftEnabled) end
        pcall(saveAllSettings)
    end)
    mobSetAutoBat = createButton("AutoBat", "BAT\nV1", 2, true, function(setActive, _)
        if not autoBatEnabled then enableAutoBat() else disableAutoBat() end
        setActive(autoBatEnabled)
        pcall(saveAllSettings)
    end)
    mobSetAutoRight = createButton("AutoRight", "AUTO\nRIGHT", 3, true, function(setActive, _)
        autoRightEnabled = not autoRightEnabled
        setActive(autoRightEnabled)
        if autoRightEnabled then startAutoRight() else stopAutoRight() end
        if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(autoRightEnabled) end
        pcall(saveAllSettings)
    end)
    mobSetTpDown = createButton("TpDown", "TP\nDOWN", 4, true, function(setActive, _)
        executeTPDown()
        setActive(true)
        task.delay(0.2, function() setActive(false) end)
    end)
    mobSetCarry = createButton("Carry", "CARRY\nSPD", 5, true, function(setActive, _)
        if not speedMode then
            speedMode=true; laggerToggled=false; laggerLevel=1; setActive(true)
            if buttons.Lagger1 and buttons.Lagger1.setActive then buttons.Lagger1.setActive(false) end
            if buttons.Lagger2 and buttons.Lagger2.setActive then buttons.Lagger2.setActive(false) end
        else
            speedMode=false; setActive(false)
        end
        refreshSpeedModeLabel()
        pcall(saveAllSettings)
    end)
    mobSetLagger1 = createButton("Lagger1", "LAGGER\nNORMAL", 6, true, function(setActive, _)
        if speedMode then speedMode=false; if mobSetCarry then mobSetCarry(false) end end
        if not laggerToggled or laggerLevel ~= 1 then
            laggerToggled = true; laggerLevel = 1; setActive(true)
            if buttons.Lagger2 and buttons.Lagger2.setActive then buttons.Lagger2.setActive(false) end
        else
            laggerToggled = false; laggerLevel = 1; setActive(false)
        end
        refreshSpeedModeLabel()
        pcall(saveAllSettings)
    end)
    mobSetLagger2 = createButton("Lagger2", "LAGGER\nCARY", 7, true, function(setActive, _)
        if speedMode then speedMode=false; if mobSetCarry then mobSetCarry(false) end end
        if not laggerToggled or laggerLevel ~= 2 then
            laggerToggled = true; laggerLevel = 2; setActive(true)
            if buttons.Lagger1 and buttons.Lagger1.setActive then buttons.Lagger1.setActive(false) end
        else
            laggerToggled = false; laggerLevel = 1; setActive(false)
        end
        refreshSpeedModeLabel()
        pcall(saveAllSettings)
    end)

    _G.ViolenceMobileButtons = buttons

    if buttons.AutoBat then buttons.AutoBat.setActive(autoBatEnabled) end
    if buttons.AutoLeft then buttons.AutoLeft.setActive(autoLeftEnabled) end
    if buttons.AutoRight then buttons.AutoRight.setActive(autoRightEnabled) end
    if buttons.Carry then buttons.Carry.setActive(speedMode) end
    -- Garante botão Carry Speed sempre presente
    if not buttons.Carry then
        mobSetCarry = createButton("Carry", "CARRY\nSPD", 5, true, function(setActive, _)
            if not speedMode then
                speedMode=true; laggerToggled=false; laggerLevel=1; setActive(true)
                if buttons.Lagger1 and buttons.Lagger1.setActive then buttons.Lagger1.setActive(false) end
                if buttons.Lagger2 and buttons.Lagger2.setActive then buttons.Lagger2.setActive(false) end
            else
                speedMode=false; setActive(false)
            end
            refreshSpeedModeLabel()
        end)
        if buttons.Carry then buttons.Carry.setActive(speedMode) end
    end
    if buttons.Lagger1 then buttons.Lagger1.setActive(laggerToggled and laggerLevel==1) end
    if buttons.Lagger2 then buttons.Lagger2.setActive(laggerToggled and laggerLevel==2) end

    if savedMobilePanelPos then
        container.Position = UDim2.new(
            savedMobilePanelPos.XScale or 1,
            savedMobilePanelPos.XOffset or (-PANEL_W - 10),
            savedMobilePanelPos.YScale or 0,
            savedMobilePanelPos.YOffset or 0
        )
    end

    -- Cada botão do painel flutuante move de forma independente (não o container inteiro)
    -- Desliga o grid e posiciona cada botão em coordenadas absolutas dentro do ScreenGui
    grid:Destroy()
    btnContainer.Visible = false
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    container.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

    local baseX = container.AbsolutePosition.X
    local baseY = container.AbsolutePosition.Y
    -- fallback se AbsolutePosition ainda for 0
    if baseX == 0 and baseY == 0 then
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vw = cam and cam.ViewportSize.X or 800
        baseX = vw - PANEL_W - 10
        baseY = 0
    end

    -- Posições salvas corrompidas (ex: Align antigo) são ignoradas
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local vw = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.X) or 800
    local vh = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.Y) or 600

    local function isPosOnScreen(sp)
        if type(sp) ~= "table" or sp.XOffset == nil or sp.YOffset == nil then return false end
        local x = (sp.XScale or 0) * vw + (sp.XOffset or 0)
        local y = (sp.YScale or 0) * vh + (sp.YOffset or 0)
        -- precisa estar visível na tela (margem pequena)
        if x < -BTN_W or y < -BTN_H then return false end
        if x > vw - 8 or y > vh - 8 then return false end
        return true
    end

    local REQUIRED_BTNS = {"DropBR", "AutoLeft", "AutoBat", "AutoRight", "TpDown", "Carry", "Lagger1", "Lagger2"}
    local savedBtnPos = (type(savedMobilePanelPos) == "table" and savedMobilePanelPos.buttons) or {}
    -- Só usa posições salvas se TODOS os 8 botões (inclui Carry + Lagger Cary) estiverem válidos na tela
    local allValid = true
    for _, reqName in ipairs(REQUIRED_BTNS) do
        local sp = savedBtnPos[reqName]
        if not (sp and isPosOnScreen(sp)) then
            allValid = false
            break
        end
    end
    if not allValid then
        savedBtnPos = {}
        if type(savedMobilePanelPos) == "table" then
            savedMobilePanelPos.buttons = nil
        end
    end

    local orderList = {}
    for name, data in pairs(buttons) do
        table.insert(orderList, {name = name, data = data, order = data.btn.LayoutOrder or 0})
    end
    table.sort(orderList, function(a, b) return a.order < b.order end)

    -- Estado de drag compartilhado: só um botão por vez
    local activeDrag = nil

    -- baseX/baseY sempre no canto direito (grade limpa)
    do
        local camR = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vwR = (camR and camR.ViewportSize.X) or 800
        baseX = vwR - PANEL_W - 10
        baseY = 12
    end

    for i, entry in ipairs(orderList) do
        local btn = entry.data.btn
        local btnName = entry.name
        btn.Parent = panel
        btn.Visible = true
        btn.Active = true
        local col = (i - 1) % COLUMNS
        local row = math.floor((i - 1) / COLUMNS)
        local defX = baseX + col * (BTN_W + GAP)
        local defY = baseY + row * (BTN_H + GAP + 10)
        local sp = savedBtnPos[btnName]
        if allValid and sp and isPosOnScreen(sp) then
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(sp.XScale or 0, sp.XOffset, sp.YScale or 0, sp.YOffset)
        else
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, defX, 0, defY)
        end
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, BTN_W, 0, BTN_H)
        btn.ZIndex = 20
        -- foto abaixo, texto por cima (sempre legível)
        local img = btn:FindFirstChild("MobileBackgroundImage")
        if img then img.ZIndex = 21 end
        local lbl = btn:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
        if lbl then
            lbl.ZIndex = 30
            lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            lbl.TextTransparency = 0
            lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
            lbl.Visible = true
        end

        -- drag/clique já tratado no createButton (estilo VisDuels)
    end

    -- Força Carry + Lagger Cary visíveis (nunca esconder)
    if buttons.Carry and buttons.Carry.btn then
        buttons.Carry.btn.Visible = true
        buttons.Carry.btn.ZIndex = 20
    end
    if buttons.Lagger2 and buttons.Lagger2.btn then
        buttons.Lagger2.btn.Visible = true
        buttons.Lagger2.btn.ZIndex = 20
    end

    -- Garante que nenhum botão ficou invisível / fora da tela
    task.defer(function()
        local cam2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vw2 = (cam2 and cam2.ViewportSize.X) or 800
        local vh2 = (cam2 and cam2.ViewportSize.Y) or 600
        local needReset = false
        for _, data in pairs(buttons) do
            local b = data.btn
            if not b or not b.Parent then
                needReset = true
                break
            end
            local p = b.AbsolutePosition
            if p.X < -80 or p.Y < -80 or p.X > vw2 - 8 or p.Y > vh2 - 8 then
                needReset = true
                break
            end
            b.Visible = true
        end
        if needReset then
            local bx = vw2 - PANEL_W - 10
            local by = 12
            local sorted = {}
            for name, data in pairs(buttons) do
                table.insert(sorted, {name = name, data = data, order = data.btn and data.btn.LayoutOrder or 0})
            end
            table.sort(sorted, function(a, b) return a.order < b.order end)
            for i, e in ipairs(sorted) do
                local col = (i - 1) % COLUMNS
                local row = math.floor((i - 1) / COLUMNS)
                if e.data.btn then
                    e.data.btn.Visible = true
                    e.data.btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, bx + col * (BTN_W + GAP), 0, by + row * (BTN_H + GAP + 10))
                end
            end
            if type(savedMobilePanelPos) == "table" then
                savedMobilePanelPos.buttons = nil
            end
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
        end
    end)

    local function getActionButtons()
        local list = {}
        for name, data in pairs(buttons) do
            if data.btn and data.btn.Parent then
                table.insert(list, {name = name, btn = data.btn, order = data.btn.LayoutOrder or 0})
            end
        end
        table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.order < b.order end)
        return list
    end

    local function saveAllBtnPositions()
        if type(savedMobilePanelPos) ~= "table" then savedMobilePanelPos = {} end
        savedMobilePanelPos.buttons = savedMobilePanelPos.buttons or {}
        for name, data in pairs(buttons) do
            local b = data.btn
            if b and b.Parent then
                savedMobilePanelPos.buttons[name] = {
                    XScale = b.Position.X.Scale,
                    XOffset = b.Position.X.Offset,
                    YScale = b.Position.Y.Scale,
                    YOffset = b.Position.Y.Offset,
                }
            end
        end
        pcall(saveAllSettings)
    end

    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not activeDrag or uiLocked then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local delta = input.Position - activeDrag.start
        if math.abs(delta.X) > 6 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 6 then
            activeDrag.moved = true
        end
        if not activeDrag.moved then return end
        if activeDrag.btn and activeDrag.btn.Parent then
            local sp = activeDrag.pos
            activeDrag.btn.Position = UDim2.new(sp.X.Scale, sp.X.Offset + delta.X, sp.Y.Scale, sp.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
        end
    end)

    UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        if not activeDrag then return end
        if activeDrag.moved then
            if activeDrag.btn then
                activeDrag.btn:SetAttribute("ViolenceJustDragged", true)
                task.delay(0.12, function()
                    if activeDrag and activeDrag.btn then return end
                end)
            end
            saveAllBtnPositions()
        end
        activeDrag = nil
    end)

    return panel
end

local function createBypassFloatingButton()
    local ACCENT = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
    local panel = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    panel.Name = "BypassButton"
    panel.ResetOnSpawn = false
    panel.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    panel.DisplayOrder = 21
    pcall(function() if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(panel) end end)
    if not pcall(function() panel.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) then
        panel.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end

    local btnFrame = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
    btnFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 60)
    btnFrame.Name = "Frame"
    if bypassFloatingPos then
        btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(bypassFloatingPos.XScale or 1,
                                      bypassFloatingPos.XOffset or (-10 - 60),
                                      bypassFloatingPos.YScale or 0,
                                      bypassFloatingPos.YOffset or (150 + 10))
    else
        btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10 - 60, 0, 150 + 10)
    end
    btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = bypassToggled and _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active or _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
    btnFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btnFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btnFrame.ZIndex = 20
    btnFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    Instance.new("UICorner", btnFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 18)
    local bypassStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btnFrame)
    bypassStroke.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
    bypassStroke.Thickness = 1.5
    bypassStroke.Transparency = 0

    _G.ViolenceMobileImg.attach(btnFrame, UDim.new(0, 18))
    pcall(function() _G.ViolenceBindFloatPressStroke(btnFrame, bypassStroke) end)

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", btnFrame)
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = "BAT\nv2"
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    label.TextTransparency = 0
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextSize = 11
    label.TextWrapped = true
    label.ZIndex = 50

    local function setActive(state)
        btnFrame:SetAttribute("ViolenceFloatActive", state and true or false)
        if state then
            btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active
            label.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
            bypassStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            bypassStroke.Thickness = 2.2
            bypassStroke.Transparency = 0
        else
            btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
            label.TextColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.text
            bypassStroke.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
            bypassStroke.Thickness = 1.5
            bypassStroke.Transparency = 0
        end
        local hasImg = _G.ViolenceMobileImg.normalize(_G.ViolenceMobileButtonImage) ~= ""
        btnFrame.BackgroundTransparency = hasImg and 1 or 0
        pcall(function() _G.ViolenceShowFloatRing(btnFrame, state and true or false) end)
    end

    local dragging = false
    local hasMoved = false
    local dragStart = nil
    local startPos = nil
    local dragThreshold = 5

    local function onInputBegan(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            hasMoved = false
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = btnFrame.Position
        end
    end

    local function onInputChanged(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            if math.abs(delta.X) > dragThreshold or math.abs(delta.Y) > dragThreshold then
                hasMoved = true
            end
            if hasMoved then
                if not uiLocked then
                    btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                                                  startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
                else
                    dragging = false
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function onInputEnded(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if dragging then
                if not hasMoved then
                    setActive(not bypassToggled)
                    toggleBypass()
                elseif not uiLocked and hasMoved then
                    bypassFloatingPos = {
                        XScale = btnFrame.Position.X.Scale,
                        XOffset = btnFrame.Position.X.Offset,
                        YScale = btnFrame.Position.Y.Scale,
                        YOffset = btnFrame.Position.Y.Offset
                    }
                    pcall(saveAllSettings)
                end
                dragging = false
                hasMoved = false
                dragStart = nil
                startPos = nil
            end
        end
    end

    btnFrame.InputBegan:Connect(onInputBegan)
    btnFrame.InputChanged:Connect(onInputChanged)
    btnFrame.InputEnded:Connect(onInputEnded)

    bypassFloatingButton = panel
    return panel
end

_G.ViolenceTPBat.createButton = function()
    local ACCENT = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
    local panel = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    panel.Name = "TPBatButton"
    panel.ResetOnSpawn = false
    panel.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    panel.DisplayOrder = 25
    pcall(function() if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(panel) end end)
    if not pcall(function() panel.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) then
        panel.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end

    local btnFrame = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
    btnFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 60)
    btnFrame.Name = "Frame"
    btnFrame.Active = true
    if _G.ViolenceTPBat.pos then
        btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(_G.ViolenceTPBat.pos.XScale or 1,
                                      _G.ViolenceTPBat.pos.XOffset or (-10 - 60),
                                      _G.ViolenceTPBat.pos.YScale or 0,
                                      _G.ViolenceTPBat.pos.YOffset or (150 + 80))
    else
        btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10 - 60, 0, 150 + 80)
    end
    btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggled and _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active or _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
    btnFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btnFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btnFrame.ZIndex = 20
    btnFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    Instance.new("UICorner", btnFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 18)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btnFrame)
    stroke.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
    stroke.Thickness = 1.8
    stroke.Transparency = 0

    _G.ViolenceMobileImg.attach(btnFrame, UDim.new(0, 18))

    local clickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", btnFrame)
    clickBtn.Name = "Click"
    clickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    clickBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    clickBtn.Text = ""
    clickBtn.ZIndex = 22
    clickBtn.AutoButtonColor = false

    pcall(function() _G.ViolenceBindFloatPressStroke(btnFrame, stroke) end)

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", btnFrame)
    label.Name = "TextLabel"
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = "TP\nBAT"
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    label.TextTransparency = 0
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextSize = 12
    label.TextWrapped = true
    label.ZIndex = 50

    local dragging = false
    local hasMoved = false
    local dragStart = nil
    local startPos = nil
    local dragThreshold = 5
    local lastToggle = 0

    local function onInputBegan(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            hasMoved = false
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = btnFrame.Position
        end
    end

    local function onInputChanged(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            if math.abs(delta.X) > dragThreshold or math.abs(delta.Y) > dragThreshold then
                hasMoved = true
            end
            if hasMoved then
                if not uiLocked then
                    btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                                                  startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
                else
                    dragging = false
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function onInputEnded(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if dragging then
                if not hasMoved then
                    if tick() - lastToggle > 0.15 then
                        lastToggle = tick()
                        pcall(function() _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggle() end)
                    end
                elseif not uiLocked and hasMoved then
                    _G.ViolenceTPBat.pos = {
                        XScale = btnFrame.Position.X.Scale,
                        XOffset = btnFrame.Position.X.Offset,
                        YScale = btnFrame.Position.Y.Scale,
                        YOffset = btnFrame.Position.Y.Offset
                    }
                    pcall(saveAllSettings)
                end
                dragging = false
                hasMoved = false
                dragStart = nil
                startPos = nil
            end
        end
    end

    clickBtn.InputBegan:Connect(onInputBegan)
    clickBtn.InputChanged:Connect(onInputChanged)
    clickBtn.InputEnded:Connect(onInputEnded)
    clickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if tick() - lastToggle < 0.2 then return end
        if not hasMoved then
            lastToggle = tick()
            pcall(function() _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggle() end)
        end
    end)

    _G.ViolenceTPBat.btn = panel
    return panel
end



_G.createInstantResetFloatingButton = function()
    local panel = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    panel.Name = "InstantResetButton"
    panel.ResetOnSpawn = false
    panel.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    panel.DisplayOrder = 26
    pcall(function() if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(panel) end end)
    if not pcall(function() panel.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) then
        panel.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end

    local btnFrame = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
    btnFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 60)
    btnFrame.Name = "Frame"
    btnFrame.Active = true
    if _G.ViolenceReset.floatPos then
        btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(_G.ViolenceReset.floatPos.XScale or 1,
                                      _G.ViolenceReset.floatPos.XOffset or (-10 - 60),
                                      _G.ViolenceReset.floatPos.YScale or 0,
                                      _G.ViolenceReset.floatPos.YOffset or (150 + 150))
    else
        btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10 - 60, 0, 150 + 150)
    end
    btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
    btnFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btnFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btnFrame.ZIndex = 20
    btnFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    Instance.new("UICorner", btnFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 18)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btnFrame)
    stroke.Color = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.stroke
    stroke.Thickness = 1.8
    stroke.Transparency = 0

    _G.ViolenceMobileImg.attach(btnFrame, UDim.new(0, 18))

    local clickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", btnFrame)
    clickBtn.Name = "Click"
    clickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    clickBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    clickBtn.Text = ""
    clickBtn.ZIndex = 22
    clickBtn.AutoButtonColor = false

    pcall(function() _G.ViolenceBindFloatPressStroke(btnFrame, stroke) end)

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", btnFrame)
    label.Name = "TextLabel"
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = "INSTA\nRESET"
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    label.TextTransparency = 0
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextSize = 11
    label.TextWrapped = true
    label.ZIndex = 50

    local dragging = false
    local hasMoved = false
    local dragStart = nil
    local startPos = nil
    local dragThreshold = 5
    local lastFire = 0

    local function pulse()
        btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.active
        task.delay(0.2, function()
            if btnFrame and btnFrame.Parent then
                btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = _G.ViolenceBtnColor.inactive
            end
        end)
    end

    local function onInputBegan(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            hasMoved = false
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = btnFrame.Position
        end
    end

    local function onInputChanged(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            if math.abs(delta.X) > dragThreshold or math.abs(delta.Y) > dragThreshold then
                hasMoved = true
            end
            if hasMoved then
                if not uiLocked then
                    btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                                                  startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
                else
                    dragging = false
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function onInputEnded(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if dragging then
                if not hasMoved then
                    if tick() - lastFire > 0.2 then
                        lastFire = tick()
                        pulse()
                        pcall(_G.violenceInstantReset)
                    end
                elseif not uiLocked and hasMoved then
                    _G.ViolenceReset.floatPos = {
                        XScale = btnFrame.Position.X.Scale,
                        XOffset = btnFrame.Position.X.Offset,
                        YScale = btnFrame.Position.Y.Scale,
                        YOffset = btnFrame.Position.Y.Offset
                    }
                    pcall(saveAllSettings)
                end
                dragging = false
                hasMoved = false
                dragStart = nil
                startPos = nil
            end
        end
    end

    clickBtn.InputBegan:Connect(onInputBegan)
    clickBtn.InputChanged:Connect(onInputChanged)
    clickBtn.InputEnded:Connect(onInputEnded)
    clickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if tick() - lastFire < 0.2 then return end
        if not hasMoved then
            lastFire = tick()
            pulse()
            pcall(_G.violenceInstantReset)
        end
    end)

    _G.ViolenceReset.floatBtn = panel
    return panel
end

-- Pré-carrega posições salvas antes de criar a GUI
pcall(function()
	if isfile and isfile(CONFIG_FILE) then
		local data = HS:JSONDecode(readfile(CONFIG_FILE))
		if data then
			if data.progressBarPos then savedProgressBarPos = data.progressBarPos end
			if data.mobilePanelPos then
				savedMobilePanelPos = data.mobilePanelPos
				-- NÃO limpa buttons aqui — preserva posições salvas dos flutuantes
			end
			if data.bypassFloatingPos then bypassFloatingPos = data.bypassFloatingPos end
			if data.miniBtnPos then savedMiniBtnPos = data.miniBtnPos end
			if data.tpBatFloatingPos then _G.ViolenceTPBat.pos = data.tpBatFloatingPos end
			if data.instantResetFloatingPos then _G.ViolenceReset = _G.ViolenceReset or {}; _G.ViolenceReset.floatPos = data.instantResetFloatingPos end
		end
	end
end)

-- Só abre a interface DEPOIS da intro (ou se a intro estiver desligada)
task.spawn(function()
	local tWait = tick()
	while not _G._ViolenceIntroDone and (tick() - tWait) < 20 do
		task.wait(0.05)
	end
	_G._ViolenceIntroDone = true

	-- Carrega configs ANTES de montar a UI (valores + toggles)
	local hadSave = false
	pcall(function()
		hadSave = loadAllSettings() and true or false
	end)

	local okGui, errGui = pcall(buildGui)
	if not okGui then
		warn("[Verzed] buildGui error: ", errGui)
	end

	if hadSave then
		pcall(updateUIFromLoaded)
	else
		pcall(_G.applyCustomSky, _G.ViolenceSky.theme or "Night")
	end

	pcall(function() MobilePanel = createMobilePanel() end)
	pcall(function() bypassFloatingButton = createBypassFloatingButton() end)
	pcall(function() _G.ViolenceTPBat.btn = _G.ViolenceTPBat.createButton() end)
	pcall(function() _G.ViolenceReset.floatBtn = _G.createInstantResetFloatingButton() end)
	pcall(function()
		pcall(function() _G.ViolenceMobileImg.applyAll() end)
	end)
	-- aplica hide DEPOIS de criar todos os botoes (inclui Reset / Bat V2 / TP Bat)
	pcall(applyMobileButtonsHidden)

	if LP.Character then
		task.wait(0.0)
		setupSpeedIndicator(LP.Character)
		pcall(_G.ViolenceNino.startDetection, LP.Character)
	end

	-- Só salva se carregou config (nunca sobrescreve com defaults)
	task.delay(1.5, function()
		if _G.ViolenceSettingsLoaded then
			pcall(saveAllSettings)
		end
	end)
end)

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    stopAutoSteal()
    stopAutoLeft()
    stopAutoRight()
    stopBatCounter()
    stopMedusaCounter()
    stopAutoTPDown()
    stopAntiRagdoll()
    stopUnwalk()
    stopDropBrainrot()
    if _G.ViolenceAutoCarry then
        _G.ViolenceAutoCarry.stop()
        _G.ViolenceAutoCarry.active = false
    end
    if autoBatEnabled then disableAutoBat() end
    if bypassToggled then stopBypassAimbot() end
    if _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggled then _G.ViolenceTPBat.stop() end

    task.wait(0.1)
    while not LP.Character or not LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or not LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") do
        task.wait()
    end
    if _G.ViolenceAutoCarry and _G.ViolenceAutoCarry.enabled then
        _G.ViolenceAutoCarry.start()
    end

    if steppedConn then steppedConn:Disconnect(); steppedConn = nil end
    if movementLoop then movementLoop:Disconnect(); movementLoop = nil end

    steppedConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LP and p.Character then
                for _, part in ipairs(p.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    movementLoop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local char2 = LP.Character
        if not char2 then return end
        local hum = char2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local hrp = char2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum or not hrp then return end

        if not autoBatEnabled and not bypassToggled and not _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggled and not autoLeftEnabled and not autoRightEnabled then
            -- Com Anti Drop ativo, LinearVelocity (No drop logic) cuida do movimento
            if not (_G.ViolenceAntiDrop and _G.ViolenceAntiDrop.enabled) then
                local md = hum.MoveDirection
                local spd
                do
                    local sp = _G.ViolenceSpeeds or {}
                    if laggerToggled then
                        spd = (laggerLevel == 2) and (tonumber(sp.L2) or 15) or (tonumber(sp.L1) or 20)
                    else
                        spd = speedMode and (tonumber(sp.CS) or 25) or (tonumber(sp.NS) or 45)
                    end
                end
                if md.Magnitude > 0 then
                    lastMoveDir = md
                    hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(md.X * spd, hrp.Velocity.Y, md.Z * spd)
                elseif antiRagdollEnabled and lastMoveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
                    local anyHeld = false
                    for key in pairs(MOVE_KEYS) do
                        if UIS:IsKeyDown(key) then anyHeld = true; break end
                    end
                    if anyHeld then
                        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(lastMoveDir.X * spd, hrp.Velocity.Y, lastMoveDir.Z * spd)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if speedLabel then
            local dispSpd
            if _G.ViolenceAntiDrop and _G.ViolenceAntiDrop.enabled and _G.ViolenceAntiDrop._getDisplaySpeed then
                dispSpd = _G.ViolenceAntiDrop._getDisplaySpeed(hum)
            else
                dispSpd = Vector3.new(hrp.Velocity.X, 0, hrp.Velocity.Z).Magnitude
            end
            speedLabel.Text = formatSpeedModeText(dispSpd)
        end
    end)

    setupSpeedIndicator(char)
    pcall(_G.ViolenceNino.startDetection, char)

    if selectedAnimPack then
        task.delay(0.4, function()
            pcall(applyAnimationPack, selectedAnimPack)
        end)
    end

    if autoBatEnabled then enableAutoBat() end
    if _G.ViolenceMelee and _G.ViolenceMelee.enabled then
        _G.ViolenceMelee.stop()
        task.delay(0.25, function()
            if _G.ViolenceMelee and _G.ViolenceMelee.enabled then _G.ViolenceMelee.start() end
        end)
    end
    if _G.ViolenceNino and _G.ViolenceNino.enabled then
        task.delay(0.3, function()
            if _G.ViolenceNino and _G.ViolenceNino.enabled and LP.Character then
                _G.ViolenceNino.createBillboard()
                _G.ViolenceNino.setupDetection(LP.Character)
            end
        end)
    end
    if autoLeftEnabled then startAutoLeft() end
    if autoRightEnabled then startAutoRight() end
    if bypassToggled then toggleBypass(true) end
    if _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggled then _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggle(true) end
    if Steal.AutoStealEnabled then pcall(startAutoSteal) end
    if jumpEnabled then startJumpMode() end
    if antiRagdollEnabled then startAntiRagdoll() end

    if medusaCounterEnabled then
        setupMedusa(char)
        if setMedusaVisual then setMedusaVisual(true) end
    else
        stopMedusaCounter()
        if setMedusaVisual then setMedusaVisual(false) end
    end

    if batCounterEnabled then startBatCounter() end
    if unwalkEnabled then startUnwalk() end
    if autoTPDownEnabled then startAutoTPDown() end
    if antiDieEnabled then task.delay(0.3, function() pcall(startAntiDieFling) end) end

    updateProgressBarVisibility()
    refreshSpeedModeLabel()
end)

local lastLaggerToggle = 0
local LAGGER_COOLDOWN = 0.3

UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if _anyKeyListening then return end
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
        if gpe or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
    elseif not isGamepadInput(input) then
        return
    end
    if not isBindableInput(input) then return end

    local kc = input.KeyCode
    if not kc then return end

    if kbMatch(KB.LaggerMode, kc) then
        if tick() - lastLaggerToggle >= LAGGER_COOLDOWN then
            lastLaggerToggle = tick()
            toggleLaggerCycle()
        end
        return
    end
    if kbMatch(KB.CarryToggle, kc) then toggleCarryMode() return end
    if kbMatch(KB.DropBrainrot, kc) then
        -- Evita drop acidental: só tecla configurada, cooldown curto, e sem TextBox
        if UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        if not dropActive then
            if dropBrainrotSetVisual then dropBrainrotSetVisual(true) end
            executeDropWithToggle(dropBrainrotSetVisual)
        end
        return
    end
    if kbMatch(KB.TPFloor, kc) then executeTPDown() return end
    if kbMatch(KB.AutoLeft, kc) then
        autoLeftEnabled = not autoLeftEnabled
        if autoLeftEnabled then
            startAutoLeft()
        else
            stopAutoLeft()
        end
        if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(autoLeftEnabled) end
        if mobSetAutoLeft then mobSetAutoLeft(autoLeftEnabled) end
        return
    end
    if kbMatch(KB.AutoRight, kc) then
        autoRightEnabled = not autoRightEnabled
        if autoRightEnabled then
            startAutoRight()
        else
            stopAutoRight()
        end
        if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(autoRightEnabled) end
        if mobSetAutoRight then mobSetAutoRight(autoRightEnabled) end
        return
    end
    if kbMatch(KB.AutoBat, kc) then
        if not autoBatEnabled then
            enableAutoBat()
            if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(true) end
            if mobSetAutoBat then mobSetAutoBat(true) end
        else
            disableAutoBat()
            if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end
            if mobSetAutoBat then mobSetAutoBat(false) end
        end
        return
    end
    if kbMatch(KB.TPBat, kc) then
        _G.ViolenceTPBat.toggle()
        if _G.ViolenceTPBat.setVisual then _G.ViolenceTPBat.setVisual(_G.ViolenceTPBat.toggled) end
        return
    end
    if kbMatch(KB.Bypass, kc) then
        toggleBypass()
        return
    end
    if kbMatch(KB.AutoTPDown, kc) then
        autoTPDownEnabled = not autoTPDownEnabled
        if autoTPDownEnabled then
            startAutoTPDown()
        else
            stopAutoTPDown()
        end
        if setAutoTPDownVisual then setAutoTPDownVisual(autoTPDownEnabled) end
        return
    end
    if kbMatch(KB.JumpMode, kc) then
        if modeSelectBtn then
            local newMode = jumpMode == 1 and 2 or 1
            jumpMode = newMode
            modeSelectBtn.Text = jumpMode == 1 and "Tap Tap" or "Hold"
            if jumpEnabled then
                stopJumpMode()
                startJumpMode()
            end
        end
        return
    end
    if kbMatch(KB.InstantReset, kc) then
        _G.violenceInstantReset()
        return
    end
    if kbMatch(KB.GuiHide, kc) then
        if main then
            if main.Visible then hideGui() else showGui() end
        end
        return
    end
end)

-- ===================== AUTO SAVE (a cada 2s + backup) =====================
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(2)
        pcall(saveAllSettings)
    end
end)
